#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Motif v14.0.0 — Complete, Production-Hardened macOS Ollama Frontend Builder
# Incorporates all Ollama protocol, KV-cache, Unified Memory, SQLite WAL+FTS5,
# macOS lifecycle, streaming, Markdown, and UI fixes.
set -euo pipefail

printf "\n===== [1/17] Verify Apple Silicon Runner & Toolchain =====\n"
(
  uname -m
  sw_vers
  xcodebuild -version || true
  swift --version
)

printf "\n===== [2/17] Create Project Directory Structure & Bundle Verified Fonts =====\n"
(
  set -e
  mkdir -p Motif/Sources/Motif/Models
  mkdir -p Motif/Sources/Motif/Engine
  mkdir -p Motif/Sources/Motif/Services
  mkdir -p Motif/Sources/Motif/Views
  mkdir -p Motif/Sources/Motif/Components
  mkdir -p Motif/Sources/Motif/Resources/Fonts
  mkdir -p build/dmg_staging
  mkdir -p build/AppIcon.iconset

  download_font() {
    local url="$1"
    local dest="$2"
    if [ -f "$dest" ] && [ "$(wc -c < "$dest" | tr -d ' ')" -ge 10000 ]; then
      echo "Font already cached: $(basename "$dest")"
      return 0
    fi
    if curl -fsSL --retry 3 --retry-delay 2 "$url" -o "$dest"; then
      local size
      size=$(wc -c < "$dest" | tr -d ' ')
      if [ "$size" -lt 10000 ]; then
        echo "WARNING: Font $(basename "$dest") unexpectedly small ($size bytes); removing." >&2
        rm -f "$dest"
      fi
    else
      echo "WARNING: Failed to download $(basename "$dest"); app will fall back to system SF font." >&2
      rm -f "$dest"
    fi
  }

  download_font "https://github.com/google/fonts/raw/main/ofl/plusjakartasans/PlusJakartaSans%5Bwght%5D.ttf" \
    "Motif/Sources/Motif/Resources/Fonts/PlusJakartaSans-Variable.ttf"
  download_font "https://github.com/google/fonts/raw/main/ofl/inter/Inter%5Bopsz,wght%5D.ttf" \
    "Motif/Sources/Motif/Resources/Fonts/Inter-Variable.ttf"
  download_font "https://github.com/google/fonts/raw/main/ofl/opensans/OpenSans%5Bwdth,wght%5D.ttf" \
    "Motif/Sources/Motif/Resources/Fonts/OpenSans-Variable.ttf"
  download_font "https://github.com/google/fonts/raw/main/ofl/geist/Geist%5Bwght%5D.ttf" \
    "Motif/Sources/Motif/Resources/Fonts/Geist-Variable.ttf"
)

printf "\n===== [3/17] Generate Programmatic macOS Squircle AppIcon.icns =====\n"
(
cat << 'EOF' > build/generate_icon.swift
import AppKit
import CoreGraphics

func renderMotifIcon(size: Int) -> Data? {
    guard let rep = NSBitmapImageRep(
        bitmapDataPlanes: nil,
        pixelsWide: size,
        pixelsHigh: size,
        bitsPerSample: 8,
        samplesPerPixel: 4,
        hasAlpha: true,
        isPlanar: false,
        colorSpaceName: .deviceRGB,
        bytesPerRow: 0,
        bitsPerPixel: 0
    ) else { return nil }

    NSGraphicsContext.saveGraphicsState()
    NSGraphicsContext.current = NSGraphicsContext(bitmapImageRep: rep)
    guard let ctx = NSGraphicsContext.current?.cgContext else {
        NSGraphicsContext.restoreGraphicsState()
        return nil
    }

    let s = CGFloat(size)
    let inset = s * 0.085
    let rect = CGRect(x: inset, y: inset, width: s - inset * 2, height: s - inset * 2)
    let cornerRadius = rect.width * 0.225

    let squircle = NSBezierPath(roundedRect: rect, xRadius: cornerRadius, yRadius: cornerRadius)
    ctx.saveGState()
    squircle.addClip()

    let colorSpace = CGColorSpaceCreateDeviceRGB()
    let gradColors = [
        CGColor(red: 0.14, green: 0.15, blue: 0.19, alpha: 1.0),
        CGColor(red: 0.07, green: 0.08, blue: 0.10, alpha: 1.0)
    ] as CFArray
    if let gradient = CGGradient(colorsSpace: colorSpace, colors: gradColors, locations: [0.0, 1.0]) {
        ctx.drawLinearGradient(
            gradient,
            start: CGPoint(x: s * 0.5, y: s - inset),
            end: CGPoint(x: s * 0.5, y: inset),
            options: []
        )
    }

    ctx.setStrokeColor(CGColor(red: 1.0, green: 1.0, blue: 1.0, alpha: 0.04))
    ctx.setLineWidth(max(1.0, s * 0.003))
    ctx.move(to: CGPoint(x: rect.minX, y: s * 0.5))
    ctx.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: rect.maxX, y: s * 0.5))
    ctx.move(to: CGPoint(x: s * 0.5, y: rect.minY))
    ctx.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: s * 0.5, y: rect.maxY))
    ctx.strokePath()

    let mPath = CGMutablePath()
    let leftX = s * 0.29
    let rightX = s * 0.71
    let midX = s * 0.50
    let botY = s * 0.31
    let topY = s * 0.69
    let valleyY = s * 0.44

    mPath.move(to: CGPoint(x: leftX, y: botY))
    mPath.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: leftX, y: topY))
    mPath.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: midX, y: valleyY))
    mPath.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: rightX, y: topY))
    mPath.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: rightX, y: botY))

    ctx.setStrokeColor(CGColor(red: 0.95, green: 0.96, blue: 0.98, alpha: 0.96))
    ctx.setLineWidth(s * 0.072)
    ctx.setLineCap(.round)
    ctx.setLineJoin(.round)
    ctx.addPath(mPath)
    ctx.strokePath()

    ctx.restoreGState()

    let borderPath = NSBezierPath(
        roundedRect: rect.insetBy(dx: s * 0.006, dy: s * 0.006),
        xRadius: cornerRadius,
        yRadius: cornerRadius
    )
    NSColor(calibratedWhite: 1.0, alpha: 0.16).setStroke()
    borderPath.lineWidth = max(1.0, s * 0.012)
    borderPath.stroke()

    NSGraphicsContext.restoreGraphicsState()
    return rep.representation(using: .png, properties: [:])
}

let specs: [(String, Int)] = [
    ("icon_16x16.png", 16),
    ("icon_16x16@2x.png", 32),
    ("icon_32x32.png", 32),
    ("icon_32x32@2x.png", 64),
    ("icon_128x128.png", 128),
    ("icon_128x128@2x.png", 256),
    ("icon_256x256.png", 256),
    ("icon_256x256@2x.png", 512),
    ("icon_512x512.png", 512),
    ("icon_512x512@2x.png", 1024)
]

for (name, px) in specs {
    if let png = renderMotifIcon(size: px) {
        try? png.write(to: URL(fileURLWithPath: "build/AppIcon.iconset/\(name)"))
    }
}
EOF
swift build/generate_icon.swift
iconutil -c icns build/AppIcon.iconset -o build/AppIcon.icns
ls -lh build/AppIcon.icns
)

printf "\n===== [4/17] Generate Package.swift =====\n"
(
cat << 'EOF' > Motif/Package.swift
// swift-tools-version: 6.0
import PackageDescription

let package = Package(
    name: "Motif",
    platforms: [
        .macOS(.v14)
    ],
    products: [
        .executable(name: "Motif", targets: ["Motif"])
    ],
    targets: [
        .executableTarget(
            name: "Motif",
            path: "Sources/Motif",
            resources: [
                .process("Resources")
            ],
            swiftSettings: [
                .swiftLanguageMode(.v5)
            ],
            linkerSettings: [
                .linkedFramework("AppKit"),
                .linkedFramework("SwiftUI"),
                .linkedFramework("Security"),
                .linkedFramework("IOKit"),
                .linkedFramework("Metal"),
                .linkedFramework("UniformTypeIdentifiers"),
                .linkedFramework("Combine"),
                .linkedFramework("CoreText"),
                .linkedFramework("ImageIO"),
                .linkedFramework("CryptoKit"),
                .linkedLibrary("sqlite3")
            ]
        )
    ]
)
EOF
)

printf "\n===== [5/17] Generate Domain Models, Proper Equatable Tokens & Tolerant Codable Schemas (DomainModels.swift) =====\n"
(
cat << 'EOF' > Motif/Sources/Motif/Models/DomainModels.swift
import Foundation
import SwiftUI
import AppKit
import CoreText

// MARK: - 6 Calibrated Architectural Themes
public enum AppThemeID: String, CaseIterable, Codable, Identifiable, Sendable {
    case alabasterStudio = "Alabaster Studio"
    case warmEditorial = "Warm Editorial"
    case nordicFrost = "Nordic Frost"
    case botanicalSage = "Botanical Sage"
    case carbonSlate = "Carbon Slate"
    case midnightNord = "Midnight Nord"

    public var id: String { rawValue }

    public var subtitle: String {
        switch self {
        case .alabasterStudio: return "Pure paper white (#FFFFFF) with monochrome charcoal ink and hairline chrome"
        case .warmEditorial: return "Warm gallery paper (#FAF9F6) with espresso ink"
        case .nordicFrost: return "Cool ice-paper (#F6F9FC) with deep slate ink"
        case .botanicalSage: return "Soft daylight mist (#F5F8F6) with deep forest ink"
        case .carbonSlate: return "Matte studio dark (#111214) with crisp silver ink"
        case .midnightNord: return "True-black OLED obsidian (#000000) with platinum ink"
        }
    }

    public var supportsCustomPalette: Bool { true }

    public var isDark: Bool {
        switch self {
        case .alabasterStudio, .warmEditorial, .nordicFrost, .botanicalSage: return false
        case .carbonSlate, .midnightNord: return true
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Custom Palette Overrides (Universal WCAG-Safe Color Customization)
public struct ThemeColorOverride: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var isCustomCanvasEnabled: Bool = false
    public var canvasRed: Double
    public var canvasGreen: Double
    public var canvasBlue: Double

    public var isCustomAccentEnabled: Bool = false
    public var accentRed: Double
    public var accentGreen: Double
    public var accentBlue: Double

    public var isCustomThinkingEnabled: Bool = false
    public var thinkingRed: Double
    public var thinkingGreen: Double
    public var thinkingBlue: Double

    public var isModified: Bool {
        isCustomCanvasEnabled || isCustomAccentEnabled || isCustomThinkingEnabled
    }

    public static let alabasterDefault = ThemeColorOverride(
        canvasRed: 1.000, canvasGreen: 1.000, canvasBlue: 1.000,
        accentRed: 0.125, accentGreen: 0.133, accentBlue: 0.153,
        thinkingRed: 0.969, thinkingGreen: 0.969, thinkingBlue: 0.973
    )

    public static let warmEditorialDefault = ThemeColorOverride(
        canvasRed: 0.980, canvasGreen: 0.976, canvasBlue: 0.965,
        accentRed: 0.173, accentGreen: 0.165, accentBlue: 0.153,
        thinkingRed: 0.949, thinkingGreen: 0.941, thinkingBlue: 0.922
    )
}

public struct CustomStudioPalettes: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var alabaster: ThemeColorOverride = .alabasterDefault
    public var warmEditorial: ThemeColorOverride = .warmEditorialDefault
    public var universal: ThemeColorOverride = .alabasterDefault

    public init() {}

    public init(from decoder: Decoder) throws {
        let c = try decoder.container(keyedBy: CodingKeys.self)
        alabaster = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(ThemeColorOverride.self, forKey: .alabaster)) ?? .alabasterDefault
        warmEditorial = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(ThemeColorOverride.self, forKey: .warmEditorial)) ?? .warmEditorialDefault
        universal = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(ThemeColorOverride.self, forKey: .universal)) ?? .alabasterDefault
    }
}

// MARK: - Centralized Design Token System (WCAG 2.1 Linearized Contrast + Monochrome Paper Hierarchy)
public struct MotifTokens: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let theme: AppThemeID
    public let customPalettes: CustomStudioPalettes
    public let isWindowActive: Bool

    public init(
        theme: AppThemeID,
        customPalettes: CustomStudioPalettes = CustomStudioPalettes(),
        isWindowActive: Bool = true
    ) {
        self.theme = theme
        self.customPalettes = customPalettes
        self.isWindowActive = isWindowActive
    }

    public var activeOverride: ThemeColorOverride? {
        if customPalettes.universal.isModified {
            return customPalettes.universal
        }
        switch theme {
        case .alabasterStudio: return customPalettes.alabaster
        case .warmEditorial: return customPalettes.warmEditorial
        default: return customPalettes.universal
        }
    }

    public var hasCustomCanvas: Bool { activeOverride?.isCustomCanvasEnabled == true }
    public var hasCustomAccent: Bool { activeOverride?.isCustomAccentEnabled == true }
    public var hasCustomThinking: Bool { activeOverride?.isCustomThinkingEnabled == true }

    // MARK: - WCAG 2.1 Linearized Luminance & Contrast Helpers
    private static func linearize(_ channel: Double) -> Double {
        let c = min(1.0, max(0.0, channel))
        return c <= 0.03928 ? (c / 12.92) : pow((c + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4)
    }

    public static func relativeLuminance(r: Double, g: Double, b: Double) -> Double {
        0.2126 * linearize(r) + 0.7152 * linearize(g) + 0.0722 * linearize(b)
    }

    public static func contrastRatio(lum1: Double, lum2: Double) -> Double {
        let hi = max(lum1, lum2)
        let lo = min(lum1, lum2)
        return (hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05)
    }

    private var baseCanvasRGB: (r: Double, g: Double, b: Double) {
        if hasCustomCanvas, let c = activeOverride {
            return (c.canvasRed, c.canvasGreen, c.canvasBlue)
        }
        switch theme {
        case .alabasterStudio: return (1.000, 1.000, 1.000)
        case .warmEditorial:   return (0.980, 0.976, 0.965)
        case .nordicFrost:     return (0.965, 0.976, 0.988)
        case .botanicalSage:   return (0.961, 0.973, 0.965)
        case .carbonSlate:     return (0.067, 0.071, 0.078)
        case .midnightNord:    return (0.000, 0.000, 0.000)
        }
    }

    private var canvasLuminance: Double {
        let rgb = baseCanvasRGB
        return Self.relativeLuminance(r: rgb.r, g: rgb.g, b: rgb.b)
    }

    /// True WCAG perceptual midpoint (~0.1791): guarantees dark ink on light colors and light ink on dark colors.
    public var isEffectivelyDark: Bool {
        hasCustomCanvas ? (canvasLuminance < 0.1791) : theme.isDark
    }

    private func shiftRGB(_ rgb: (r: Double, g: Double, b: Double), toward target: (r: Double, g: Double, b: Double), amount: Double) -> Color {
        let t = min(1.0, max(0.0, amount))
        return Color(
            red: rgb.r + (target.r - rgb.r) * t,
            green: rgb.g + (target.g - rgb.g) * t,
            blue: rgb.b + (target.b - rgb.b) * t
        )
    }

    /// Guaranteed WCAG AAA (>= 7:1) or maximum-possible primary ink subtly harmonized with the canvas hue.
    private var primaryInkRGB: (r: Double, g: Double, b: Double) {
        let bg = baseCanvasRGB
        let bgLum = canvasLuminance
        if isEffectivelyDark {
            let soft = (r: 0.945 - (1.0 - bg.r) * 0.03, g: 0.948 - (1.0 - bg.g) * 0.03, b: 0.956 - (1.0 - bg.b) * 0.03)
            if Self.contrastRatio(lum1: bgLum, lum2: Self.relativeLuminance(r: soft.r, g: soft.g, b: soft.b)) >= 7.0 {
                return soft
            }
            return (0.985, 0.988, 0.992)
        } else {
            let soft = (r: 0.085 + bg.r * 0.025, g: 0.085 + bg.g * 0.025, b: 0.095 + bg.b * 0.025)
            if Self.contrastRatio(lum1: bgLum, lum2: Self.relativeLuminance(r: soft.r, g: soft.g, b: soft.b)) >= 7.0 {
                return soft
            }
            return (0.04, 0.04, 0.05)
        }
    }

    /// Guaranteed WCAG AA (>= 4.8:1) secondary ink.
    private var secondaryInkRGB: (r: Double, g: Double, b: Double) {
        let bg = baseCanvasRGB
        let bgLum = canvasLuminance
        let p = primaryInkRGB
        for mix in [0.36, 0.28, 0.20, 0.12, 0.0] {
            let cand = (
                r: p.r + (bg.r - p.r) * mix,
                g: p.g + (bg.g - p.g) * mix,
                b: p.b + (bg.b - p.b) * mix
            )
            let lum = Self.relativeLuminance(r: cand.r, g: cand.g, b: cand.b)
            if Self.contrastRatio(lum1: bgLum, lum2: lum) >= 4.8 {
                return cand
            }
        }
        return p
    }

    public var computedTextContrastRatio: Double {
        let p = primaryInkRGB
        return Self.contrastRatio(lum1: canvasLuminance, lum2: Self.relativeLuminance(r: p.r, g: p.g, b: p.b))
    }

    public var canvasBackground: Color {
        let rgb = baseCanvasRGB
        return Color(red: rgb.r, green: rgb.g, blue: rgb.b)
    }

    public var composerSurface: Color {
        let rgb = baseCanvasRGB
        if hasCustomCanvas {
            return isEffectivelyDark
                ? shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (1.0, 1.0, 1.0), amount: 0.050)
                : shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (1.0, 1.0, 1.0), amount: 0.52)
        }
        switch theme {
        case .alabasterStudio: return Color.white
        case .warmEditorial:   return Color(red: 0.996, green: 0.994, blue: 0.988)
        case .nordicFrost:     return Color(red: 0.988, green: 0.994, blue: 1.000)
        case .botanicalSage:   return Color(red: 0.986, green: 0.994, blue: 0.988)
        case .carbonSlate:     return Color(red: 0.102, green: 0.106, blue: 0.118)
        case .midnightNord:    return Color(red: 0.058, green: 0.063, blue: 0.075)
        }
    }

    public var userBubbleSurface: Color {
        let rgb = baseCanvasRGB
        if hasCustomCanvas {
            return isEffectivelyDark
                ? shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (1.0, 1.0, 1.0), amount: 0.075)
                : shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (0.0, 0.0, 0.0), amount: 0.055)
        }
        switch theme {
        case .alabasterStudio: return Color(red: 0.949, green: 0.949, blue: 0.957)
        case .warmEditorial:   return Color(red: 0.941, green: 0.933, blue: 0.914)
        case .nordicFrost:     return Color(red: 0.914, green: 0.937, blue: 0.961)
        case .botanicalSage:   return Color(red: 0.910, green: 0.937, blue: 0.918)
        case .carbonSlate:     return Color(red: 0.129, green: 0.133, blue: 0.149)
        case .midnightNord:    return Color(red: 0.082, green: 0.090, blue: 0.106)
        }
    }

    public var codeSurface: Color {
        let rgb = baseCanvasRGB
        if hasCustomCanvas {
            return isEffectivelyDark
                ? shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (1.0, 1.0, 1.0), amount: 0.038)
                : shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (0.0, 0.0, 0.0), amount: 0.036)
        }
        switch theme {
        case .alabasterStudio: return Color(red: 0.969, green: 0.969, blue: 0.973)
        case .warmEditorial:   return Color(red: 0.957, green: 0.949, blue: 0.933)
        case .nordicFrost:     return Color(red: 0.937, green: 0.957, blue: 0.976)
        case .botanicalSage:   return Color(red: 0.933, green: 0.957, blue: 0.941)
        case .carbonSlate:     return Color(red: 0.092, green: 0.096, blue: 0.108)
        case .midnightNord:    return Color(red: 0.047, green: 0.051, blue: 0.063)
        }
    }

    public var hoverSurface: Color {
        isEffectivelyDark ? Color.white.opacity(0.058) : Color.black.opacity(0.042)
    }

    public var surroundSubstrate: Color { sidebarBackground }

    public var sidebarBackground: Color {
        let rgb = baseCanvasRGB
        if hasCustomCanvas {
            return isEffectivelyDark
                ? shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (0.0, 0.0, 0.0), amount: 0.18)
                : shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (0.0, 0.0, 0.0), amount: 0.025)
        }
        switch theme {
        case .alabasterStudio: return Color(red: 0.980, green: 0.980, blue: 0.984)
        case .warmEditorial:   return Color(red: 0.957, green: 0.949, blue: 0.933)
        case .nordicFrost:     return Color(red: 0.941, green: 0.957, blue: 0.976)
        case .botanicalSage:   return Color(red: 0.937, green: 0.957, blue: 0.945)
        case .carbonSlate:     return Color(red: 0.051, green: 0.055, blue: 0.063)
        case .midnightNord:    return Color(red: 0.024, green: 0.027, blue: 0.035)
        }
    }

    public var elevatedSurface: Color {
        let rgb = baseCanvasRGB
        if hasCustomCanvas {
            return isEffectivelyDark
                ? shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (1.0, 1.0, 1.0), amount: 0.055)
                : shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (0.0, 0.0, 0.0), amount: 0.044)
        }
        switch theme {
        case .alabasterStudio: return Color(red: 0.961, green: 0.961, blue: 0.969)
        case .warmEditorial:   return Color(red: 0.945, green: 0.937, blue: 0.918)
        case .nordicFrost:     return Color(red: 0.918, green: 0.941, blue: 0.965)
        case .botanicalSage:   return Color(red: 0.914, green: 0.941, blue: 0.922)
        case .carbonSlate:     return Color(red: 0.110, green: 0.114, blue: 0.129)
        case .midnightNord:    return Color(red: 0.055, green: 0.063, blue: 0.078)
        }
    }

    public var thinkingSurface: Color {
        if hasCustomThinking, let c = activeOverride {
            return Color(red: c.thinkingRed, green: c.thinkingGreen, blue: c.thinkingBlue)
        }
        let rgb = baseCanvasRGB
        return isEffectivelyDark
            ? shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (1.0, 1.0, 1.0), amount: 0.024)
            : shiftRGB(rgb, toward: (0.0, 0.0, 0.0), amount: 0.024)
    }

    public var thinkingText: Color {
        if hasCustomThinking, let c = activeOverride {
            let lum = Self.relativeLuminance(r: c.thinkingRed, g: c.thinkingGreen, b: c.thinkingBlue)
            return lum < 0.1791
                ? Color(red: 0.78, green: 0.80, blue: 0.85)
                : Color(red: 0.33, green: 0.34, blue: 0.38)
        }
        return secondaryText
    }

    public var selectionPill: Color {
        isEffectivelyDark ? Color.white.opacity(0.09) : Color.black.opacity(0.06)
    }

    public var primaryText: Color {
        if hasCustomCanvas {
            let p = primaryInkRGB
            return Color(red: p.r, green: p.g, blue: p.b)
        }
        switch theme {
        case .alabasterStudio: return Color(red: 0.094, green: 0.094, blue: 0.106)
        case .warmEditorial:   return Color(red: 0.141, green: 0.133, blue: 0.122)
        case .nordicFrost:     return Color(red: 0.075, green: 0.106, blue: 0.165)
        case .botanicalSage:   return Color(red: 0.075, green: 0.149, blue: 0.110)
        case .carbonSlate:     return Color(red: 0.933, green: 0.937, blue: 0.949)
        case .midnightNord:    return Color(red: 0.949, green: 0.957, blue: 0.973)
        }
    }

    public var secondaryText: Color {
        if hasCustomCanvas {
            let s = secondaryInkRGB
            return Color(red: s.r, green: s.g, blue: s.b)
        }
        switch theme {
        case .alabasterStudio: return Color(red: 0.415, green: 0.427, blue: 0.459)
        case .warmEditorial:   return Color(red: 0.443, green: 0.420, blue: 0.384)
        case .nordicFrost:     return Color(red: 0.345, green: 0.400, blue: 0.475)
        case .botanicalSage:   return Color(red: 0.318, green: 0.412, blue: 0.361)
        case .carbonSlate:     return Color(red: 0.600, green: 0.616, blue: 0.655)
        case .midnightNord:    return Color(red: 0.565, green: 0.596, blue: 0.667)
        }
    }

    public var mutedText: Color {
        if hasCustomCanvas {
            let s = secondaryInkRGB
            return Color(red: s.r, green: s.g, blue: s.b).opacity(0.76)
        }
        switch theme {
        case .alabasterStudio: return Color(red: 0.555, green: 0.565, blue: 0.596)
        case .warmEditorial:   return Color(red: 0.555, green: 0.529, blue: 0.486)
        case .nordicFrost:     return Color(red: 0.475, green: 0.530, blue: 0.600)
        case .botanicalSage:   return Color(red: 0.450, green: 0.540, blue: 0.490)
        case .carbonSlate:     return Color(red: 0.455, green: 0.471, blue: 0.514)
        case .midnightNord:    return Color(red: 0.420, green: 0.455, blue: 0.530)
        }
    }

    public var hairline: Color {
        isEffectivelyDark ? Color.white.opacity(0.09) : Color.black.opacity(0.085)
    }

    public var strongBorder: Color {
        isEffectivelyDark ? Color.white.opacity(0.20) : Color.black.opacity(0.17)
    }

    /// Low-key monochrome primary action across ALL themes (never garish neon blue).
    public var accentAction: Color {
        if !isWindowActive { return mutedText }
        if hasCustomAccent, let c = activeOverride {
            return Color(red: c.accentRed, green: c.accentGreen, blue: c.accentBlue)
        }
        if isEffectivelyDark {
            return Color(red: 0.910, green: 0.918, blue: 0.933)
        }
        switch theme {
        case .warmEditorial: return Color(red: 0.180, green: 0.172, blue: 0.160)
        case .nordicFrost:   return Color(red: 0.135, green: 0.185, blue: 0.265)
        case .botanicalSage: return Color(red: 0.125, green: 0.225, blue: 0.170)
        default:             return Color(red: 0.125, green: 0.130, blue: 0.145)
        }
    }

    public var accentForeground: Color {
        if hasCustomAccent, let c = activeOverride {
            let lum = Self.relativeLuminance(r: c.accentRed, g: c.accentGreen, b: c.accentBlue)
            return lum > 0.1791 ? Color(red: 0.07, green: 0.08, blue: 0.09) : Color.white
        }
        return isEffectivelyDark ? Color(red: 0.07, green: 0.075, blue: 0.085) : Color.white
    }

    /// Dedicated calm, theme-matched Send button tokens (guaranteed never bright blue).
    public var sendButtonActive: Color {
        isEffectivelyDark ? Color(red: 0.88, green: 0.89, blue: 0.91) : primaryText.opacity(0.88)
    }

    public var sendButtonDisabled: Color {
        isEffectivelyDark ? Color.white.opacity(0.07) : Color.black.opacity(0.06)
    }

    public var sendButtonForeground: Color {
        isEffectivelyDark ? Color(red: 0.07, green: 0.075, blue: 0.085) : Color.white
    }

    public var statusOnline: Color {
        isEffectivelyDark
            ? Color(red: 0.30, green: 0.78, blue: 0.48)
            : Color(red: 0.14, green: 0.54, blue: 0.30)
    }

    public var statusWarning: Color {
        isEffectivelyDark
            ? Color(red: 0.94, green: 0.68, blue: 0.24)
            : Color(red: 0.76, green: 0.44, blue: 0.06)
    }

    public static let sidebarDefaultWidth: CGFloat = 248.0
    public static let sidebarMinWidth: CGFloat = 212.0
    public static let sidebarMaxWidth: CGFloat = 340.0
    public static let inspectorDefaultWidth: CGFloat = 390.0
    public static let inspectorMinWidth: CGFloat = 300.0
    public static let inspectorMaxWidth: CGFloat = 580.0
}

// MARK: - Font Engine
public enum AppFontFamily: String, CaseIterable, Codable, Identifiable, Sendable {
    case plusJakartaSans = "Plus Jakarta Sans"
    case inter = "Inter"
    case openSans = "Open Sans"
    case geist = "Geist"

    public var id: String { rawValue }
}

@MainActor
public enum CustomFontRegistrar {
    public private(set) static var resolvedJakartaName: String = "Plus Jakarta Sans"
    public private(set) static var resolvedInterName: String = "Inter"
    public private(set) static var resolvedOpenSansName: String = "Open Sans"
    public private(set) static var resolvedGeistName: String = "Geist"
    private static var hasRegistered: Bool = false

    public static func registerBundledFonts() {
        guard !hasRegistered else { return }
        hasRegistered = true
        guard let resourceURL = Bundle.main.resourceURL else { return }
        let fontDirs = [
            resourceURL.appendingPathComponent("Fonts"),
            resourceURL.appendingPathComponent("Motif_Motif.bundle/Contents/Resources/Fonts"),
            resourceURL
        ]
        for dir in fontDirs {
            guard let files = try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(at: dir, includingPropertiesForKeys: nil) else { continue }
            for file in files where file.pathExtension.lowercased() == "ttf" || file.pathExtension.lowercased() == "otf" {
                CTFontManagerRegisterFontsForURL(file as CFURL, .process, nil)
                if let descriptors = CTFontManagerCreateFontDescriptorsFromURL(file as CFURL) as? [CTFontDescriptor],
                   let first = descriptors.first,
                   let familyName = CTFontDescriptorCopyAttribute(first, kCTFontFamilyNameAttribute) as? String {
                    let lower = file.lastPathComponent.lowercased()
                    if lower.contains("jakarta") { resolvedJakartaName = familyName }
                    else if lower.contains("inter") { resolvedInterName = familyName }
                    else if lower.contains("opensans") { resolvedOpenSansName = familyName }
                    else if lower.contains("geist") { resolvedGeistName = familyName }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

public struct TypographyConfiguration: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var fontFamily: AppFontFamily = .inter
    public var windowedBaseFontSize: Double = 14.5
    public var fullscreenBaseFontSize: Double = 15.5
    public var lineSpacingMultiplier: Double = 1.62
    public var comfortableReadingWidth: Double = 820.0

    @MainActor
    public var resolvedFamilyName: String {
        switch fontFamily {
        case .plusJakartaSans: return CustomFontRegistrar.resolvedJakartaName
        case .inter: return CustomFontRegistrar.resolvedInterName
        case .openSans: return CustomFontRegistrar.resolvedOpenSansName
        case .geist: return CustomFontRegistrar.resolvedGeistName
        }
    }

    public func effectiveFontSize(forCanvasWidth width: CGFloat, isFullscreen: Bool) -> CGFloat {
        if isFullscreen { return CGFloat(fullscreenBaseFontSize) }
        let progress = min(1.0, max(0.0, (Double(width) - 860.0) / (1480.0 - 860.0)))
        return CGFloat(windowedBaseFontSize + progress * (fullscreenBaseFontSize - windowedBaseFontSize))
    }

    /// Responsive desktop reading column: generous ~820pt windowed, expanding up to ~1040pt on wide/fullscreen Macs.
    public func effectiveReadingWidth(forCanvasWidth width: CGFloat, isFullscreen: Bool) -> CGFloat {
        let horizontalMargin: CGFloat = width < 960 ? 44.0 : 88.0
        let available = max(460.0, width - horizontalMargin)
        let target: CGFloat
        if isFullscreen || width >= 1360 {
            target = min(1040.0, max(CGFloat(comfortableReadingWidth) + 140.0, width * 0.74))
        } else {
            target = min(880.0, max(CGFloat(comfortableReadingWidth), width * 0.80))
        }
        return min(available, target)
    }

    @MainActor
    public func sans(size: CGFloat, weight: Font.Weight = .regular) -> Font {
        let candidate = resolvedFamilyName
        if NSFont(name: candidate, size: size) != nil {
            return .custom(candidate, size: size).weight(weight)
        }
        return .system(size: size, weight: weight, design: .default)
    }

    @MainActor
    public func nsFont(size: CGFloat, weight: NSFont.Weight = .regular) -> NSFont {
        let candidate = resolvedFamilyName
        if let base = NSFont(name: candidate, size: size) {
            if weight == .regular { return base }
            let desc = base.fontDescriptor.addingAttributes([
                .traits: [NSFontDescriptor.TraitKey.weight: weight]
            ])
            return NSFont(descriptor: desc, size: size) ?? base
        }
        return NSFont.systemFont(ofSize: size, weight: weight)
    }

    public func mono(size: CGFloat = 11.5, weight: Font.Weight = .regular) -> Font {
        .system(size: size, weight: weight, design: .monospaced).monospacedDigit()
    }

    @MainActor
    public func wordmark(size: CGFloat = 16.0) -> Font {
        sans(size: size, weight: .semibold)
    }
}

        ),

            title: "Production Engineer",

        ),
        SlashPersona(
            trigger: "/editor",
            title: "Editorial Writer",
            systemPrompt: "You are an executive editor. Write with crisp clarity, strong rhythm, and zero filler."
        ),

// MARK: - Authoritative Model Thinking Capability Profile (`/api/show`)
public enum ModelThinkingSupportKind: String, Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    case unsupported = "Standard (No Think)"
    case toggleable = "Binary Think (Off / On)"
    case alwaysOnReasoning = "Native <think> Tag"
    case effortTiered = "Multi-Level Thinking"
}

/// Exact representation of thinking choice: preserves verbatim server-declared level strings.
public enum ThinkingModeOption: Hashable, Identifiable, Sendable {
    case off
    case on
    case level(String)

    public var id: String {
        switch self {
        case .off: return "__off__"
        case .on: return "__on__"
        case .level(let raw): return "level:\(raw)"
        }
    }

    public var displayLabel: String {
        switch self {
        case .off: return "Off"
        case .on: return "On"
        case .level(let raw):
            let lower = raw.lowercased()
            if lower == "medium" { return "Med" }
            return raw.capitalized
        }
    }

    public var isThinkingEnabled: Bool {
        if case .off = self { return false }
        return true
    }

    /// JSON value for Ollama's `think` field (Bool or exact String).
    public var ollamaPayloadValue: Any {
        switch self {
        case .off: return false
        case .on: return true
        case .level(let raw): return raw
        }
    }
}

extension ThinkingModeOption: Codable {
    public init(from decoder: Decoder) throws {
        let single = try decoder.singleValueContainer()
        let raw = try single.decode(String.self)
        switch raw {
        case "Off", "__off__": self = .off
        case "On", "__on__": self = .on
        case "Low": self = .level("low")
        case "Med": self = .level("medium")
        case "High": self = .level("high")
        default:
            if raw.hasPrefix("level:") {
                self = .level(String(raw.dropFirst("level:".count)))
            } else {
                self = .level(raw)
            }
        }
    }

    public func encode(to encoder: Encoder) throws {
        var single = encoder.singleValueContainer()
        try single.encode(id)
    }
}

public struct ModelThinkingProfile: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var kind: ModelThinkingSupportKind = .unsupported
    public var supportsSlashDirectives: Bool = false
    public var supportsNativeThinkFlag: Bool = false
    public var supportsEffortLevels: Bool = false
    public var allowsDisable: Bool = true
    public var serverDeclaredLevels: [String]? = nil
    public var serverDefaultOption: ThinkingModeOption? = nil
    public var detectedMechanismSummary: String = "Standard completion model"
    public var detectedTemplateTokens: String = "None"

    public init(
        kind: ModelThinkingSupportKind = .unsupported,
        supportsSlashDirectives: Bool = false,
        supportsNativeThinkFlag: Bool = false,
        supportsEffortLevels: Bool = false,
        allowsDisable: Bool = true,
        serverDeclaredLevels: [String]? = nil,
        serverDefaultOption: ThinkingModeOption? = nil,
        detectedMechanismSummary: String = "Standard completion model",
        detectedTemplateTokens: String = "None"
    ) {
        self.kind = kind
        self.supportsSlashDirectives = supportsSlashDirectives
        self.supportsNativeThinkFlag = supportsNativeThinkFlag
        self.supportsEffortLevels = supportsEffortLevels
        self.allowsDisable = allowsDisable
        self.serverDeclaredLevels = serverDeclaredLevels
        self.serverDefaultOption = serverDefaultOption
        self.detectedMechanismSummary = detectedMechanismSummary
        self.detectedTemplateTokens = detectedTemplateTokens
    }

    public init(from decoder: Decoder) throws {
        let c = try decoder.container(keyedBy: CodingKeys.self)
        kind = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(ModelThinkingSupportKind.self, forKey: .kind)) ?? .unsupported
        supportsSlashDirectives = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .supportsSlashDirectives)) ?? false
        supportsNativeThinkFlag = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .supportsNativeThinkFlag)) ?? false
        supportsEffortLevels = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .supportsEffortLevels)) ?? false
        allowsDisable = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .allowsDisable)) ?? true
        serverDeclaredLevels = try? c.decodeIfPresent([String].self, forKey: .serverDeclaredLevels)
        serverDefaultOption = try? c.decodeIfPresent(ThinkingModeOption.self, forKey: .serverDefaultOption)
        detectedMechanismSummary = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(String.self, forKey: .detectedMechanismSummary)) ?? "Standard completion model"
        detectedTemplateTokens = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(String.self, forKey: .detectedTemplateTokens)) ?? "None"
    }

    public var validOptions: [ThinkingModeOption] {
        if let declared = serverDeclaredLevels, !declared.isEmpty {
            var opts: [ThinkingModeOption] = allowsDisable ? [.off] : []
            for lvl in declared {
                let opt = ThinkingModeOption.level(lvl)
                if !opts.contains(opt) { opts.append(opt) }
            }
            return opts.isEmpty ? [.on] : opts
        }
        switch kind {
        case .unsupported:
            return [.off]
        case .toggleable:
            return allowsDisable ? [.off, .on] : [.on]
        case .alwaysOnReasoning:
            return [.on]
        case .effortTiered:
            return allowsDisable
                ? [.off, .level("low"), .level("medium"), .level("high")]
                : [.level("low"), .level("medium"), .level("high")]
        }
    }

    public var preferredDefaultOption: ThinkingModeOption {
        if let def = serverDefaultOption, validOptions.contains(def) {
            return def
        }
        return validOptions.last ?? .off
    }
}

public enum MirostatMode: Int, CaseIterable, Codable, Identifiable, Sendable {
    case disabled = 0
    case mirostat1 = 1
    case mirostat2 = 2

    public var id: Int { rawValue }
    public var label: String {
        switch self {
        case .disabled: return "Off (0)"
        case .mirostat1: return "Mirostat v1"
        case .mirostat2: return "Mirostat v2"
        }
    }
}

public enum KVCacheQuantization: String, CaseIterable, Codable, Identifiable, Sendable {
    case f16 = "f16"
    case q8_0 = "q8_0"
    case q4_0 = "q4_0"

    public var id: String { rawValue }
    public var displayTitle: String {
        switch self {
        case .f16: return "FP16 (Uncompressed)"
        case .q8_0: return "Q8_0 (8-bit · Recommended · ~48% less KV RAM)"
        case .q4_0: return "Q4_0 (4-bit · Tight RAM · ~72% less KV RAM)"
        }
    }
    public var bytesPerElement: Double {
        switch self {
        case .f16: return 2.0
        case .q8_0: return 1.0625
        case .q4_0: return 0.5625
        }
    }
}

/// Optional sampling parameters preserve Modelfile defaults (`nil` = omit from Ollama `options`).
public struct ModelRuntimeParameters: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var thinkingMode: ThinkingModeOption = .on
    public var forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported: Bool = false
    public var tightRAMModeEnabled: Bool = false
    public var numCtx: Int = 8192
    public var kvCacheQuant: KVCacheQuantization = .q8_0

    // Optional sampling overrides (`nil` = Model Native Default from Modelfile)
    public var temperature: Double? = nil
    public var topP: Double? = nil
    public var topK: Int? = nil
    public var minP: Double? = nil
    public var repeatPenalty: Double? = nil
    public var typicalP: Double? = nil
    public var repeatLastN: Int? = nil
    public var presencePenalty: Double? = nil
    public var frequencyPenalty: Double? = nil
    public var mirostat: MirostatMode? = nil
    public var mirostatTau: Double? = nil
    public var mirostatEta: Double? = nil
    public var numPredict: Int? = nil
    public var numKeep: Int? = nil
    public var seed: Int? = nil
    public var penalizeNewline: Bool? = nil

    // Runtime & hardware execution parameters (must match between warm-up & chat!)
    public var numBatch: Int = 512
    public var numThread: Int = 0
    public var numGpu: Int = -1
    public var keepAliveMinutes: Int = 10
    public var stopSequencesCSV: String = ""
    public var useMmap: Bool = true
    public var useMlock: Bool = false
    public var enforceJSONMode: Bool = false
    public var jsonSchemaText: String = ""

    public init(
        thinkingMode: ThinkingModeOption = .on,
        forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported: Bool = false,
        tightRAMModeEnabled: Bool = false,
        numCtx: Int = 8192,
        kvCacheQuant: KVCacheQuantization = .q8_0,
        temperature: Double? = nil,
        topP: Double? = nil,
        topK: Int? = nil,
        minP: Double? = nil,
        repeatPenalty: Double? = nil,
        numPredict: Int? = nil,
        numKeep: Int? = nil,
        numBatch: Int = 512,
        numThread: Int = 0,
        numGpu: Int = -1,
        keepAliveMinutes: Int = 10,
        useMmap: Bool = true,
        useMlock: Bool = false
    ) {
        self.thinkingMode = thinkingMode
        self.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported = forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported
        self.tightRAMModeEnabled = tightRAMModeEnabled
        self.numCtx = numCtx
        self.kvCacheQuant = kvCacheQuant
        self.temperature = temperature
        self.topP = topP
        self.topK = topK
        self.minP = minP
        self.repeatPenalty = repeatPenalty
        self.numPredict = numPredict
        self.numKeep = numKeep
        self.numBatch = numBatch
        self.numThread = numThread
        self.numGpu = numGpu
        self.keepAliveMinutes = keepAliveMinutes
        self.useMmap = useMmap
        self.useMlock = useMlock
    }

    public init(from decoder: Decoder) throws {
        let c = try decoder.container(keyedBy: CodingKeys.self)
        thinkingMode = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(ThinkingModeOption.self, forKey: .thinkingMode)) ?? .on
        forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported)) ?? false
        tightRAMModeEnabled = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .tightRAMModeEnabled)) ?? false
        numCtx = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .numCtx)) ?? 8192
        kvCacheQuant = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(KVCacheQuantization.self, forKey: .kvCacheQuant)) ?? .q8_0
        temperature = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .temperature)
        topP = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .topP)
        topK = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .topK)
        minP = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .minP)
        repeatPenalty = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .repeatPenalty)
        typicalP = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .typicalP)
        repeatLastN = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .repeatLastN)
        presencePenalty = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .presencePenalty)
        frequencyPenalty = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .frequencyPenalty)
        mirostat = try? c.decodeIfPresent(MirostatMode.self, forKey: .mirostat)
        mirostatTau = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .mirostatTau)
        mirostatEta = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .mirostatEta)
        numPredict = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .numPredict)
        numKeep = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .numKeep)
        seed = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .seed)
        penalizeNewline = try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .penalizeNewline)
        numBatch = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .numBatch)) ?? 512
        numThread = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .numThread)) ?? 0
        numGpu = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .numGpu)) ?? -1
        keepAliveMinutes = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .keepAliveMinutes)) ?? 10
        stopSequencesCSV = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(String.self, forKey: .stopSequencesCSV)) ?? ""
        useMmap = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .useMmap)) ?? true
        useMlock = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .useMlock)) ?? false
        enforceJSONMode = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .enforceJSONMode)) ?? false
        jsonSchemaText = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(String.self, forKey: .jsonSchemaText)) ?? ""
    }

    /// Exact hardware/context load options shared by warm-up and chat requests to prevent double-reloads.
        /// Emit `num_ctx` plus ONLY non-default runtime options so Ollama never triggers spurious runner reloads.
    /// Emit `num_ctx` plus ONLY non-default runtime options so Ollama never triggers spurious runner reloads.
public func loadOptionsDictionary() -> [String: Any] {
    var opts: [String: Any] = [
        "num_ctx": numCtx
    ]
    if numBatch != 512 { opts["num_batch"] = numBatch }
    if !useMmap { opts["use_mmap"] = false }
    if useMlock { opts["use_mlock"] = true }
    if numThread > 0 { opts["num_thread"] = numThread }
    if numGpu >= 0 { opts["num_gpu"] = numGpu }
    return opts
}

    /// Full chat options: always includes load options, plus ONLY explicitly set sampling overrides.
    public func chatOptionsDictionary() -> [String: Any] {
        var opts = loadOptionsDictionary()
        if let v = temperature { opts["temperature"] = v }
        if let v = topP { opts["top_p"] = v }
        if let v = topK { opts["top_k"] = v }
        if let v = minP { opts["min_p"] = v }
        if let v = repeatPenalty { opts["repeat_penalty"] = v }
        if let v = typicalP { opts["typical_p"] = v }
        if let v = repeatLastN { opts["repeat_last_n"] = v }
        if let v = presencePenalty { opts["presence_penalty"] = v }
        if let v = frequencyPenalty { opts["frequency_penalty"] = v }
        if let v = mirostat, v != .disabled {
            opts["mirostat"] = v.rawValue
            if let tau = mirostatTau { opts["mirostat_tau"] = tau }
            if let eta = mirostatEta { opts["mirostat_eta"] = eta }
        }
        if let v = numPredict { opts["num_predict"] = v }
        if let v = numKeep { opts["num_keep"] = v }
        if let v = seed, v >= 0 { opts["seed"] = v }
        if let v = penalizeNewline { opts["penalize_newline"] = v }

        let stops = stopSequencesCSV
            .components(separatedBy: ",")
            .map { $0.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines) }
            .filter { !$0.isEmpty }
        if !stops.isEmpty { opts["stop"] = stops }
        return opts
    }

    public static let nativeBalanced = ModelRuntimeParameters(
        thinkingMode: .on, numCtx: 8192, kvCacheQuant: .q8_0
    )
    public static let precise = ModelRuntimeParameters(
        thinkingMode: .on, numCtx: 16384, kvCacheQuant: .q8_0,
        temperature: 0.15, topP: 0.85, topK: 20, minP: 0.08, repeatPenalty: 1.05
    )
    public static let tightRAM = ModelRuntimeParameters(
        thinkingMode: .off, tightRAMModeEnabled: true, numCtx: 4096, kvCacheQuant: .q4_0,
        numPredict: 8192, numKeep: 128, numBatch: 128, keepAliveMinutes: 5
    )
}

    public var id: UUID = UUID()
    public var name: String
    public var summary: String
    public var parameters: ModelRuntimeParameters
    public var systemPrompt: String

    public static let defaults: [ParameterPreset] = [
        ParameterPreset(
            name: "Model Default",
            summary: "Respects Modelfile defaults (Q8_0 KV · 8k ctx)",
            parameters: .nativeBalanced,
            systemPrompt: "You are a clear, accurate, and thoughtful assistant."
        ),
        ParameterPreset(
            name: "Precise",
            summary: "Deterministic coding & architecture (Temp 0.15 · 16k ctx)",
            parameters: .precise,
            systemPrompt: "You are a principal software engineer. Provide concise, production-grade code and direct technical explanations."
        ),
        ParameterPreset(
            name: "Tight RAM",
            summary: "Low-memory mode (Q4_0 KV · 4k ctx · 128 batch)",
            parameters: .tightRAM,
            systemPrompt: "You are a concise, accurate assistant."
        )
    ]
}

// MARK: - Model Descriptor (`/api/tags` + `/api/show` + `/api/ps`)
public enum ModelResidencyState: String, Codable, Sendable {
    case dormantOnDisk = "On disk"
    case loadingIntoMemory = "Loading"
    case activeInUnifiedRAM = "In RAM"
}

public struct LocalModelDescriptor: Identifiable, Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var id: String { name }
    public var name: String
    public var displayName: String
    public var family: String
    public var parameterSize: String
    public var quantizationLevel: String
    public var sizeBytes: Int64
    public var digest: String
    public var modifiedAt: Date
    public var residency: ModelResidencyState = .dormantOnDisk
    public var totalLoadedSizeBytes: Int64 = 0
    public var vramAllocatedBytes: Int64 = 0
    public var numLayers: Int = 32
    public var numKVHeads: Int = 8
    public var headDim: Int = 128
    public var maxContextLength: Int = 32768
    public var supportsVision: Bool = false
    public var supportsTools: Bool = false
    public var thinkingProfile: ModelThinkingProfile = ModelThinkingProfile()
    public var hasIntrospectedShowMetadata: Bool = false
    public var residencyExpiresAt: Date? = nil
    public var loadedContextLength: Int? = nil

    public var sizeGB: Double { max(0.1, Double(sizeBytes) / 1_073_741_824.0) }
    public var formattedSize: String { String(format: "%.1f GB", sizeGB) }
    public var shortDisplayName: String { Self.shortenModelName(displayName) }

    /// Exact GPU offload ratio from `/api/ps` (`size_vram / size`). Detects silent CPU spillover!
    public var gpuOffloadFraction: Double? {
        guard residency == .activeInUnifiedRAM, totalLoadedSizeBytes > 0 else { return nil }
        return min(1.0, max(0.0, Double(vramAllocatedBytes) / Double(totalLoadedSizeBytes)))
    }

    public var hasCPUSpillover: Bool {
        guard let frac = gpuOffloadFraction else { return false }
        return frac < 0.98
    }

    public static func shortenModelName(_ raw: String) -> String {
        var s = raw.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        if let lastSlash = s.lastIndex(of: "/") {
            s = String(s[s.index(after: lastSlash)...])
        }
        if s.lowercased().hasSuffix(":latest") {
            s = String(s.dropLast(":latest".count))
        }
        return s
    }
}

// MARK: - Attachments & Message Tree Ledger
public enum AttachmentKind: String, Codable, Sendable {
    case pastedDocumentChip = "Pasted text"
    case sourceCodeFile = "File"
    case downscaledVisionImage = "Image"
}

public struct MessageAttachment: Identifiable, Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var id: UUID = UUID()
    public var title: String
    public var kind: AttachmentKind
    public var byteCount: Int
    public var lineCount: Int
    public var previewText: String
    public var localAssetFilename: String?
    public var base64ImagePayload: String? = nil

    private enum CodingKeys: String, CodingKey {
        case id, title, kind, byteCount, lineCount, previewText, localAssetFilename
    }

    public var tokenEstimate: Int {
        if kind == .downscaledVisionImage { return 768 }
        return max(1, byteCount / 4)
    }

    public var formattedByteSize: String {
        if byteCount >= 1_048_576 { return String(format: "%.1f MB", Double(byteCount) / 1_048_576.0) }
        if byteCount >= 1024 { return String(format: "%.1f KB", Double(byteCount) / 1024.0) }
        return "\(byteCount) B"
    }
}

public enum MessageRole: String, Codable, Sendable {
    case system
    case user
    case assistant
}

public struct GenerationMetrics: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var tokensPerSecond: Double = 0.0
    public var totalGenerationDurationSec: Double = 0.0
    public var thinkingDurationSec: Double = 0.0
    public var loadDurationSec: Double = 0.0
    public var promptEvalDurationSec: Double = 0.0
    public var totalTokens: Int = 0
    public var thinkingTokens: Int = 0
    public var promptEvalTokens: Int = 0
    public var promptEvalCachedTokens: Int = 0
    public var doneReason: String = ""

    public init(
        tokensPerSecond: Double = 0.0,
        totalGenerationDurationSec: Double = 0.0,
        thinkingDurationSec: Double = 0.0,
        loadDurationSec: Double = 0.0,
        promptEvalDurationSec: Double = 0.0,
        totalTokens: Int = 0,
        thinkingTokens: Int = 0,
        promptEvalTokens: Int = 0,
        promptEvalCachedTokens: Int = 0,
        doneReason: String = ""
    ) {
        self.tokensPerSecond = tokensPerSecond
        self.totalGenerationDurationSec = totalGenerationDurationSec
        self.thinkingDurationSec = thinkingDurationSec
        self.loadDurationSec = loadDurationSec
        self.promptEvalDurationSec = promptEvalDurationSec
        self.totalTokens = totalTokens
        self.thinkingTokens = thinkingTokens
        self.promptEvalTokens = promptEvalTokens
        self.promptEvalCachedTokens = promptEvalCachedTokens
        self.doneReason = doneReason
    }

    public init(from decoder: Decoder) throws {
        let c = try decoder.container(keyedBy: CodingKeys.self)
        tokensPerSecond = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .tokensPerSecond)) ?? 0.0
        totalGenerationDurationSec = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .totalGenerationDurationSec)) ?? 0.0
        thinkingDurationSec = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .thinkingDurationSec)) ?? 0.0
        loadDurationSec = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .loadDurationSec)) ?? 0.0
        promptEvalDurationSec = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Double.self, forKey: .promptEvalDurationSec)) ?? 0.0
        totalTokens = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .totalTokens)) ?? 0
        thinkingTokens = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .thinkingTokens)) ?? 0
        promptEvalTokens = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .promptEvalTokens)) ?? 0
        promptEvalCachedTokens = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .promptEvalCachedTokens)) ?? 0
        doneReason = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(String.self, forKey: .doneReason)) ?? ""
    }
}

public struct ConversationMessageNode: Identifiable, Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var id: UUID = UUID()
    public var parentID: UUID?
    public var childrenIDs: [UUID] = []
    public var activeChildIndex: Int = 0
    public var role: MessageRole
    public var content: String
    public var apiPromptOverride: String? = nil
    public var isSummaryHandoffRequest: Bool = false
    public var thinkingTrace: String = ""
    public var isThinkingComplete: Bool = true
    public var isThinkingExpanded: Bool = false
    public var createdAt: Date = Date()
    public var modelUsed: String = ""
    public var modelDigest: String = ""
    public var numCtxUsed: Int = 0
    public var attachments: [MessageAttachment] = []
    public var metrics: GenerationMetrics? = nil
    public var wasStoppedMidStream: Bool = false
    public var wasStoppedMidThinking: Bool = false
    public var errorNote: String? = nil

    public init(
        id: UUID = UUID(),
        parentID: UUID? = nil,
        childrenIDs: [UUID] = [],
        activeChildIndex: Int = 0,
        role: MessageRole,
        content: String,
        apiPromptOverride: String? = nil,
        isSummaryHandoffRequest: Bool = false,
        thinkingTrace: String = "",
        isThinkingComplete: Bool = true,
        isThinkingExpanded: Bool = false,
        createdAt: Date = Date(),
        modelUsed: String = "",
        modelDigest: String = "",
        numCtxUsed: Int = 0,
        attachments: [MessageAttachment] = [],
        metrics: GenerationMetrics? = nil,
        wasStoppedMidStream: Bool = false,
        wasStoppedMidThinking: Bool = false,
        errorNote: String? = nil
    ) {
        self.id = id
        self.parentID = parentID
        self.childrenIDs = childrenIDs
        self.activeChildIndex = activeChildIndex
        self.role = role
        self.content = content
        self.apiPromptOverride = apiPromptOverride
        self.isSummaryHandoffRequest = isSummaryHandoffRequest
        self.thinkingTrace = thinkingTrace
        self.isThinkingComplete = isThinkingComplete
        self.isThinkingExpanded = isThinkingExpanded
        self.createdAt = createdAt
        self.modelUsed = modelUsed
        self.modelDigest = modelDigest
        self.numCtxUsed = numCtxUsed
        self.attachments = attachments
        self.metrics = metrics
        self.wasStoppedMidStream = wasStoppedMidStream
        self.wasStoppedMidThinking = wasStoppedMidThinking
        self.errorNote = errorNote
    }

    public init(from decoder: Decoder) throws {
        let c = try decoder.container(keyedBy: CodingKeys.self)
        id = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(UUID.self, forKey: .id)) ?? UUID()
        parentID = try? c.decodeIfPresent(UUID.self, forKey: .parentID)
        childrenIDs = (try? c.decodeIfPresent([UUID].self, forKey: .childrenIDs)) ?? []
        activeChildIndex = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .activeChildIndex)) ?? 0
        role = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(MessageRole.self, forKey: .role)) ?? .user
        content = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(String.self, forKey: .content)) ?? ""
        apiPromptOverride = try? c.decodeIfPresent(String.self, forKey: .apiPromptOverride)
        isSummaryHandoffRequest = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .isSummaryHandoffRequest)) ?? false
        thinkingTrace = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(String.self, forKey: .thinkingTrace)) ?? ""
        isThinkingComplete = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .isThinkingComplete)) ?? true
        isThinkingExpanded = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .isThinkingExpanded)) ?? false
        createdAt = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Date.self, forKey: .createdAt)) ?? Date()
        modelUsed = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(String.self, forKey: .modelUsed)) ?? ""
        modelDigest = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(String.self, forKey: .modelDigest)) ?? ""
        numCtxUsed = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Int.self, forKey: .numCtxUsed)) ?? 0
        attachments = (try? c.decodeIfPresent([MessageAttachment].self, forKey: .attachments)) ?? []
        metrics = try? c.decodeIfPresent(GenerationMetrics.self, forKey: .metrics)
        wasStoppedMidStream = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .wasStoppedMidStream)) ?? false
        wasStoppedMidThinking = (try? c.decodeIfPresent(Bool.self, forKey: .wasStoppedMidThinking)) ?? false
        errorNote = try? c.decodeIfPresent(String.self, forKey: .errorNote)
    }

    /// No double-counting: `content` holds only user prose; attachments are counted once.
    public var estimatedTokenFootprint: Int {
        let textTok = content.isEmpty ? 0 : max(1, content.count / 4)
        let attTok = attachments.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.tokenEstimate }
        return max(1, textTok + attTok)
    }
}

public struct WorkspaceArtifact: Identifiable, Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var id: UUID = UUID()
    public var title: String
    public var language: String
    public var content: String
    public var createdAt: Date = Date()
    public var sourceMessageID: UUID?
}

public struct ConversationSession: Identifiable, Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var id: UUID = UUID()
    public var title: String
    public var folderID: UUID? = nil
    public var isPinned: Bool = false
    public var isTrashed: Bool = false
    public var trashedAt: Date? = nil
    public var createdAt: Date = Date()
    public var updatedAt: Date = Date()
    public var selectedModelName: String = ""
    public var activePersonaTrigger: String? = nil
    public var systemPrompt: String = "You are a clear, accurate assistant."
    public var parameters: ModelRuntimeParameters = .nativeBalanced

    public var draftText: String = ""
    public var draftAttachments: [MessageAttachment] = []

    public var nodes: [UUID: ConversationMessageNode] = [:]
    public var rootMessageIDs: [UUID] = []
    public var activeRootIndex: Int = 0
    public var artifacts: [WorkspaceArtifact] = []

    public var activeBranchThread: [ConversationMessageNode] {
        guard !rootMessageIDs.isEmpty else { return [] }
        let clampedRoot = min(max(0, activeRootIndex), rootMessageIDs.count - 1)
        var currentID: UUID? = rootMessageIDs[clampedRoot]
        var thread: [ConversationMessageNode] = []
        var visited = Set<UUID>()

        while let id = currentID, !visited.contains(id), let node = nodes[id] {
            visited.insert(id)
            thread.append(node)
            if node.childrenIDs.isEmpty {
                currentID = nil
            } else {
                let idx = min(max(0, node.activeChildIndex), node.childrenIDs.count - 1)
                currentID = node.childrenIDs[idx]
            }
        }
        return thread
    }

    public var estimatedActiveTokens: Int {
        let thread = activeBranchThread
        let sysTokens = systemPrompt.isEmpty ? 0 : max(1, systemPrompt.count / 4)
        let msgTokens = thread.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.estimatedTokenFootprint }
        let draftTok = (draftText.count / 4) + draftAttachments.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.tokenEstimate }
        let heuristicTotal = sysTokens + msgTokens + draftTok
        if let lastAssistant = thread.last(where: { $0.role == .assistant }),
           let reportedPrompt = lastAssistant.metrics?.promptEvalTokens,
           reportedPrompt > 0 {
            let replyTok = lastAssistant.metrics?.totalTokens ?? lastAssistant.estimatedTokenFootprint
            return max(heuristicTotal, reportedPrompt + replyTok + draftTok)
        }
        return heuristicTotal
    }

    public func siblingInfo(for nodeID: UUID) -> (currentIndex: Int, totalCount: Int) {
        guard let node = nodes[nodeID] else { return (0, 1) }
        if let parentID = node.parentID, let parent = nodes[parentID] {
            let idx = parent.childrenIDs.firstIndex(of: nodeID) ?? 0
            return (idx, max(1, parent.childrenIDs.count))
        } else {
            let idx = rootMessageIDs.firstIndex(of: nodeID) ?? 0
            return (idx, max(1, rootMessageIDs.count))
        }
    }

    public static func sanitizeTitle(_ raw: String) -> String {
        let firstLine = raw.components(separatedBy: .newlines).first ?? raw
        let stripped = firstLine
            .replacingOccurrences(of: "/think", with: "")
            .replacingOccurrences(of: "/no_think", with: "")
            .replacingOccurrences(of: "**", with: "")
            .replacingOccurrences(of: "__", with: "")
            .replacingOccurrences(of: "`", with: "")
            .replacingOccurrences(of: "#", with: "")
        let collapsed = stripped
            .components(separatedBy: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
            .filter { !$0.isEmpty }
            .joined(separator: " ")
        let punctuation = CharacterSet(charactersIn: ".,;:!?-–—'\"/\\| ")
        let trimmed = collapsed.trimmingCharacters(in: punctuation)
        guard !trimmed.isEmpty else { return "New Chat" }
        if trimmed.count > 46 {
            return String(trimmed.prefix(45)).trimmingCharacters(in: punctuation) + "…"
        }
        return trimmed
    }
}

public struct ProjectFolder: Identifiable, Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var id: UUID = UUID()
    public var name: String
    public var isExpanded: Bool = true
}

public struct RegistryDownloadTask: Identifiable, Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public var id: UUID = UUID()
    public var modelReference: String
    public var statusText: String
    public var layerProgress: [String: LayerBytes] = [:]
    public var isCompleted: Bool

    public struct LayerBytes: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
        public var completed: Int64
        public var total: Int64
    }

    public var completedBytes: Int64 {
        layerProgress.values.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.completed }
    }

    public var totalBytes: Int64 {
        layerProgress.values.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.total }
    }

    public var progressFraction: Double {
        let tot = totalBytes
        guard tot > 0 else { return isCompleted ? 1.0 : 0.0 }
        return min(1.0, Double(completedBytes) / Double(tot))
    }

    public var formattedProgressGB: String {
        let tot = totalBytes
        guard tot > 1024 else { return statusText }
        let doneGB = Double(completedBytes) / 1_073_741_824.0
        let totGB = Double(tot) / 1_073_741_824.0
        let pct = Int(progressFraction * 100.0)
        return String(format: "%.2f / %.2f GB (%d%%)", doneGB, totGB, pct)
    }
}

(
cat << 'EOF' > Motif/Sources/Motif/Engine/ReasoningAndMemoryEngine.swift
import Foundation
import Darwin
import Metal

// MARK: - Stop-Safe Stateful Thinking Stream Parser
public final class StatefulThinkingStreamParser {
    public enum State {
        case normalOrAwaitingOpeningTag
        case insideThinkingBlock
        case postThinkingAnswer
    }

    public private(set) var state: State = .normalOrAwaitingOpeningTag
    private var pendingBuffer: String = ""
    private let openTags = ["<think>", "<thought>", "<reasoning>", "<|thought|>"]
    private let closeTags = ["</think>", "</thought>", "</reasoning>", "<|/thought|>"]

    public private(set) var accumulatedThinking: String = ""
    public private(set) var accumulatedAnswer: String = ""
    public private(set) var isCurrentlyThinking: Bool = false
    public private(set) var thinkingStartTime: Date?
    public private(set) var thinkingDurationSeconds: Double = 0.0

    public init() {}

    public func preloadExisting(
        thinking: String,
        answer: String,
        resumeInsideThinking: Bool,
        priorThinkingDuration: Double = 0.0
    ) {
        accumulatedThinking = thinking
        accumulatedAnswer = answer
        thinkingDurationSeconds = priorThinkingDuration
        if resumeInsideThinking {
            state = .insideThinkingBlock
            isCurrentlyThinking = true
            thinkingStartTime = Date().addingTimeInterval(-priorThinkingDuration)
        } else if !thinking.isEmpty || !answer.isEmpty {
            state = .postThinkingAnswer
            isCurrentlyThinking = false
        } else {
            state = .normalOrAwaitingOpeningTag
            isCurrentlyThinking = false
        }
    }

    public func ingestExplicitThinkingDelta(_ delta: String) {
        guard !delta.isEmpty else { return }
        if thinkingStartTime == nil { thinkingStartTime = Date() }
        isCurrentlyThinking = true
        accumulatedThinking += delta
        if let start = thinkingStartTime {
            thinkingDurationSeconds = Date().timeIntervalSince(start)
        }
    }

    public func ingestContentChunk(_ chunk: String) {
        guard !chunk.isEmpty else { return }
        pendingBuffer += chunk
        processBuffer()
    }

    public func flushPendingBufferWithoutLosingState(markComplete: Bool) {
        if !pendingBuffer.isEmpty {
            switch state {
            case .insideThinkingBlock: accumulatedThinking += pendingBuffer
            case .normalOrAwaitingOpeningTag, .postThinkingAnswer: accumulatedAnswer += pendingBuffer
            }
            pendingBuffer = ""
        }
        if let start = thinkingStartTime, isCurrentlyThinking {
            thinkingDurationSeconds = Date().timeIntervalSince(start)
        }
        if markComplete {
            isCurrentlyThinking = false
        }
    }

    private func processBuffer() {
        var keepProcessing = true
        while keepProcessing && !pendingBuffer.isEmpty {
            keepProcessing = false
            switch state {
            case .normalOrAwaitingOpeningTag:
                if let match = findFirstTag(in: pendingBuffer, tags: openTags) {
                    let prefix = String(pendingBuffer[..<match.range.lowerBound])
                    if !prefix.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty {
                        accumulatedAnswer += prefix
                    }
                    pendingBuffer.removeSubrange(..<match.range.upperBound)
                    state = .insideThinkingBlock
                    isCurrentlyThinking = true
                    if thinkingStartTime == nil { thinkingStartTime = Date() }
                    keepProcessing = true
                } else if let partialIdx = trailingPartialTagIndex(in: pendingBuffer, tags: openTags) {
                    accumulatedAnswer += String(pendingBuffer[..<partialIdx])
                    pendingBuffer.removeSubrange(..<partialIdx)
                } else {
                    if isCurrentlyThinking && !accumulatedThinking.isEmpty {
                        if let start = thinkingStartTime {
                            thinkingDurationSeconds = Date().timeIntervalSince(start)
                        }
                        isCurrentlyThinking = false
                    }
                    accumulatedAnswer += pendingBuffer
                    pendingBuffer = ""
                }

            case .insideThinkingBlock:
                if let start = thinkingStartTime {
                    thinkingDurationSeconds = Date().timeIntervalSince(start)
                }
                if let match = findFirstTag(in: pendingBuffer, tags: closeTags) {
                    accumulatedThinking += String(pendingBuffer[..<match.range.lowerBound])
                    pendingBuffer.removeSubrange(..<match.range.upperBound)
                    if pendingBuffer.hasPrefix("\n") { pendingBuffer.removeFirst() }
                    isCurrentlyThinking = false
                    state = .postThinkingAnswer
                    keepProcessing = true
                } else if let partialIdx = trailingPartialTagIndex(in: pendingBuffer, tags: closeTags) {
                    accumulatedThinking += String(pendingBuffer[..<partialIdx])
                    pendingBuffer.removeSubrange(..<partialIdx)
                } else {
                    accumulatedThinking += pendingBuffer
                    pendingBuffer = ""
                }

            case .postThinkingAnswer:
                if let match = findFirstTag(in: pendingBuffer, tags: closeTags) {
                    accumulatedThinking += String(pendingBuffer[..<match.range.lowerBound])
                    pendingBuffer.removeSubrange(..<match.range.upperBound)
                    if pendingBuffer.hasPrefix("\n") { pendingBuffer.removeFirst() }
                    isCurrentlyThinking = false
                    keepProcessing = true
                } else {
                    accumulatedAnswer += pendingBuffer
                    pendingBuffer = ""
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private func findFirstTag(in text: String, tags: [String]) -> (tag: String, range: Range<String.Index>)? {
        var earliest: (String, Range<String.Index>)? = nil
        for tag in tags {
            if let range = text.range(of: tag) {
                if earliest == nil || range.lowerBound < earliest!.1.lowerBound {
                    earliest = (tag, range)
                }
            }
        }
        return earliest
    }

    private func trailingPartialTagIndex(in text: String, tags: [String]) -> String.Index? {
        guard let lastAngle = text.lastIndex(of: "<") else { return nil }
        let suffix = String(text[lastAngle...])
        for tag in tags where tag.hasPrefix(suffix) && suffix.count < tag.count {
            return lastAngle
        }
        return nil
    }
}

// MARK: - Kernel Mach VM + Targeted proc_pidinfo + Metal Headroom Telemetry
public struct UnifiedMemorySnapshot: Equatable, Sendable {
    public var totalPhysicalRAMBytes: Int64
    public var totalSystemUsedBytes: Int64
    public var systemAndOSWiredBytes: Int64
    public var modelWeightsResidentBytes: Int64
    public var kvCacheAllocatedBytes: Int64
    public var metalRecommendedMaxBytes: Int64
    public var baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad: Int64
    public var targetModelSizeBytes: Int64
    public var historySamplesGB: [Double]

    public var freeHeadroomBytes: Int64 { max(0, totalPhysicalRAMBytes - totalSystemUsedBytes) }
    public var totalGB: Double { Double(totalPhysicalRAMBytes) / 1_073_741_824.0 }
    public var usedGB: Double { Double(totalSystemUsedBytes) / 1_073_741_824.0 }
    public var systemGB: Double { Double(systemAndOSWiredBytes) / 1_073_741_824.0 }
    public var weightsGB: Double { Double(modelWeightsResidentBytes) / 1_073_741_824.0 }
    public var kvCacheGB: Double { Double(kvCacheAllocatedBytes) / 1_073_741_824.0 }
    public var headroomGB: Double { Double(freeHeadroomBytes) / 1_073_741_824.0 }
    public var formattedHeadroom: String { String(format: "%.1f GB free", headroomGB) }
    public var metalMaxGB: Double { Double(metalRecommendedMaxBytes) / 1_073_741_824.0 }
    public var targetModelGB: Double { Double(targetModelSizeBytes) / 1_073_741_824.0 }

    public var activeRAMDeltaGB: Double {
        if baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad > 0 {
            let rawDelta = Double(totalSystemUsedBytes - baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad) / 1_073_741_824.0
            if rawDelta > 0.05 { return rawDelta }
        }
        return weightsGB
    }

    public var loadProgressAgainstTarget: Double {
        guard targetModelSizeBytes > 0 else { return 0.0 }
        let allocated = max(Double(modelWeightsResidentBytes), activeRAMDeltaGB * 1_073_741_824.0)
        return min(0.99, max(0.0, allocated / Double(targetModelSizeBytes)))
    }

    public var usageFraction: Double {
        guard totalPhysicalRAMBytes > 0 else { return 0 }
        return min(1.0, Double(totalSystemUsedBytes) / Double(totalPhysicalRAMBytes))
    }

    public var weightsFraction: Double {
        guard totalPhysicalRAMBytes > 0 else { return 0 }
        return min(1.0, Double(modelWeightsResidentBytes) / Double(totalPhysicalRAMBytes))
    }
}

public enum HardwareMemoryEngine {
    public static let cachedMetalRecommendedMaxBytes: Int64 = {
        let totalRAM = Int64(ProcessInfo.processInfo.physicalMemory)
        return Int64(MTLCreateSystemDefaultDevice()?.recommendedMaxWorkingSetSize ?? UInt64(Double(totalRAM) * 0.72))
    }()

    /// Expensive PID scan: call ONLY during active model weight loading or explicit popover inspection.
    public static func queryOllamaProcessResidentBytes() -> Int64 {
        let maxPids = 2048
        var pids = [pid_t](repeating: 0, count: maxPids)
        let byteSize = Int32(maxPids * MemoryLayout<pid_t>.stride)
        let countBytes = proc_listallpids(&pids, byteSize)
        guard countBytes > 0 else { return 0 }
        let pidCount = Int(countBytes) / MemoryLayout<pid_t>.stride

        var totalOllamaRSS: Int64 = 0
        var nameBuffer = [CChar](repeating: 0, count: 256)

        for i in 0..<pidCount {
            let pid = pids[i]
            guard pid > 0 else { continue }
            let nameLen = proc_name(pid, &nameBuffer, UInt32(nameBuffer.count))
            if nameLen > 0 {
                let procName = String(cString: nameBuffer).lowercased()
                if procName.contains("ollama") {
                    var taskInfo = proc_taskinfo()
                    let infoSize = Int32(MemoryLayout<proc_taskinfo>.stride)
                    if proc_pidinfo(pid, PROC_PIDTASKINFO, 0, &taskInfo, infoSize) == infoSize {
                        totalOllamaRSS += Int64(taskInfo.pti_resident_size)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return totalOllamaRSS
    }

    public static func estimateKVCacheBytes(
        numLayers: Int,
        numKVHeads: Int,
        headDim: Int,
        numCtx: Int,
        quant: KVCacheQuantization,
        numParallel: Int
    ) -> Int64 {
        let perToken = 2.0
            * Double(max(1, numLayers))
            * Double(max(1, numKVHeads))
            * Double(max(1, headDim))
            * quant.bytesPerElement
            * Double(max(1, numParallel))
        return Int64(perToken * Double(max(1, numCtx)))
    }

    public static func queryAppleSiliconMemory(
        liveOllamaTotalBytes: Int64,
        estimatedKVCacheBytes: Int64,
        baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad: Int64,
        baselineOllamaRSSBeforeLoad: Int64,
        targetModelSizeBytes: Int64,
        isLoadingWeights: Bool,
        sampleKernelProcessRSS: Bool,
        previousHistory: [Double]
    ) -> UnifiedMemorySnapshot {
        let totalRAM = Int64(ProcessInfo.processInfo.physicalMemory)
        var vmStats = vm_statistics64()
        var count = mach_msg_type_number_t(MemoryLayout<vm_statistics64>.size / MemoryLayout<integer_t>.size)
        let hostPort = mach_host_self()
        var pageSize: vm_size_t = vm_kernel_page_size
        host_page_size(hostPort, &pageSize)
        let pageBytes = Int64(pageSize)

        let kernResult = withUnsafeMutablePointer(to: &vmStats) {
            $0.withMemoryRebound(to: integer_t.self, capacity: Int(count)) {
                host_statistics64(hostPort, HOST_VM_INFO64, $0, &count)
            }
        }

        var trueSystemUsedBytes: Int64 = totalRAM / 2
        if kernResult == KERN_SUCCESS {
            let appPages = max(0, Int64(vmStats.internal_page_count) - Int64(vmStats.purgeable_count))
            let wiredPages = Int64(vmStats.wire_count)
            let compressedPages = Int64(vmStats.compressor_page_count)
            trueSystemUsedBytes = min(totalRAM, (appPages + wiredPages + compressedPages) * pageBytes)
        }

        let kernelRSS: Int64 = (isLoadingWeights || sampleKernelProcessRSS)
            ? queryOllamaProcessResidentBytes()
            : 0

        let effectiveOllamaBytes: Int64
        if isLoadingWeights {
            let runnerDelta = max(0, kernelRSS - baselineOllamaRSSBeforeLoad)
            effectiveOllamaBytes = max(liveOllamaTotalBytes, runnerDelta)
        } else {
            effectiveOllamaBytes = max(liveOllamaTotalBytes, kernelRSS)
        }

        // Accurate KV cache accounting (no arbitrary / 4 cap!)
        let actualKVBytes: Int64 = effectiveOllamaBytes > 0
            ? min(estimatedKVCacheBytes, max(0, effectiveOllamaBytes - 134_217_728))
            : estimatedKVCacheBytes
        let actualWeightsBytes = effectiveOllamaBytes > 0
            ? max(0, effectiveOllamaBytes - actualKVBytes)
            : 0
        let otherSystemUsedBytes = max(0, trueSystemUsedBytes - effectiveOllamaBytes)
        let totalUsed = min(totalRAM, trueSystemUsedBytes)

        var history = previousHistory
        history.append(Double(totalUsed) / 1_073_741_824.0)
        if history.count > 30 { history.removeFirst() }

        return UnifiedMemorySnapshot(
            totalPhysicalRAMBytes: totalRAM,
            totalSystemUsedBytes: totalUsed,
            systemAndOSWiredBytes: otherSystemUsedBytes,
            modelWeightsResidentBytes: actualWeightsBytes,
            kvCacheAllocatedBytes: actualKVBytes,
            metalRecommendedMaxBytes: cachedMetalRecommendedMaxBytes,
            baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad: baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad,
            targetModelSizeBytes: targetModelSizeBytes,
            historySamplesGB: history
        )
    }
}
EOF
)
