    peakLoadedBytesDuringCurrentLoad = 0
    baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad = memorySnapshot.totalSystemUsedBytes
    targetLoadingModelBytes = max(1, targetModel.sizeBytes)

    Task.detached(priority: .utility) { [weak self] in
        let rss = HardwareMemoryEngine.queryOllamaProcessResidentBytes()
        await MainActor.run { self?.baselineOllamaRSSBeforeLoad = rss }
    }

    withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.20)) {
        isHoldingLoadCompletionState = false
        isLoadingModelWeights = true
        loadingModelName = targetModel.shortDisplayName
        loadingModelInternalID = targetModel.name
    }
    if let mIdx = availableModels.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == targetModel.name }) {
        availableModels[mIdx].residency = .loadingIntoMemory
    }

    guard let url = URL(string: "\(supervisor.normalizedEndpoint)/api/chat") else {
        isLoadingModelWeights = false
        return
    }

    var req = URLRequest(url: url)
    req.httpMethod = "POST"
    req.setValue("application/json", forHTTPHeaderField: "Content-Type")
    req.timeoutInterval = 300.0
    let payload: [String: Any] = [
        "model": targetModel.name,
        "messages": [],
        "keep_alive": "\(sessionParams.keepAliveMinutes)m",
        "options": sessionParams.loadOptionsDictionary()
    ]
    req.httpBody = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: payload)

    var loadSucceeded = false
    do {
        let (_, response) = try await OllamaNetworking.ephemeralSession.data(for: req)
        guard !Task.isCancelled else { return }
        if let http = response as? HTTPURLResponse, http.statusCode == 200 {
            loadSucceeded = true
            modelsLoadedByMotif.insert(targetModel.name)
            await pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
            recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
            NSHapticFeedbackManager.defaultPerformer.perform(.levelChange, performanceTime: .default)
        } else {
            humanReadableErrorBanner = "Could not load “\(targetModel.shortDisplayName)”. Check Ollama logs in Settings."
        }
    } catch {
        if !Task.isCancelled {
            humanReadableErrorBanner = "Failed to load “\(targetModel.shortDisplayName)”: \(error.localizedDescription)"
        }
    }

    guard !Task.isCancelled else { return }

    if loadSucceeded {
        let refreshedModel = availableModels.first(where: { $0.name == targetModel.name }) ?? targetModel
        withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.14)) {
            isHoldingLoadCompletionState = true
        }
        triggerModelLoadedCelebration(model: refreshedModel, ctx: sessionParams.numCtx)
        if !isAppActive {
            NSApp.requestUserAttention(.informationalRequest)
        } else {
            requestComposerFocus()
        }
        Task { @MainActor [weak self] in
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 550_000_000)
            guard let self = self else { return }
            withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.22)) {
                self.isHoldingLoadCompletionState = false
                self.isLoadingModelWeights = false
                self.loadingModelName = ""
                self.loadingModelInternalID = ""
                self.isConfirmingModelLoadCancel = false
            }
            self.peakLoadedBytesDuringCurrentLoad = 0
        }
    } else {
        if let mIdx = availableModels.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == targetModel.name }),
           availableModels[mIdx].residency == .loadingIntoMemory {
            availableModels[mIdx].residency = .dormantOnDisk
        }
        withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.20)) {
            isHoldingLoadCompletionState = false
            isLoadingModelWeights = false
            loadingModelName = ""
            loadingModelInternalID = ""
            isConfirmingModelLoadCancel = false
        }
        peakLoadedBytesDuringCurrentLoad = 0
    }
}

    public func unloadModelFromMemory(_ model: LocalModelDescriptor) async {
        if let mIdx = availableModels.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == model.name }) {
            availableModels[mIdx].residency = .dormantOnDisk
            availableModels[mIdx].vramAllocatedBytes = 0
            availableModels[mIdx].totalLoadedSizeBytes = 0
            availableModels[mIdx].residencyExpiresAt = nil
            availableModels[mIdx].loadedContextLength = nil
        }
        await unloadModelByName(model.name)
        await pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
        recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
        baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad = memorySnapshot.totalSystemUsedBytes
    }

    private func unloadModelByName(_ modelName: String) async {
        modelsLoadedByMotif.remove(modelName)
        guard let url = URL(string: "\(supervisor.normalizedEndpoint)/api/chat") else { return }
        var req = URLRequest(url: url)
        req.httpMethod = "POST"
        req.setValue("application/json", forHTTPHeaderField: "Content-Type")
        req.timeoutInterval = 20.0
        req.httpBody = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: ["model": modelName, "messages": [], "keep_alive": 0])
        _ = try? await OllamaNetworking.ephemeralSession.data(for: req)
    }

    public func triggerModelLoadCompletionFlicker() {
        Task { @MainActor in
            withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.09)) { self.modelLoadCompletionRimFlashOpacity = 1.0 }
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 110_000_000)
            withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.08)) { self.modelLoadCompletionRimFlashOpacity = 0.22 }
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 95_000_000)
            withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.11)) { self.modelLoadCompletionRimFlashOpacity = 1.0 }
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 220_000_000)
            withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.32)) { self.modelLoadCompletionRimFlashOpacity = 0.0 }
        }
    }

    /// Peripheral-vision "Model Ready" choreography: emerald Horizon Sweep + Composer Halo + 1.9s Ready Pill.
    public func triggerModelLoadedCelebration(model: LocalModelDescriptor, ctx: Int) {
        triggerModelLoadCompletionFlicker()
        let gpuText: String
        if let frac = model.gpuOffloadFraction {
            let pct = Int(round(frac * 100.0))
            gpuText = pct >= 98 ? "100% GPU" : "\(pct)% GPU"
        } else {
            gpuText = "In RAM"
        }
        let loadedCtx = model.loadedContextLength ?? ctx
        let banner = "\(model.shortDisplayName) ready · \(gpuText) · \(loadedCtx / 1024)k ctx"
        let myPulse = UUID()
        modelReadySweepID = myPulse

        Task { @MainActor in
            withAnimation(.spring(response: 0.32, dampingFraction: 0.82)) {
                self.modelReadyBannerText = banner
                self.composerHaloOpacity = 1.0
            }
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 1_100_000_000)
            withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.45)) {
                self.composerHaloOpacity = 0.0
            }
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 850_000_000)
            if self.modelReadySweepID == myPulse {
                withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.30)) {
                    self.modelReadyBannerText = nil
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public func requestComposerFocus() {
        composerFocusTrigger &+= 1
    }

    /// Direct ⌘V screenshot / clipboard image attachment.
    public func handleClipboardImagePaste(_ imageData: Data) {
        let modelSupportsVision = activeModelDescriptor?.supportsVision ?? false
        if !modelSupportsVision {
            humanReadableErrorBanner = "“\(activeModelDescriptor?.shortDisplayName ?? "Current model")” did not advertise vision capability — image attached anyway, or switch to a vision model."
        }
        if let processed = vault.downscaleAndPersistVisionImage(data: imageData) {
            let stamp = DateFormatter.localizedString(from: Date(), dateStyle: .none, timeStyle: .medium)
            pendingAttachments.append(MessageAttachment(
                title: "Pasted Image (\(stamp))",
                kind: .downscaledVisionImage,
                byteCount: processed.byteCount,
                lineCount: 1,
                previewText: "Clipboard image",
                localAssetFilename: processed.filename,
                base64ImagePayload: processed.base64JPEG
            ))
            saveCurrentDraftToActiveSession()
        }
    }

    /// Next safe context tier that fits within both the model's max context and available Unified Memory.
    public func nextHigherContextTier(for session: ConversationSession) -> Int? {
        guard let model = activeModelDescriptor else { return nil }
        let tiers = [4096, 8192, 16384, 32768, 65536, 131072]
        guard let next = tiers.first(where: { $0 > session.parameters.numCtx && $0 <= model.maxContextLength }) else {
            return nil
        }
        let nextKV = HardwareMemoryEngine.estimateKVCacheBytes(
            numLayers: model.numLayers,
            numKVHeads: model.numKVHeads,
            headDim: model.headDim,
            numCtx: next,
            quant: session.parameters.kvCacheQuant,
            numParallel: max(1, supervisor.parallelRequestSlots)
        )
        let extraNeeded = max(0, nextKV - estimatedKVCacheBytes)
        return extraNeeded < (memorySnapshot.freeHeadroomBytes - 536_870_912) ? next : nil
    }

    public func stepUpContextTier() {
        guard let sIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }),
              let nextTier = nextHigherContextTier(for: sessions[sIdx]) else { return }
        sessions[sIdx].parameters.numCtx = nextTier
        isContextCeilingBannerDismissed = false
        recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
        recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
    }

    public func copyLatestAssistantReply() {
        guard let latest = activeSession?.activeBranchThread.last(where: { $0.role == .assistant }) else { return }
        let text = latest.content.isEmpty ? latest.thinkingTrace : latest.content
        guard !text.isEmpty else { return }
        NSPasteboard.general.clearContents()
        NSPasteboard.general.setString(text, forType: .string)
    }

    // MARK: - Drafts, Dedicated Large-Paste Handler (Never Wipes Draft!) & Vision Guard
    private func saveCurrentDraftToActiveSession() {
        guard let id = selectedSessionID,
              let idx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
        sessions[idx].draftText = composerDraft
        sessions[idx].draftAttachments = pendingAttachments
    }

    private func restoreDraftFromSelectedSession() {
        guard let id = selectedSessionID,
              let session = sessions.first(where: { $0.id == id }) else {
            composerDraft = ""
            pendingAttachments = []
            return
        }
        composerDraft = session.draftText
        pendingAttachments = session.draftAttachments
    }

    public func handleComposerTextChange(_ newText: String) {
        if !newText.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).hasPrefix("/") {
            isSlashMenuDismissedForCurrentToken = false
            slashMenuHighlightedIndex = 0
        }
        composerDraft = newText
        saveCurrentDraftToActiveSession()
    }

    public func handleLargePasteAsChip(_ pastedText: String) {
        let lines = pastedText.components(separatedBy: .newlines)
        let preview = lines.prefix(6).joined(separator: "\n")
        let assetFile = vault.persistHeavyAsset(data: Data(pastedText.utf8), preferredExtension: "txt")
        let chip = MessageAttachment(
            title: "Pasted text (\(lines.count) lines)",
            kind: .pastedDocumentChip,
            byteCount: pastedText.utf8.count,
            lineCount: lines.count,
            previewText: preview,
            localAssetFilename: assetFile
        )
        pendingAttachments.append(chip)
        saveCurrentDraftToActiveSession()
    }

    public func attachFilesFromURLs(_ urls: [URL]) {
        let modelSupportsVision = activeModelDescriptor?.supportsVision ?? false
        for url in urls {
            guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url) else { continue }
            let ext = url.pathExtension.lowercased()
            let isImage = ["png", "jpg", "jpeg", "webp", "heic", "tiff", "bmp"].contains(ext)
            if isImage {
                if !modelSupportsVision {
                    humanReadableErrorBanner = "“\(activeModelDescriptor?.shortDisplayName ?? "Current model")” did not advertise vision capability — image attached anyway, or switch to a vision model."
                }
                if let processed = vault.downscaleAndPersistVisionImage(data: data) {
                    pendingAttachments.append(MessageAttachment(
                        title: url.lastPathComponent,
                        kind: .downscaledVisionImage,
                        byteCount: processed.byteCount,
                        lineCount: 1,
                        previewText: url.lastPathComponent,
                        localAssetFilename: processed.filename,
                        base64ImagePayload: processed.base64JPEG
                    ))
                }
            } else if let fullText = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) ?? String(data: data, encoding: .isoLatin1) {
                let lines = fullText.components(separatedBy: .newlines)
                let preview = lines.prefix(8).joined(separator: "\n")
                let savedAsset = vault.persistHeavyAsset(data: Data(fullText.utf8), preferredExtension: ext.isEmpty ? "txt" : ext)
                pendingAttachments.append(MessageAttachment(
                    title: url.lastPathComponent,
                    kind: .sourceCodeFile,
                    byteCount: fullText.utf8.count,
                    lineCount: lines.count,
                    previewText: preview,
                    localAssetFilename: savedAsset
                ))
            }
        }
        saveCurrentDraftToActiveSession()
    }

    // MARK: - App Nap Hold & Generation-ID-Safe Inter-Token Watchdog
    private func beginStreamingActivityHold(generationID: UUID) {
        if appNapActivity == nil {
            appNapActivity = ProcessInfo.processInfo.beginActivity(
                options: .userInitiatedAllowingIdleSystemSleep,
                reason: "Motif is streaming a response from local Ollama"
            )
        }
        lastStreamActivityTime = Date()
        streamWatchdogTask?.cancel()
        streamWatchdogTask = Task { [weak self] in
            while !Task.isCancelled {
                try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 5_000_000_000)
                guard let self = self, !Task.isCancelled else { break }
                guard self.activeGenerationID == generationID, self.isStreamingGeneration else { break }
                if Date().timeIntervalSince(self.lastStreamActivityTime) > 180.0 {
                    let alive = await self.supervisor.probeVersion()
                    if !alive && self.activeGenerationID == generationID {
                        self.streamingTask?.cancel()
                        break
                    }
                    self.lastStreamActivityTime = Date()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private func endStreamingActivityHold() {
        if let activity = appNapActivity {
            ProcessInfo.processInfo.endActivity(activity)
            appNapActivity = nil
        }
        streamWatchdogTask?.cancel()
        streamWatchdogTask = nil
    }

    // MARK: - Send, Edit, Delete (With Context Meter Invalidation & Role Safety)
    public var sendDisabledReason: String? {
        if activeSession == nil { return "Start a new chat first." }
        if availableModels.isEmpty { return "No local Ollama models detected. Open Settings to pull a model." }
        let trimmed = composerDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        if trimmed.isEmpty && pendingAttachments.isEmpty { return "Type a message to send." }
        return nil
    }

    public func sendActivePrompt(customPrompt: String? = nil) {
        guard let sessionIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }) else { return }
        if isStreamingGeneration { stopActiveGeneration() }

        let rawText = (customPrompt ?? composerDraft).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !rawText.isEmpty || !pendingAttachments.isEmpty else { return }
        guard let activeModel = activeModelDescriptor else {
            humanReadableErrorBanner = "No local model selected. Choose or pull a model first."
            return
        }

        if sessions[sessionIdx].selectedModelName.isEmpty {
            sessions[sessionIdx].selectedModelName = activeModel.name
        }
        perModelSavedParameters[activeModel.name] = sessions[sessionIdx].parameters

        humanReadableErrorBanner = nil
        isScrolledAwayFromBottom = false
        isAutoScrollPaused = false
        isComposerCanvasExpanded = false

        let targetSessionID = sessions[sessionIdx].id
        let lastNodeID = sessions[sessionIdx].activeBranchThread.last?.id

        // Keep user prose clean & separate from attachments (prevents double-counted tokens & broken code fences!)
        let userNode = ConversationMessageNode(
            parentID: lastNodeID,
            role: .user,
            content: rawText,
            modelUsed: activeModel.name,
            modelDigest: activeModel.digest,
            numCtxUsed: sessions[sessionIdx].parameters.numCtx,
            attachments: pendingAttachments
        )

        appendNodeToTree(userNode, inSessionAt: sessionIdx)
        if customPrompt == nil {
            composerDraft = ""
            pendingAttachments.removeAll()
            sessions[sessionIdx].draftText = ""
            sessions[sessionIdx].draftAttachments = []
        }
        if sessions[sessionIdx].title == "New Chat" && !rawText.isEmpty {
            sessions[sessionIdx].title = ConversationSession.sanitizeTitle(rawText)
        }

        streamAssistantResponse(
            parentUserNodeID: userNode.id,
            inSessionID: targetSessionID,
            continuingAssistantNodeID: nil,
            overrideModelName: nil
        )
    }

    private func invalidatePromptMetrics(inSessionAt sIdx: Int) {
        for key in sessions[sIdx].nodes.keys {
            if var node = sessions[sIdx].nodes[key], var m = node.metrics {
                m.promptEvalTokens = 0
                node.metrics = m
                sessions[sIdx].nodes[key] = node
            }
        }
    }

    public func updateAssistantMessageInPlace(nodeID: UUID, updatedText: String, clearThinking: Bool) {
        guard let sIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }),
              var node = sessions[sIdx].nodes[nodeID],
              node.role == .assistant else { return }
        node.content = updatedText.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        if clearThinking { node.thinkingTrace = "" }
        if var m = node.metrics {
            m.totalTokens = max(1, node.content.count / 4)
            if clearThinking { m.thinkingTokens = 0 }
            node.metrics = m
        }
        sessions[sIdx].nodes[nodeID] = node
        invalidatePromptMetrics(inSessionAt: sIdx)
        sessions[sIdx].updatedAt = Date()
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func deleteSingleMessageNode(_ nodeID: UUID) {
        guard let sIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }),
              let targetNode = sessions[sIdx].nodes[nodeID] else { return }
        if streamingNodeID == nodeID { stopActiveGeneration() }

        let parentID = targetNode.parentID
        let childrenIDs = targetNode.childrenIDs

        for childID in childrenIDs {
            if var child = sessions[sIdx].nodes[childID] {
                child.parentID = parentID
                sessions[sIdx].nodes[childID] = child
            }
        }

        if let pID = parentID, var parent = sessions[sIdx].nodes[pID] {
            if let idxInParent = parent.childrenIDs.firstIndex(of: nodeID) {
                parent.childrenIDs.remove(at: idxInParent)
                parent.childrenIDs.insert(contentsOf: childrenIDs, at: min(idxInParent, parent.childrenIDs.count))
                parent.activeChildIndex = min(max(0, parent.activeChildIndex), max(0, parent.childrenIDs.count - 1))
            }
            sessions[sIdx].nodes[pID] = parent
        } else {
            if let idxInRoot = sessions[sIdx].rootMessageIDs.firstIndex(of: nodeID) {
                sessions[sIdx].rootMessageIDs.remove(at: idxInRoot)
                sessions[sIdx].rootMessageIDs.insert(contentsOf: childrenIDs, at: min(idxInRoot, sessions[sIdx].rootMessageIDs.count))
                sessions[sIdx].activeRootIndex = min(max(0, sessions[sIdx].activeRootIndex), max(0, sessions[sIdx].rootMessageIDs.count - 1))
            }
        }

        sessions[sIdx].nodes.removeValue(forKey: nodeID)
        invalidatePromptMetrics(inSessionAt: sIdx)
        sessions[sIdx].updatedAt = Date()
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func trimOldestTurnInActiveSession() {
        guard let session = activeSession else { return }
        let thread = session.activeBranchThread
        guard let oldest = thread.first else { return }
        deleteSingleMessageNode(oldest.id)
        if thread.count >= 2 && thread[1].role == .assistant {
            deleteSingleMessageNode(thread[1].id)
        }
        kernelMemoryPressureWarning = nil
    }

    public func continueStoppedGeneration(forAssistantNodeID nodeID: UUID) {
        guard !isStreamingGeneration,
              let sessionIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }),
              let existingNode = sessions[sessionIdx].nodes[nodeID],
              existingNode.role == .assistant,
              let parentUserID = existingNode.parentID else { return }
        isScrolledAwayFromBottom = false
        isAutoScrollPaused = false
        streamAssistantResponse(
            parentUserNodeID: parentUserID,
            inSessionID: sessions[sessionIdx].id,
            continuingAssistantNodeID: nodeID,
            overrideModelName: existingNode.modelUsed.isEmpty ? nil : existingNode.modelUsed
        )
    }

    public func createEditedMessageBranch(originalNodeID: UUID, updatedText: String) {
        let trimmed = updatedText.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !trimmed.isEmpty,
              let sessionIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }),
              let originalNode = sessions[sessionIdx].nodes[originalNodeID] else { return }
        if isStreamingGeneration { stopActiveGeneration() }
        isScrolledAwayFromBottom = false
        isAutoScrollPaused = false

        let branchedUserNode = ConversationMessageNode(
            parentID: originalNode.parentID,
            role: .user,
            content: trimmed,
            modelUsed: sessions[sessionIdx].selectedModelName,
            attachments: originalNode.attachments
        )
        appendNodeToTree(branchedUserNode, inSessionAt: sessionIdx)
        streamAssistantResponse(
            parentUserNodeID: branchedUserNode.id,
            inSessionID: sessions[sessionIdx].id,
            continuingAssistantNodeID: nil,
            overrideModelName: nil
        )
    }

    public func regenerateAssistantBranch(forAssistantNodeID nodeID: UUID, withOverrideModel modelName: String? = nil) {
        guard let sessionIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }),
              let assistantNode = sessions[sessionIdx].nodes[nodeID],
              let parentUserID = assistantNode.parentID else { return }
        if isStreamingGeneration { stopActiveGeneration() }
        if let newModel = modelName { sessions[sessionIdx].selectedModelName = newModel }
        isScrolledAwayFromBottom = false
        isAutoScrollPaused = false
        streamAssistantResponse(
            parentUserNodeID: parentUserID,
            inSessionID: sessions[sessionIdx].id,
            continuingAssistantNodeID: nil,
            overrideModelName: modelName
        )
    }

    public func regenerateOrAnswerUserNode(nodeID: UUID) {
        guard let sIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }) else { return }
        streamAssistantResponse(
            parentUserNodeID: nodeID,
            inSessionID: sessions[sIdx].id,
            continuingAssistantNodeID: nil,
            overrideModelName: nil
        )
    }

    public func branchMessageToNewChat(upToNodeID nodeID: UUID) {
        guard let currentSession = activeSession else { return }
        let thread = currentSession.activeBranchThread
        guard let cutoffIndex = thread.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == nodeID }) else { return }
        let prefixNodes = Array(thread[0...cutoffIndex])
        guard !prefixNodes.isEmpty else { return }

        var cleanBase = ConversationSession.sanitizeTitle(currentSession.title)
        if cleanBase.lowercased().hasPrefix("branch · ") { cleanBase = String(cleanBase.dropFirst("Branch · ".count)) }
        let branchTitle = ConversationSession.sanitizeTitle("Branch · \(cleanBase)")

        var forkedSession = ConversationSession(
            title: branchTitle,
            folderID: currentSession.folderID,
            selectedModelName: currentSession.selectedModelName,
            activePersonaTrigger: currentSession.activePersonaTrigger,
            systemPrompt: currentSession.systemPrompt,
            parameters: currentSession.parameters
        )

        var previousNewID: UUID? = nil
        for oldNode in prefixNodes {
            var cloned = oldNode
            cloned.id = UUID()
            cloned.parentID = previousNewID
            cloned.childrenIDs = []
            cloned.activeChildIndex = 0
            forkedSession.nodes[cloned.id] = cloned
            if let prevID = previousNewID, var parentNode = forkedSession.nodes[prevID] {
                parentNode.childrenIDs = [cloned.id]
                parentNode.activeChildIndex = 0
                forkedSession.nodes[prevID] = parentNode
            } else {
                forkedSession.rootMessageIDs = [cloned.id]
                forkedSession.activeRootIndex = 0
            }
            previousNewID = cloned.id
        }

        sessions.insert(forkedSession, at: 0)
        selectedSessionID = forkedSession.id
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func summarizeContextIntoNewBranch() {
        guard let sessionIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }) else { return }
        let currentThread = sessions[sessionIdx].activeBranchThread.filter { !$0.content.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty }
        guard !currentThread.isEmpty else { return }
        if isStreamingGeneration { stopActiveGeneration() }

        let turnCount = currentThread.count
        let transcript = currentThread
            .map { "\($0.role == .user ? "USER" : "ASSISTANT"): \($0.content)" }
            .joined(separator: "\n\n")
        let hiddenSummaryDirective = "Summarize the following conversation transcript (\(turnCount) turns) into a structured, high-density context handoff. Preserve all key decisions, technical constraints, code architecture, and active goals so we can continue seamlessly from this summary:\n\n\(transcript.suffix(16000))"

        let newRootUserNode = ConversationMessageNode(
            parentID: nil,
            role: .user,
            content: "Context Summary Handoff — Compressed \(turnCount) \(turnCount == 1 ? "turn" : "turns") into a fresh branch.",
            apiPromptOverride: hiddenSummaryDirective,
            isSummaryHandoffRequest: true,
            modelUsed: sessions[sessionIdx].selectedModelName
        )
        appendNodeToTree(newRootUserNode, inSessionAt: sessionIdx)
        isContextCeilingBannerDismissed = false
        kernelMemoryPressureWarning = nil

        streamAssistantResponse(
            parentUserNodeID: newRootUserNode.id,
            inSessionID: sessions[sessionIdx].id,
            continuingAssistantNodeID: nil,
            overrideModelName: nil
        )
    }

    public func exportSessionAsMarkdown(_ sessionID: UUID) {
        guard let session = sessions.first(where: { $0.id == sessionID }) else { return }
        var md = "# \(session.title)\n\n- **Model:** `\(LocalModelDescriptor.shortenModelName(session.selectedModelName))`\n\n---\n\n"
        for node in session.activeBranchThread {
            let roleHeader = node.role == .user ? "### You" : "### \(LocalModelDescriptor.shortenModelName(node.modelUsed.isEmpty ? "Assistant" : node.modelUsed))"
            md += "\(roleHeader)\n\n"
            if !node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty {
                md += "<details>\n<summary>Thinking Process</summary>\n\n\(node.thinkingTrace)\n\n</details>\n\n"
            }
            md += "\(node.content)\n\n---\n\n"
        }
        let savePanel = NSSavePanel()
        savePanel.title = "Export Chat as Markdown"
        savePanel.nameFieldStringValue = "\(session.title.replacingOccurrences(of: " ", with: "_")).md"
        savePanel.allowedContentTypes = [.plainText]
        if savePanel.runModal() == .OK, let url = savePanel.url {
            try? md.write(to: url, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
        }
    }

    public func switchBranchVersion(forNodeID nodeID: UUID, delta: Int) {
        guard let sessionIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }),
              let node = sessions[sessionIdx].nodes[nodeID] else { return }
        if let parentID = node.parentID, var parent = sessions[sessionIdx].nodes[parentID] {
            parent.activeChildIndex = min(max(0, parent.activeChildIndex + delta), parent.childrenIDs.count - 1)
            sessions[sessionIdx].nodes[parentID] = parent
        } else {
            sessions[sessionIdx].activeRootIndex = min(max(0, sessions[sessionIdx].activeRootIndex + delta), sessions[sessionIdx].rootMessageIDs.count - 1)
        }
        saveAllState()
    }

    private func appendNodeToTree(_ node: ConversationMessageNode, inSessionAt sessionIdx: Int) {
        sessions[sessionIdx].nodes[node.id] = node
        if let parentID = node.parentID, var parent = sessions[sessionIdx].nodes[parentID] {
            parent.childrenIDs.append(node.id)
            parent.activeChildIndex = parent.childrenIDs.count - 1
            sessions[sessionIdx].nodes[parentID] = parent
        } else {
            sessions[sessionIdx].rootMessageIDs.append(node.id)
            sessions[sessionIdx].activeRootIndex = sessions[sessionIdx].rootMessageIDs.count - 1
        }
        sessions[sessionIdx].updatedAt = Date()
    }

    // MARK: - Race-Free, Prefix-Cache-Safe Streaming Engine
    private func streamAssistantResponse(
        parentUserNodeID: UUID,
        inSessionID targetSessionID: UUID,
        continuingAssistantNodeID: UUID?,
        overrideModelName: String?
    ) {
        streamingTask?.cancel()
        guard let initialIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == targetSessionID }) else { return }

        let resolvedModel: LocalModelDescriptor?
        if let override = overrideModelName,
           let matched = availableModels.first(where: { $0.name == override || $0.shortDisplayName == override }) {
            resolvedModel = matched
        } else {
            resolvedModel = activeModelDescriptor
        }
        guard let model = resolvedModel else { return }

        let activeThread = sessions[initialIdx].activeBranchThread
        guard let parentIndex = activeThread.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == parentUserNodeID }) else { return }
        let historyUpToParent = Array(activeThread.prefix(through: parentIndex))

        let sessionParams = sessions[initialIdx].parameters
        let isSummaryTurn = sessions[initialIdx].nodes[parentUserNodeID]?.isSummaryHandoffRequest == true
        let modelSupportsThinking = model.thinkingProfile.kind != .unsupported || sessionParams.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported
        let shouldThink = isSummaryTurn
            ? (modelSupportsThinking && supervisor.useThinkingForSummary)
            : (modelSupportsThinking && sessionParams.thinkingMode.isThinkingEnabled)

        let assistantID: UUID
        let initialThinking: String
        let initialAnswer: String
        let resumeInThinking: Bool
        let priorThinkSec: Double
        let priorAnswerTokens: Int
        let priorThinkingTokens: Int

        if let contID = continuingAssistantNodeID, var existing = sessions[initialIdx].nodes[contID] {
            resumeInThinking = existing.wasStoppedMidThinking && existing.content.isEmpty
            priorThinkSec = existing.metrics?.thinkingDurationSec ?? 0.0
            priorAnswerTokens = existing.metrics?.totalTokens ?? 0
            priorThinkingTokens = existing.metrics?.thinkingTokens ?? 0
            existing.wasStoppedMidStream = false
            existing.wasStoppedMidThinking = false
            existing.errorNote = nil
            if resumeInThinking {
                existing.isThinkingComplete = false
                existing.isThinkingExpanded = true
            }
            sessions[initialIdx].nodes[contID] = existing
            assistantID = contID
            initialThinking = existing.thinkingTrace
            initialAnswer = existing.content
        } else {
            resumeInThinking = false
            priorThinkSec = 0.0
            priorAnswerTokens = 0
            priorThinkingTokens = 0
            let placeholderAssistant = ConversationMessageNode(
                parentID: parentUserNodeID,
                role: .assistant,
                content: "",
                thinkingTrace: "",
                isThinkingComplete: !shouldThink,
                isThinkingExpanded: true,
                modelUsed: model.shortDisplayName,
                modelDigest: model.digest,
                numCtxUsed: sessionParams.numCtx
            )
            appendNodeToTree(placeholderAssistant, inSessionAt: initialIdx)
            assistantID = placeholderAssistant.id
            initialThinking = ""
            initialAnswer = ""
        }

        let parser = StatefulThinkingStreamParser()
        parser.preloadExisting(
            thinking: initialThinking,
            answer: initialAnswer,
            resumeInsideThinking: resumeInThinking,
            priorThinkingDuration: priorThinkSec
        )
        self.activeStreamParser = parser

        let myGenerationID = UUID()
        self.activeGenerationID = myGenerationID
        self.isStreamingGeneration = true
        self.streamingSessionID = targetSessionID
        self.streamingNodeID = assistantID
        self.liveTokensPerSec = 0.0
        self.liveTotalTokens = priorAnswerTokens
        self.liveThinkingTokens = priorThinkingTokens
        self.liveElapsedSeconds = priorThinkSec
        beginStreamingActivityHold(generationID: myGenerationID)

        let neededInitialLoad = (model.residency != .activeInUnifiedRAM)
            || (model.loadedContextLength != nil && model.loadedContextLength != sessionParams.numCtx)
        let endpoint = supervisor.normalizedEndpoint
        let systemPromptCopy = sessions[initialIdx].systemPrompt

        streamingTask = Task { [weak self] in
            guard let self = self else { return }
            defer {
                if self.activeGenerationID == myGenerationID {
                    self.endStreamingActivityHold()
                }
            }

            if neededInitialLoad {
    self.peakLoadedBytesDuringCurrentLoad = 0
    self.baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad = self.memorySnapshot.totalSystemUsedBytes
    self.targetLoadingModelBytes = max(1, model.sizeBytes)
    Task.detached(priority: .utility) { [weak self] in
        let rss = HardwareMemoryEngine.queryOllamaProcessResidentBytes()
        await MainActor.run { self?.baselineOllamaRSSBeforeLoad = rss }
    }
    self.isHoldingLoadCompletionState = false
    self.isLoadingModelWeights = true
    self.loadingModelName = model.shortDisplayName
    self.loadingModelInternalID = model.name
    if let mIdx = self.availableModels.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == model.name }) {
        self.availableModels[mIdx].residency = .loadingIntoMemory
    }
}

            if historyUpToParent.count > 1 {
                self.isEvaluatingConversationContext = true
            }

            guard let url = URL(string: "\(endpoint)/api/chat") else {
                if self.activeGenerationID == myGenerationID {
                    self.finishStreamingCleanup(generationID: myGenerationID)
                }
                return
            }

            var request = URLRequest(url: url)
            request.httpMethod = "POST"
            request.setValue("application/json", forHTTPHeaderField: "Content-Type")
            request.timeoutInterval = 600.0

            // Build role-safe, attachment-expanded API messages (never sends old thinking traces)
            var apiMessages: [[String: Any]] = []
            if !systemPromptCopy.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty {
                apiMessages.append(["role": "system", "content": systemPromptCopy])
            }

            var lastAppendedRole: MessageRole? = nil
            for (idx, turn) in historyUpToParent.enumerated() {
                if turn.role == .user && turn.id != parentUserNodeID {
                    let hasAssistantNext = (idx + 1 < historyUpToParent.count) && (historyUpToParent[idx + 1].role == .assistant)
                    if !hasAssistantNext { continue }
                }
                // Guard against leading assistant turn after manual deletions
                if lastAppendedRole == nil && turn.role == .assistant { continue }

                var composedText = turn.apiPromptOverride ?? turn.content
                for att in turn.attachments where att.kind != .downscaledVisionImage {
                    let fileBody: String
                    if let fname = att.localAssetFilename, let loaded = self.vault.loadHeavyAssetText(filename: fname) {
                        fileBody = loaded
                    } else {
                        fileBody = att.previewText
                    }
                    if !fileBody.isEmpty {
                        let fence = fileBody.contains("```") ? "~~~~" : "```"
                        composedText += "\n\n// File: \(att.title)\n\(fence)\n\(fileBody)\n\(fence)"
                    }
                }

                let imagesBase64: [String] = turn.attachments.compactMap { att in
                    if let memB64 = att.base64ImagePayload, !memB64.isEmpty { return memB64 }
                    if att.kind == .downscaledVisionImage, let fname = att.localAssetFilename {
                        return self.vault.loadHeavyAssetBase64(filename: fname)
                    }
                    return nil
                }

                // Coalesce consecutive same-role messages (prevents strict chat template 400 errors)
                if lastAppendedRole == turn.role, !apiMessages.isEmpty {
                    let prevText = (apiMessages[apiMessages.count - 1]["content"] as? String) ?? ""
                    apiMessages[apiMessages.count - 1]["content"] = prevText + "\n\n" + composedText
                    if !imagesBase64.isEmpty {
                        var existingImgs = (apiMessages[apiMessages.count - 1]["images"] as? [String]) ?? []
                        existingImgs.append(contentsOf: imagesBase64)
                        apiMessages[apiMessages.count - 1]["images"] = existingImgs
                    }
                } else {
                    var msgDict: [String: Any] = [
                        "role": turn.role.rawValue,
                        "content": composedText
                    ]
                    if !imagesBase64.isEmpty {
                        msgDict["images"] = imagesBase64
                    }
                    apiMessages.append(msgDict)
                    lastAppendedRole = turn.role
                }
            }

            if continuingAssistantNodeID != nil {
                if !initialAnswer.isEmpty {
                    apiMessages.append(["role": "assistant", "content": initialAnswer])
                    apiMessages.append([
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": "Continue directly from where you stopped without repeating prior text: \"\(initialAnswer.suffix(140))\""
                    ])
                } else if !initialThinking.isEmpty {
                    apiMessages.append([
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": "You stopped mid-reasoning:\n\"\(initialThinking.suffix(400))\"\nBriefly finish reasoning and output the complete final answer."
                    ])
                }
            }

            var payload: [String: Any] = [
                "model": model.name,
                "messages": apiMessages,
                "stream": true,
                "keep_alive": "\(sessionParams.keepAliveMinutes)m",
                "options": sessionParams.chatOptionsDictionary()
            ]

            if modelSupportsThinking {
                if isSummaryTurn && !self.supervisor.useThinkingForSummary {
                    payload["think"] = false
                } else if continuingAssistantNodeID != nil && !initialAnswer.isEmpty {
                    payload["think"] = false
                } else if !shouldThink {
                    payload["think"] = false
                } else {
                    payload["think"] = sessionParams.thinkingMode.ollamaPayloadValue
                }
            }

            if sessionParams.enforceJSONMode {
                let schemaTrimmed = sessionParams.jsonSchemaText.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
                if !schemaTrimmed.isEmpty,
                   let schemaData = schemaTrimmed.data(using: .utf8),
                   let schemaObj = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: schemaData) {
                    payload["format"] = schemaObj
                } else {
                    payload["format"] = "json"
                }
            }

            request.httpBody = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: payload)

            var firstTokenWall: Date? = nil
            var thinkingTokenCounter = 0
            var answerTokenCounter = 0
            var evalTokenCountFromEngine: Int? = nil
            var promptEvalCountFromEngine: Int? = nil
            var promptEvalCachedCountFromEngine: Int? = nil
            var loadDurationNs: Int64? = nil
            var promptEvalDurationNs: Int64? = nil
            var evalDurationNs: Int64? = nil
            var doneReasonFromEngine: String = ""

            @MainActor
            func drainStream(_ stream: URLSession.AsyncBytes) async throws {
                for try await line in stream.lines {
                    if Task.isCancelled { break }
                    if self.activeGenerationID != myGenerationID { break }
                    await self.processStreamLine(
                        line,
                        generationID: myGenerationID,
                        priorAnswerTokens: priorAnswerTokens,
                        priorThinkingTokens: priorThinkingTokens,
                        firstTokenWall: &firstTokenWall,
                        thinkingTokenCounter: &thinkingTokenCounter,
                        answerTokenCounter: &answerTokenCounter,
                        evalTokenCountFromEngine: &evalTokenCountFromEngine,
                        promptEvalCountFromEngine: &promptEvalCountFromEngine,
                        promptEvalCachedCountFromEngine: &promptEvalCachedCountFromEngine,
                        loadDurationNs: &loadDurationNs,
                        promptEvalDurationNs: &promptEvalDurationNs,
                        evalDurationNs: &evalDurationNs,
                        doneReasonFromEngine: &doneReasonFromEngine,
                        parser: parser,
                        assistantID: assistantID,
                        targetSessionID: targetSessionID
                    )
                }
            }

            do {
                let (bytes, response) = try await OllamaNetworking.ephemeralSession.bytes(for: request)
                guard let http = response as? HTTPURLResponse else { throw URLError(.badServerResponse) }

                if http.statusCode == 400 && payload["think"] != nil {
                    // Progressive degradation: level string -> bool true -> omit think
                    var retryPayload = payload
                    if payload["think"] is String {
                        retryPayload["think"] = true
                    } else {
                        retryPayload.removeValue(forKey: "think")
                    }
                    request.httpBody = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: retryPayload)
                    let (retryBytes, retryResp) = try await OllamaNetworking.ephemeralSession.bytes(for: request)
                    if let retryHttp = retryResp as? HTTPURLResponse, retryHttp.statusCode == 200 {
                        try await drainStream(retryBytes)
                    } else if payload["think"] is String, (retryResp as? HTTPURLResponse)?.statusCode == 400 {
                        var strippedPayload = payload
                        strippedPayload.removeValue(forKey: "think")
                        request.httpBody = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: strippedPayload)
                        let (finalBytes, finalResp) = try await OllamaNetworking.ephemeralSession.bytes(for: request)
                        if let finalHttp = finalResp as? HTTPURLResponse, finalHttp.statusCode == 200 {
                            try await drainStream(finalBytes)
                        } else if let finalHttp = finalResp as? HTTPURLResponse {
                            var errBody = ""
                            for try await l in finalBytes.lines { errBody += l }
                            self.recordStreamError(assistantID: assistantID, targetSessionID: targetSessionID, message: "Ollama HTTP \(finalHttp.statusCode): \(errBody)")
                        }
                    } else if let retryHttp = retryResp as? HTTPURLResponse {
                        var errBody = ""
                        for try await l in retryBytes.lines { errBody += l }
                        self.recordStreamError(assistantID: assistantID, targetSessionID: targetSessionID, message: "Ollama HTTP \(retryHttp.statusCode): \(errBody)")
                    }
                } else if http.statusCode != 200 {
                    var errBody = ""
                    for try await l in bytes.lines { errBody += l }
                    self.recordStreamError(assistantID: assistantID, targetSessionID: targetSessionID, message: "Ollama HTTP \(http.statusCode): \(errBody)")
                } else {
                    try await drainStream(bytes)
                }
            } catch {
                if !Task.isCancelled && self.activeGenerationID == myGenerationID {
                    self.recordStreamError(
                        assistantID: assistantID,
                        targetSessionID: targetSessionID,
                        message: "Connection to Ollama failed (\(error.localizedDescription)). Check that Ollama is running."
                    )
                    self.finishStreamingCleanup(generationID: myGenerationID)
                    return
                }
            }

            // Watchdog-triggered cancellation (only if WE are still the active generation!)
            if Task.isCancelled {
                if self.activeGenerationID == myGenerationID && self.isStreamingGeneration {
                    parser.flushPendingBufferWithoutLosingState(markComplete: true)
                    self.recordStreamError(
                        assistantID: assistantID,
                        targetSessionID: targetSessionID,
                        message: "Stream aborted — Ollama stopped responding. Partial output was preserved."
                    )
                    self.finishStreamingCleanup(generationID: myGenerationID)
                }
                return
            }

            guard self.activeGenerationID == myGenerationID else { return }
            parser.flushPendingBufferWithoutLosingState(markComplete: true)

            // Thought-only recovery: if a reasoning model finished thinking with zero final answer, nudge it once.
            let hasThoughtOnly = !parser.accumulatedThinking.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty
                && parser.accumulatedAnswer.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty
                && continuingAssistantNodeID == nil
            if hasThoughtOnly {
                var followMessages = apiMessages
                followMessages.append(["role": "assistant", "content": "<think>\n\(parser.accumulatedThinking)\n</think>"])
                followMessages.append(["role": "user", "content": "Now provide the complete final response based on your reasoning above."])
                var followPayload = payload
                followPayload["messages"] = followMessages
                followPayload["think"] = false
                request.httpBody = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: followPayload)
                if let (followBytes, followResp) = try? await OllamaNetworking.ephemeralSession.bytes(for: request),
                   let followHttp = followResp as? HTTPURLResponse, followHttp.statusCode == 200 {
                    try? await drainStream(followBytes)
                    parser.flushPendingBufferWithoutLosingState(markComplete: true)
                }
            }

            let genElapsed = max(0.05, Date().timeIntervalSince(firstTokenWall ?? Date()))
            var finalTPS = self.liveTokensPerSec
            if let ec = evalTokenCountFromEngine, let ed = evalDurationNs, ed > 0 {
                finalTPS = Double(ec) / (Double(ed) / 1_000_000_000.0)
            }

            let finalThinkingTok = priorThinkingTokens + max(
                thinkingTokenCounter,
                parser.accumulatedThinking.isEmpty ? 0 : max(1, parser.accumulatedThinking.count / 4)
            )
            let finalAnswerTok: Int
            if parser.accumulatedThinking.isEmpty, let ec = evalTokenCountFromEngine, ec > 0 {
                finalAnswerTok = priorAnswerTokens + ec
            } else if let ec = evalTokenCountFromEngine, ec > thinkingTokenCounter && thinkingTokenCounter > 0 {
                finalAnswerTok = priorAnswerTokens + max(1, ec - thinkingTokenCounter)
            } else {
                finalAnswerTok = priorAnswerTokens + max(answerTokenCounter, parser.accumulatedAnswer.isEmpty ? 0 : max(1, parser.accumulatedAnswer.count / 4))
            }

            self.updateStreamingNode(
                assistantID: assistantID,
                targetSessionID: targetSessionID,
                parser: parser,
                tps: finalTPS,
                elapsed: genElapsed,
                loadSec: Double(loadDurationNs ?? 0) / 1_000_000_000.0,
                promptEvalSec: Double(promptEvalDurationNs ?? 0) / 1_000_000_000.0,
                answerTokens: finalAnswerTok,
                thinkingTokens: finalThinkingTok,
                promptTokens: promptEvalCountFromEngine ?? 0,
                promptCachedTokens: promptEvalCachedCountFromEngine ?? 0,
                doneReason: doneReasonFromEngine,
                isFinal: true
            )
            self.modelsLoadedByMotif.insert(model.name)
            self.extractArtifactsFromCompletedMessage(assistantID: assistantID, targetSessionID: targetSessionID)
            self.finishStreamingCleanup(generationID: myGenerationID)
            await self.pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
            self.recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
        }
    }

    private func finishStreamingCleanup(generationID: UUID) {
        guard activeGenerationID == generationID else { return }
        activeGenerationID = nil
        isStreamingGeneration = false
        isLoadingModelWeights = false
        isHoldingLoadCompletionState = false
        isEvaluatingConversationContext = false
        loadingModelName = ""
        if !loadingModelInternalID.isEmpty,
           let mIdx = availableModels.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == loadingModelInternalID }),
           availableModels[mIdx].residency == .loadingIntoMemory {
            availableModels[mIdx].residency = .dormantOnDisk
        }
        loadingModelInternalID = ""
        streamingSessionID = nil
        streamingNodeID = nil
        activeStreamParser = nil
        endStreamingActivityHold()
        saveAllState()
    }

    private func processStreamLine(
        _ line: String,
        generationID: UUID,
        priorAnswerTokens: Int,
        priorThinkingTokens: Int,
        firstTokenWall: inout Date?,
        thinkingTokenCounter: inout Int,
        answerTokenCounter: inout Int,
        evalTokenCountFromEngine: inout Int?,
        promptEvalCountFromEngine: inout Int?,
        promptEvalCachedCountFromEngine: inout Int?,
        loadDurationNs: inout Int64?,
        promptEvalDurationNs: inout Int64?,
        evalDurationNs: inout Int64?,
        doneReasonFromEngine: inout String,
        parser: StatefulThinkingStreamParser,
        assistantID: UUID,
        targetSessionID: UUID
    ) async {
        guard activeGenerationID == generationID,
              let data = line.data(using: .utf8),
              let json = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any] else { return }

        lastStreamActivityTime = Date()
        if isEvaluatingConversationContext { isEvaluatingConversationContext = false }

        if isLoadingModelWeights {
            let loadedID = loadingModelInternalID
            isLoadingModelWeights = false
            isHoldingLoadCompletionState = false
            loadingModelName = ""
            loadingModelInternalID = ""
            peakLoadedBytesDuringCurrentLoad = 0
            if let mIdx = availableModels.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == loadedID }) {
                availableModels[mIdx].residency = .activeInUnifiedRAM
            }
            triggerModelLoadCompletionFlicker()
            Task { await self.pollOllamaRunningProcesses() }
        }

        if let errStr = json["error"] as? String {
            recordStreamError(assistantID: assistantID, targetSessionID: targetSessionID, message: errStr)
            return
        }

        if let msg = json["message"] as? [String: Any] {
            if let explicitThink = msg["thinking"] as? String, !explicitThink.isEmpty {
                if firstTokenWall == nil { firstTokenWall = Date() }
                parser.ingestExplicitThinkingDelta(explicitThink)
                thinkingTokenCounter += 1
            }
            if let contentChunk = msg["content"] as? String, !contentChunk.isEmpty {
                if firstTokenWall == nil { firstTokenWall = Date() }
                let prevAnsLen = parser.accumulatedAnswer.count
                parser.ingestContentChunk(contentChunk)
                if parser.accumulatedAnswer.count > prevAnsLen {
                    answerTokenCounter += 1
                } else {
                    thinkingTokenCounter += 1
                }
            }
        }

        if let pe = (json["prompt_eval_count"] as? NSNumber)?.intValue { promptEvalCountFromEngine = pe }
        if let pc = (json["prompt_eval_cached_count"] as? NSNumber)?.intValue { promptEvalCachedCountFromEngine = pc }
        if let ec = (json["eval_count"] as? NSNumber)?.intValue { evalTokenCountFromEngine = ec }
        if let ld = (json["load_duration"] as? NSNumber)?.int64Value { loadDurationNs = ld }
        if let ped = (json["prompt_eval_duration"] as? NSNumber)?.int64Value { promptEvalDurationNs = ped }
        if let ed = (json["eval_duration"] as? NSNumber)?.int64Value { evalDurationNs = ed }
        if let dr = json["done_reason"] as? String, !dr.isEmpty { doneReasonFromEngine = dr }

        let totalGenerated = thinkingTokenCounter + answerTokenCounter
        let isDoneChunk = (json["done"] as? Bool) == true
        let now = Date()
        guard isDoneChunk || totalGenerated <= 2 || now.timeIntervalSince(lastStreamUIUpdateTime) >= 0.036 else {
            return
        }
        lastStreamUIUpdateTime = now

        let elapsed = max(0.05, now.timeIntervalSince(firstTokenWall ?? now))
        var tps = totalGenerated > 0 ? Double(totalGenerated) / elapsed : 0.0
        if let ec = evalTokenCountFromEngine, let ed = evalDurationNs, ed > 0 {
            tps = Double(ec) / (Double(ed) / 1_000_000_000.0)
        }

        let currentThinkTok = priorThinkingTokens + max(thinkingTokenCounter, parser.accumulatedThinking.count / 4)
        let currentAnswerTok = priorAnswerTokens + max(answerTokenCounter, parser.accumulatedAnswer.count / 4)

        updateStreamingNode(
            assistantID: assistantID,
            targetSessionID: targetSessionID,
            parser: parser,
            tps: tps,
            elapsed: elapsed,
            loadSec: Double(loadDurationNs ?? 0) / 1_000_000_000.0,
            promptEvalSec: Double(promptEvalDurationNs ?? 0) / 1_000_000_000.0,
            answerTokens: currentAnswerTok,
            thinkingTokens: currentThinkTok,
            promptTokens: promptEvalCountFromEngine ?? 0,
            promptCachedTokens: promptEvalCachedCountFromEngine ?? 0,
            doneReason: doneReasonFromEngine,
            isFinal: false
        )
    }

    private func recordStreamError(assistantID: UUID, targetSessionID: UUID, message: String) {
        humanReadableErrorBanner = message
        guard let sIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == targetSessionID }),
              var node = sessions[sIdx].nodes[assistantID] else { return }
        node.isThinkingComplete = true
        node.errorNote = message
        sessions[sIdx].nodes[assistantID] = node
    }

    private func updateStreamingNode(
        assistantID: UUID,
        targetSessionID: UUID,
        parser: StatefulThinkingStreamParser,
        tps: Double,
        elapsed: Double,
        loadSec: Double,
        promptEvalSec: Double,
        answerTokens: Int,
        thinkingTokens: Int,
        promptTokens: Int,
        promptCachedTokens: Int,
        doneReason: String,
        isFinal: Bool
    ) {
        guard let sIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == targetSessionID }),
              var node = sessions[sIdx].nodes[assistantID] else { return }

        node.thinkingTrace = parser.accumulatedThinking
        node.content = parser.accumulatedAnswer
        node.isThinkingComplete = !parser.isCurrentlyThinking || isFinal

        liveTokensPerSec = tps
        liveTotalTokens = answerTokens
        liveThinkingTokens = thinkingTokens
        liveElapsedSeconds = elapsed

        let prev = node.metrics
        node.metrics = GenerationMetrics(
            tokensPerSecond: tps,
            totalGenerationDurationSec: elapsed,
            thinkingDurationSec: parser.thinkingDurationSeconds,
            loadDurationSec: loadSec > 0 ? loadSec : (prev?.loadDurationSec ?? 0),
            promptEvalDurationSec: promptEvalSec > 0 ? promptEvalSec : (prev?.promptEvalDurationSec ?? 0),
            totalTokens: answerTokens,
            thinkingTokens: thinkingTokens,
            promptEvalTokens: promptTokens > 0 ? promptTokens : (prev?.promptEvalTokens ?? 0),
            promptEvalCachedTokens: promptCachedTokens > 0 ? promptCachedTokens : (prev?.promptEvalCachedTokens ?? 0),
            doneReason: !doneReason.isEmpty ? doneReason : (prev?.doneReason ?? "")
        )
        sessions[sIdx].nodes[assistantID] = node

        let combined = answerTokens + thinkingTokens
        if !isScrolledAwayFromBottom && !isAutoScrollPaused && !isFinal && combined % 4 == 0 {
            streamScrollPulse &+= 1
        }
    }

    public func stopActiveGeneration() {
        if let parser = activeStreamParser {
            parser.flushPendingBufferWithoutLosingState(markComplete: false)
        }
        if let sID = streamingSessionID,
           let nID = streamingNodeID,
           let sIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == sID }),
           var node = sessions[sIdx].nodes[nID] {
            if let parser = activeStreamParser {
                node.thinkingTrace = parser.accumulatedThinking
                node.content = parser.accumulatedAnswer
                node.wasStoppedMidThinking = parser.isCurrentlyThinking || (node.content.isEmpty && !node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty)
            }
            node.isThinkingComplete = true
            node.wasStoppedMidStream = true
            if !node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty && node.content.isEmpty {
                node.isThinkingExpanded = true
            }
            sessions[sIdx].nodes[nID] = node
        }
        activeGenerationID = nil
        streamingTask?.cancel()
        streamingTask = nil
        activeStreamParser = nil
        isStreamingGeneration = false
        endStreamingActivityHold()
        isLoadingModelWeights = false
        isHoldingLoadCompletionState = false
        isEvaluatingConversationContext = false
        loadingModelName = ""
        if !loadingModelInternalID.isEmpty,
           let mIdx = availableModels.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == loadingModelInternalID }),
           availableModels[mIdx].residency == .loadingIntoMemory {
            availableModels[mIdx].residency = .dormantOnDisk
        }
        loadingModelInternalID = ""
        isConfirmingModelLoadCancel = false
        streamingSessionID = nil
        streamingNodeID = nil
        saveAllState()
    }

    private func extractArtifactsFromCompletedMessage(assistantID: UUID, targetSessionID: UUID) {
        guard let sIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == targetSessionID }),
              let node = sessions[sIdx].nodes[assistantID] else { return }

        let pattern = "```([a-zA-Z0-9_-]*)\\n([\\s\\S]*?)```"
        guard let regex = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: pattern) else { return }
        let nsContent = node.content as NSString
        let matches = regex.matches(in: node.content, range: NSRange(location: 0, length: nsContent.length))

        for (idx, match) in matches.enumerated() where match.numberOfRanges >= 3 {
            let lang = nsContent.substring(with: match.range(at: 1)).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
            let code = nsContent.substring(with: match.range(at: 2))
            guard code.count > 32 else { continue }
            let title = "\(lang.isEmpty ? "Code" : lang.capitalized) #\(idx + 1)"
            if !sessions[sIdx].artifacts.contains(where: { $0.content == code }) {
                let artifact = WorkspaceArtifact(
                    title: title,
                    language: lang.isEmpty ? "swift" : lang,
                    content: code,
                    sourceMessageID: assistantID
                )
                sessions[sIdx].artifacts.append(artifact)
                selectedArtifactID = artifact.id
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Sessions, Workspaces, Presets, Trash & Asset Garbage Collection
    public func createNewSession(folderID: UUID? = nil) {
        let defaultModel = activeModelDescriptor?.name ?? availableModels.first?.name ?? ""
        let inheritedParams = preferredParameters(forModelName: defaultModel)
        let newSession = ConversationSession(
            title: "New Chat",
            folderID: folderID,
            selectedModelName: defaultModel,
            parameters: inheritedParams
        )
        sessions.insert(newSession, at: 0)
        selectedSessionID = newSession.id
        saveAllState()
        requestComposerFocus()
    }

    public func createWorkspace(name: String) {
        let cleaned = name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !cleaned.isEmpty else { return }
        folders.append(ProjectFolder(name: cleaned, isExpanded: true))
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func renameWorkspace(folderID: UUID, newName: String) {
        let cleaned = newName.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !cleaned.isEmpty, let idx = folders.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == folderID }) else { return }
        folders[idx].name = cleaned
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func deleteWorkspace(folderID: UUID) {
        for idx in sessions.indices where sessions[idx].folderID == folderID {
            sessions[idx].folderID = nil
        }
        folders.removeAll { $0.id == folderID }
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func createPreset(name: String) {
        let cleaned = name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !cleaned.isEmpty else { return }
        let p = activeSession?.parameters ?? .nativeBalanced
        let prompt = activeSession?.systemPrompt ?? ""
        let tempLabel = p.temperature.map { String(format: "Temp %.2f", $0) } ?? "Native Temp"
        let preset = ParameterPreset(
            name: cleaned,
            summary: "\(p.thinkingMode.displayLabel) · \(tempLabel) · \(p.numCtx / 1024)k ctx · KV \(p.kvCacheQuant.rawValue.uppercased())",
            parameters: p,
            systemPrompt: prompt
        )
        presets.append(preset)
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func updatePresetWithCurrentSettings(_ presetID: UUID) {
        guard let pIdx = presets.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == presetID }),
              let session = activeSession else { return }
        let p = session.parameters
        let tempLabel = p.temperature.map { String(format: "Temp %.2f", $0) } ?? "Native Temp"
        presets[pIdx].parameters = p
        presets[pIdx].systemPrompt = session.systemPrompt
        presets[pIdx].summary = "\(p.thinkingMode.displayLabel) · \(tempLabel) · \(p.numCtx / 1024)k ctx · KV \(p.kvCacheQuant.rawValue.uppercased())"
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func deletePreset(_ presetID: UUID) {
        presets.removeAll { $0.id == presetID }
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func moveSessionToTrash(_ sessionID: UUID) {
        if streamingSessionID == sessionID { stopActiveGeneration() }
        guard let idx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == sessionID }) else { return }
        sessions[idx].isTrashed = true
        sessions[idx].trashedAt = Date()
        if selectedSessionID == sessionID {
            selectedSessionID = sessions.first(where: { !$0.isTrashed })?.id
        }
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func restoreSessionFromTrash(_ sessionID: UUID) {
        guard let idx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == sessionID }) else { return }
        sessions[idx].isTrashed = false
        sessions[idx].trashedAt = nil
        selectedSessionID = sessionID
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func permanentlyDeleteSession(_ sessionID: UUID) {
        if streamingSessionID == sessionID { stopActiveGeneration() }
        sessions.removeAll { $0.id == sessionID }
        if selectedSessionID == sessionID {
            selectedSessionID = sessions.first(where: { !$0.isTrashed })?.id
        }
        saveAllState()
        runAssetGarbageCollection()
    }

    public func emptyTrashPermanently() {
        sessions.removeAll { $0.isTrashed }
        saveAllState()
        runAssetGarbageCollection()
    }

    private func runAssetGarbageCollection() {
        var referenced = Set<String>()
        for session in sessions {
            for att in session.draftAttachments {
                if let f = att.localAssetFilename { referenced.insert(f) }
            }
            for node in session.nodes.values {
                for att in node.attachments {
                    if let f = att.localAssetFilename { referenced.insert(f) }
                }
            }
        }
        for att in pendingAttachments {
            if let f = att.localAssetFilename { referenced.insert(f) }
        }
        vault.garbageCollectUnreferencedAssets(referencedFilenames: referenced)
    }

    public func duplicateSession(_ sessionID: UUID) {
        guard let orig = sessions.first(where: { $0.id == sessionID }) else { return }
        var copy = orig
        copy.id = UUID()
        copy.title = "\(orig.title) Copy"
        copy.isTrashed = false
        copy.createdAt = Date()
        copy.updatedAt = Date()
        sessions.insert(copy, at: 0)
        selectedSessionID = copy.id
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func commitInlineRename(sessionID: UUID, proposedName: String) {
        guard let idx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == sessionID }) else { return }
        sessions[idx].title = ConversationSession.sanitizeTitle(proposedName)
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func adjustActiveFontSize(delta: Double) {
        if isWindowFullscreen {
            typography.fullscreenBaseFontSize = min(22.0, max(13.0, typography.fullscreenBaseFontSize + delta))
        } else {
            typography.windowedBaseFontSize = min(19.0, max(11.5, typography.windowedBaseFontSize + delta))
        }
    }

    public func saveAllState() {
        vault.scheduleDebouncedSave(sessions: sessions, folders: folders, presets: presets, personas: slashPersonas)
    }

    // MARK: - Per-Digest Model Pulling (Smooth Non-Jumping Progress!)
    public func startModelDownload(reference: String) {
        let trimmed = reference.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !trimmed.isEmpty,
              let url = URL(string: "\(supervisor.normalizedEndpoint)/api/pull") else { return }

        let taskID = UUID()
        let newTask = RegistryDownloadTask(
            id: taskID,
            modelReference: trimmed,
            statusText: "Starting download…",
            layerProgress: [:],
            isCompleted: false
        )
        downloadTasks.insert(newTask, at: 0)

        let handle = Task {
            var req = URLRequest(url: url)
            req.httpMethod = "POST"
            req.setValue("application/json", forHTTPHeaderField: "Content-Type")
            req.timeoutInterval = 3600.0
            req.httpBody = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: ["model": trimmed, "stream": true])

            do {
                let (bytes, response) = try await OllamaNetworking.ephemeralSession.bytes(for: req)
                if let http = response as? HTTPURLResponse, http.statusCode != 200 {
                    if let tIdx = self.downloadTasks.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == taskID }) {
                        self.downloadTasks[tIdx].statusText = "HTTP \(http.statusCode) error"
                    }
                    self.downloadTaskHandles.removeValue(forKey: taskID)
                    return
                }

                for try await line in bytes.lines {
                    if Task.isCancelled { break }
                    guard let data = line.data(using: .utf8),
                          let json = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any] else { continue }
                    if let err = json["error"] as? String {
                        if let tIdx = self.downloadTasks.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == taskID }) {
                            self.downloadTasks[tIdx].statusText = "Error: \(err)"
                        }
                        break
                    }

                    let status = (json["status"] as? String) ?? "Downloading…"
                    let digest = (json["digest"] as? String) ?? ""
                    let completed = (json["completed"] as? NSNumber)?.int64Value ?? 0
                    let total = (json["total"] as? NSNumber)?.int64Value ?? 0

                    if let tIdx = self.downloadTasks.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == taskID }) {
                        self.downloadTasks[tIdx].statusText = status
                        if !digest.isEmpty && total > 0 {
                            let prev = self.downloadTasks[tIdx].layerProgress[digest]
                            self.downloadTasks[tIdx].layerProgress[digest] = RegistryDownloadTask.LayerBytes(
                                completed: max(completed, prev?.completed ?? 0),
                                total: max(total, prev?.total ?? 0)
                            )
                        }
                        if status == "success" {
                            self.downloadTasks[tIdx].isCompleted = true
                        }
                    }
                }
                if !Task.isCancelled {
                    await self.refreshLocalEngineModels()
                }
            } catch {
                if !Task.isCancelled, let tIdx = self.downloadTasks.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == taskID }) {
                    self.downloadTasks[tIdx].statusText = "Failed: \(error.localizedDescription)"
                }
            }
            self.downloadTaskHandles.removeValue(forKey: taskID)
        }
        downloadTaskHandles[taskID] = handle
    }

    public func cancelModelDownload(_ taskID: UUID) {
        downloadTaskHandles[taskID]?.cancel()
        downloadTaskHandles.removeValue(forKey: taskID)
        downloadTasks.removeAll { $0.id == taskID }
    }

    private func loadSavedSessionsAndWorkspaces() {
        if let (savedSessions, savedFolders, savedPresets, savedPersonas) = vault.loadSavedState() {
            self.sessions = savedSessions
            self.folders = savedFolders
            self.presets = savedPresets
            self.slashPersonas = savedPersonas
            if let savedIDStr = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: "MotifSelectedSessionIDV8"),
               let savedUUID = UUID(uuidString: savedIDStr),
               self.sessions.contains(where: { $0.id == savedUUID && !$0.isTrashed }) {
                self.selectedSessionID = savedUUID
            } else {
                self.selectedSessionID = self.sessions.first(where: { !$0.isTrashed })?.id
            }
            restoreDraftFromSelectedSession()
        }
    }
}
EOF
)
