import SwiftUI
import AppKit
import UniformTypeIdentifiers

private struct ComposerHeightPreferenceKey: PreferenceKey {
    static var defaultValue: CGFloat = 78.0
    static func reduce(value: inout CGFloat, nextValue: () -> CGFloat) {
        value = max(value, nextValue())
    }
}

public struct EditorialConversationView: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    public let tokens: MotifTokens

    // Plain @State reference so ScrollJumpOverlayView updates without re-rendering the entire conversation list!
    @State private var scrollController = ChatScrollController()
    @State private var editingMessageNodeID: UUID? = nil
    @State private var editMessageDraft: String = ""
    @State private var clearThinkingOnAssistantEdit: Bool = false
    @State private var isEditBoxExpandedFull: Bool = false
    @State private var confirmingDeleteNodeID: UUID? = nil
    @State private var composerMeasuredHeight: CGFloat = 78.0
    @State private var isFileDropTargeted: Bool = false

    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }

    public var body: some View {
        GeometryReader { geo in
            let canvasWidth = geo.size.width
            let isFullscreenOrWide = store.isWindowFullscreen || canvasWidth >= 1320
            let isCompactWindow = canvasWidth < 920
            let horizontalOuterPadding: CGFloat = canvasWidth < 900 ? 22.0 : 34.0

            let opticalFontSize = typo.effectiveFontSize(forCanvasWidth: canvasWidth, isFullscreen: isFullscreenOrWide)
            let readingColumnWidth = typo.effectiveReadingWidth(forCanvasWidth: canvasWidth, isFullscreen: isFullscreenOrWide)

            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                topHeaderBar(isCompactWindow: isCompactWindow)

                ZStack {
                    HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)
                    ModelLoadedHorizonSweep(pulseID: store.modelReadySweepID, accentColor: tokens.statusOnline)
                }

                if let errBanner = store.humanReadableErrorBanner {
                    errorNoticeBanner(text: errBanner)
                }

                if let session = store.activeSession {
                    contextAndMemoryTopBanner(session: session)

                    ZStack(alignment: .bottom) {
                        if session.activeBranchThread.isEmpty {
                            quietNewChatCanvas(
                                session: session,
                                columnWidth: readingColumnWidth
                            )
                        } else {
                            conversationStreamCanvas(
                                session: session,
                                columnWidth: readingColumnWidth,
                                horizontalPadding: horizontalOuterPadding,
                                opticalFontSize: opticalFontSize,
                                isFullscreenOrWide: isFullscreenOrWide
                            )
                        }

                        // Soft paper dissolve behind the floating single-line pill composer
                        VStack(spacing: 0) {
                            LinearGradient(
                                colors: [tokens.canvasBackground.opacity(0.0), tokens.canvasBackground],
                                startPoint: .top,
                                endPoint: .bottom
                            )
                            .frame(height: 24)
                            .allowsHitTesting(false)

                            VStack(spacing: 8) {
                                if let readyMsg = store.modelReadyBannerText {
                                    ModelLoadedReadyPill(message: readyMsg, tokens: tokens, typo: typo)
                                        .transition(.move(edge: .bottom).combined(with: .opacity))
                                }

                                ProgressiveComposerBar(
                                    store: store,
                                    tokens: tokens,
                                    maxBarWidth: readingColumnWidth,
                                    activeFontSize: opticalFontSize,
                                    isWideOrFullscreen: isFullscreenOrWide
                                )
                            }
                            .padding(.horizontal, horizontalOuterPadding)
                            .padding(.bottom, 16)
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                            .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                        }
                        .background(
                            GeometryReader { compGeo in
                                Color.clear.preference(key: ComposerHeightPreferenceKey.self, value: compGeo.size.height)
                            }
                        )
                    }
                    .onPreferenceChange(ComposerHeightPreferenceKey.self) { newH in
                        if abs(composerMeasuredHeight - newH) > 1.0 { composerMeasuredHeight = newH }
                    }
                    .onChange(of: store.selectedSessionID) { _, _ in
                        scrollController.reset()
                        editingMessageNodeID = nil
                        confirmingDeleteNodeID = nil
                    }
                } else {
                    strictNoSessionLandingGate
                }
            }
            .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
            .overlay {
                if isFileDropTargeted {
                    ZStack {
                        tokens.canvasBackground.opacity(0.88)
                        VStack(spacing: 10) {
                            Image(systemName: "arrow.down.doc")
                                .font(.system(size: 26, weight: .light))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            Text("Drop files, code, or images to attach")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 14, weight: .medium))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        }
                        .padding(28)
                        .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14, style: .continuous))
                        .overlay(
                            RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14, style: .continuous)
                                .strokeBorder(tokens.strongBorder, style: StrokeStyle(lineWidth: 1.25, dash: [6, 5]))
                        )
                        .shadow(color: Color.black.opacity(0.06), radius: 16, y: 4)
                    }
                    .allowsHitTesting(false)
                    .transition(.opacity)
                }
            }
            .onDrop(of: [.fileURL, .image], isTargeted: $isFileDropTargeted) { providers in
                handleDroppedProviders(providers)
            }
        }
    }

    private func handleDroppedProviders(_ providers: [NSItemProvider]) -> Bool {
        guard store.activeSession != nil else { return false }
        var handled = false
        for provider in providers {
            if provider.hasItemConformingToTypeIdentifier(UTType.fileURL.identifier) {
                handled = true
                provider.loadItem(forTypeIdentifier: UTType.fileURL.identifier, options: nil) { item, _ in
                    var resolvedURL: URL? = nil
                    if let data = item as? Data, let str = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) {
                        resolvedURL = URL(string: str)
                    } else if let url = item as? URL {
                        resolvedURL = url
                    }
                    if let url = resolvedURL {
                        Task { @MainActor in store.attachFilesFromURLs([url]) }
                    }
                }
            } else if provider.hasItemConformingToTypeIdentifier(UTType.image.identifier) {
                handled = true
                provider.loadDataRepresentation(forTypeIdentifier: UTType.image.identifier) { data, _ in
                    if let data = data {
                        Task { @MainActor in store.handleClipboardImagePaste(data) }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return handled
    }

    // MARK: - Top Header Bar (Model Selector Only Appears Here Once First Message Is Sent!)
    private func topHeaderBar(isCompactWindow: Bool) -> some View {
        let leadingClearance: CGFloat = (!store.isSidebarVisible && !store.isWindowFullscreen) ? 78.0 : 16.0
        let hasActiveMessages = !(store.activeSession?.activeBranchThread.isEmpty ?? true)

        return HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 8) {
            if !store.isSidebarVisible {
                QuietIconButton(
                    systemName: "sidebar.left",
                    tokens: tokens,
                    size: 13.5,
                    helpText: "Show Sidebar (⌘\(store.shortcuts.toggleSidebarKey.uppercased()))"
                ) {
                    store.isSidebarVisible = true
                }

                QuietIconButton(
                    systemName: "square.and.pencil",
                    tokens: tokens,
                    size: 13.5,
                    helpText: "New Chat (⌘\(store.shortcuts.newChatKey.uppercased()))"
                ) {
                    store.createNewSession(folderID: nil)
                }
            }

            Text(store.activeSession?.title ?? "Motif")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 13.5, weight: .regular))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                .lineLimit(1)
                .padding(.leading, store.isSidebarVisible ? 2 : 4)

            if let pillMsg = store.subtleStatusPillMessage {
                Text(pillMsg)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                    .padding(.vertical, 3)
                    .background(Capsule().fill(tokens.codeSurface))
                    .overlay(Capsule().strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
            }

            Spacer(minLength: 10)

            if hasActiveMessages {
                topRightModelSelectorPill(isCompactWindow: isCompactWindow)
            }

            HeaderMemoryIndicatorView(store: store, tokens: tokens, isCompactWindow: isCompactWindow)

            if store.activeSession != nil && !isCompactWindow {
                QuietIconButton(
                    systemName: "sidebar.right",
                    tokens: tokens,
                    size: 13.5,
                    isActive: store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen,
                    helpText: "Toggle Code Inspector (⌘\(store.shortcuts.toggleInspectorKey))"
                ) {
                    store.userManuallyClosedRightPaneInWideMode = store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen
                    store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen.toggle()
                }
            }
        }
        .padding(.leading, leadingClearance)
        .padding(.trailing, 14)
        .frame(height: 44)
        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
    }

    // MARK: - Quiet Living Model Selector Pill (Top-Right During Active Conversations)
    @ViewBuilder
    private func topRightModelSelectorPill(isCompactWindow: Bool) -> some View {
        let isLoading = store.isLoadingModelWeights
        let isHoldingDone = store.isHoldingLoadCompletionState
        let snap = store.memorySnapshot
        let loadFraction: CGFloat = isHoldingDone
            ? 1.0
            : (isLoading ? CGFloat(min(0.99, max(0.02, snap.loadProgressAgainstTarget))) : 0.0)
        let pct = isHoldingDone ? 100 : Int(loadFraction * 100.0)
        let isResident = store.activeModelDescriptor?.residency == .activeInUnifiedRAM || isHoldingDone
        let flashOpacity = store.modelLoadCompletionRimFlashOpacity
        let isFlashingCompletion = flashOpacity > 0.01 || isHoldingDone
        let activeModelTitle = isLoading
            ? LocalModelDescriptor.shortenModelName(store.loadingModelName)
            : (store.activeModelDescriptor?.shortDisplayName ?? "Select model")

        let thinkSuffix: String? = {
            guard !isLoading, let session = store.activeSession, let m = store.activeModelDescriptor else { return nil }
            let supports = m.thinkingProfile.kind != .unsupported || session.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported
            guard supports, session.parameters.thinkingMode.isThinkingEnabled else { return nil }
            return session.parameters.thinkingMode.displayLabel
        }()

        HStack(spacing: 5) {
            if isLoading && store.isConfirmingModelLoadCancel && !isHoldingDone {
                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                    Text("Cancel \(activeModelTitle)?")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    Button("Cancel load") { store.confirmCancelModelLoad() }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 3)
                        .background(Capsule().fill(tokens.accentAction))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    Button("Keep") { store.isConfirmingModelLoadCancel = false }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 10)
                .frame(height: 28)
                .background(tokens.codeSurface)
                .clipShape(Capsule())
                .overlay(Capsule().strokeBorder(tokens.strongBorder, lineWidth: 1))
            } else {
                Button {
                    store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented.toggle()
                } label: {
                    HStack(spacing: 6) {
                        Circle()
                            .fill((isHoldingDone || isResident) ? tokens.statusOnline : (isLoading ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.mutedText.opacity(0.55)))
                            .frame(width: 5.5, height: 5.5)

                        Text(activeModelTitle)
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            .lineLimit(1)
                            .frame(maxWidth: isCompactWindow ? 120 : 190, alignment: .leading)

                        if let suffix = thinkSuffix {
                            Text(suffix)
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .regular))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                        }

                        if isHoldingDone {
                            Text("100%")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold).monospacedDigit())
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.statusOnline)
                        } else if isLoading {
                            Text("\(pct)%")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        } else {
                            Image(systemName: "chevron.down")
                                .font(.system(size: 8.5, weight: .medium))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal, 11)
                    .frame(height: 28)
                    .background(
                        GeometryReader { pillGeo in
                            ZStack(alignment: .leading) {
                                Rectangle().fill(tokens.composerSurface)
                                if isLoading {
                                    Rectangle()
                                        .fill(isHoldingDone ? tokens.statusOnline.opacity(0.18) : tokens.selectionPill)
                                        .frame(width: max(8, pillGeo.size.width * loadFraction))
                                        .animation(.easeOut(duration: 0.18), value: loadFraction)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    )
                    .clipShape(Capsule())
                    .overlay(
                        Capsule().strokeBorder(
                            isFlashingCompletion ? tokens.statusOnline.opacity(max(0.55, flashOpacity)) : tokens.hairline,
                            lineWidth: isFlashingCompletion ? 1.4 : 1.0
                        )
                    )
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                .help("Select or load local model (⌘⇧M)")
                .popover(isPresented: $store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented, arrowEdge: .bottom) {
                    ModelPickerPopoverView(store: store, tokens: tokens)
                }

                if isLoading && !isHoldingDone {
                    Button { store.requestCancelModelLoadConfirmation() } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "xmark")
                            .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .semibold))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                            .frame(width: 22, height: 22)
                            .background(Circle().fill(tokens.codeSurface))
                            .overlay(Circle().strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .help("Cancel model loading")
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Context Ceiling & Memory Pressure Notice
    @ViewBuilder
    private func contextAndMemoryTopBanner(session: ConversationSession) -> some View {
        let usedTok = session.estimatedActiveTokens
        let maxTok = max(1, session.parameters.numCtx)
        let ratio = Double(usedTok) / Double(maxTok)
        let hasMemWarn = store.kernelMemoryPressureWarning != nil
        let showContextWarn = ratio >= 0.85 && !store.isContextCeilingBannerDismissed

        if hasMemWarn || showContextWarn {
            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                HStack(spacing: 10) {
                    Circle().fill(tokens.statusWarning).frame(width: 6, height: 6)
                    if let memText = store.kernelMemoryPressureWarning {
                        Text(memText).font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    } else {
                        Text("Context window at \(Int(min(100, ratio * 100)))% (\(usedTok) / \(maxTok) tok)")
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    }
                    Spacer()

                    if let nextTier = store.nextHigherContextTier(for: session), !hasMemWarn {
                        Button("Expand to \(nextTier / 1024)k") { store.stepUpContextTier() }
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            .padding(.horizontal, 9).padding(.vertical, 4)
                            .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                            .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }

                    Button("Trim oldest") { store.trimOldestTurnInActiveSession() }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 9).padding(.vertical, 4)
                        .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)

                    Button("Summarize branch") { store.summarizeContextIntoNewBranch() }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 9).padding(.vertical, 4)
                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)

                    Button {
                        store.kernelMemoryPressureWarning = nil
                        store.isContextCeilingBannerDismissed = true
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "xmark").font(.system(size: 10, weight: .medium)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 20)
                .padding(.vertical, 8)
                .background(tokens.codeSurface)
                HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)
            }
        }
    }

    private func errorNoticeBanner(text: String) -> some View {
        VStack(spacing: 0) {
            HStack(spacing: 12) {
                Text(text)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                Spacer()
                if !store.supervisor.isEngineOnline {
                    Button("Start Ollama") { Task { await store.bootstrapLocalOllamaConnection() } }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
                Button("Dismiss") { store.humanReadableErrorBanner = nil }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 20)
            .padding(.vertical, 8)
            .background(tokens.codeSurface)
            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)
        }
    }

    private var strictNoSessionLandingGate: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 16) {
            Spacer()
            Text("Motif")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 26, weight: .semibold))
                .tracking(-0.5)
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
            Text("Start a conversation with your local models.")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 14))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
            Button {
                store.createNewSession(folderID: nil)
            } label: {
                HStack(spacing: 7) {
                    Image(systemName: "plus").font(.system(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                    Text("New chat").font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .medium))
                }
                .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                .padding(.horizontal, 16).padding(.vertical, 8)
                .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            Spacer()
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)
    }

    // MARK: - Quiet New-Chat Studio Canvas (Model Loader + Thinking + Context + KV Precision + Temp/Top-P/Min-P)
    @ViewBuilder
    private func quietNewChatCanvas(session: ConversationSession, columnWidth: CGFloat) -> some View {
        let activeSessionIdx = store.sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == session.id })
        let model = store.activeModelDescriptor
        let isLoading = store.isLoadingModelWeights
        let isHoldingDone = store.isHoldingLoadCompletionState
        let snap = store.memorySnapshot
        let loadFraction = isHoldingDone ? 1.0 : min(0.99, max(0.02, snap.loadProgressAgainstTarget))
        let pct = isHoldingDone ? 100 : Int(loadFraction * 100.0)
        let targetGB = max(0.1, snap.targetModelGB)
        let allocatedGB = isHoldingDone ? targetGB : min(targetGB, max(snap.weightsGB, snap.activeRAMDeltaGB))
        let flashOpacity = store.modelLoadCompletionRimFlashOpacity
        let isFlashingCompletion = flashOpacity > 0.01 || isHoldingDone

        ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: false) {
            VStack(spacing: 22) {
                Spacer(minLength: 42)

                Text(session.title == "New Chat" ? "What can I help with?" : session.title)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 24, weight: .medium))
                    .tracking(-0.45)
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    .multilineTextAlignment(.center)

                if let sIdx = activeSessionIdx, let m = model {
                    let sessionBinding = $store.sessions[sIdx]
                    let profile = m.thinkingProfile
                    let supportsThinking = profile.kind != .unsupported || sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported
                    let isResident = m.residency == .activeInUnifiedRAM || isHoldingDone

                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                        // 1. Model Selector + Explicit RAM Loader (Transitions to Top-Right After First Message)
                        HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 10) {
                            Button {
                                store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented.toggle()
                            } label: {
                                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                                    Circle()
                                        .fill((isHoldingDone || isResident) ? tokens.statusOnline : (isLoading ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.mutedText.opacity(0.55)))
                                        .frame(width: 6.5, height: 6.5)
                                    Text(m.shortDisplayName)
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .semibold))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                        .lineLimit(1)
                                    Text("· \(m.formattedSize)")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5).monospacedDigit())
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                    Image(systemName: "chevron.up.chevron.down")
                                        .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .medium))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                                }
                                .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                                .frame(height: 32)
                                .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous))
                                .overlay(
                                    RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous)
                                        .strokeBorder(
                                            isFlashingCompletion ? tokens.statusOnline.opacity(max(0.55, flashOpacity)) : tokens.hairline,
                                            lineWidth: isFlashingCompletion ? 1.5 : 1.0
                                        )
                                )
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            .help("Choose local model (⌘⇧M)")
                            .popover(isPresented: $store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented, arrowEdge: .bottom) {
                                ModelPickerPopoverView(store: store, tokens: tokens)
                            }

                            Spacer()

                            if isLoading && !isHoldingDone {
                                if store.isConfirmingModelLoadCancel {
                                    HStack(spacing: 6) {
                                        Button("Confirm cancel") { store.confirmCancelModelLoad() }
                                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                                            .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                                            .padding(.horizontal, 10).frame(height: 28)
                                            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                        Button("Keep") { store.isConfirmingModelLoadCancel = false }
                                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    Button("Cancel load") { store.requestCancelModelLoadConfirmation() }
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                        .padding(.horizontal, 11).frame(height: 30)
                                        .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous))
                                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                }
                            } else if isResident {
                                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                                    Text(isHoldingDone ? "Loaded 100%" : "In RAM")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.statusOnline)
                                    Button("Unload") { Task { await store.unloadModelFromMemory(m) } }
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .regular))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                        .padding(.horizontal, 10).frame(height: 28)
                                        .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous))
                                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                }
                            } else {
                                Button("Load into RAM") { store.selectAndLoadModel(m) }
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                                    .padding(.horizontal, 12).frame(height: 30)
                                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            }
                        }

                        if isLoading {
                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 5) {
                                HStack {
                                    Text(isHoldingDone
                                         ? "\(store.loadingModelName) loaded in Unified Memory"
                                         : "Loading \(store.loadingModelName) into Unified Memory…")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                                        .foregroundColor(isHoldingDone ? tokens.statusOnline : tokens.secondaryText)
                                    Spacer()
                                    Text(String(format: "%.1f / %.1f GB (%d%%)", allocatedGB, targetGB, pct))
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                                        .foregroundColor(isHoldingDone ? tokens.statusOnline : tokens.primaryText)
                                }
                                ProgressView(value: loadFraction)
                                    .tint(isHoldingDone ? tokens.statusOnline : tokens.primaryText)
                            }
                        }

                        HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                        // 2. Thinking Mode
                        HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 12) {
                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 1) {
                                Text("Thinking")
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                Text(profile.detectedMechanismSummary)
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                                    .lineLimit(1)
                            }
                            Spacer()
                            if supportsThinking {
                                let allowedModes: [ThinkingModeOption] = sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported
                                    ? [.off, .on, .level("low"), .level("medium"), .level("high")]
                                    : profile.validOptions

                                HStack(spacing: 2) {
                                    ForEach(allowedModes) { mode in
                                        let selected = sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.thinkingMode == mode
                                        Button {
                                            sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.thinkingMode = mode
                                            store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                                        } label: {
                                            Text(mode.displayLabel)
                                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: selected ? .semibold : .regular))
                                                .foregroundColor(selected ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.secondaryText)
                                                .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 4)
                                                .background(
                                                    RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous)
                                                        .fill(selected ? tokens.composerSurface : Color.clear)
                                                )
                                                .overlay(
                                                    RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous)
                                                        .strokeBorder(selected ? tokens.hairline : Color.clear, lineWidth: 1)
                                                )
                                        }
                                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                    }
                                }
                                .padding(2.5)
                                .background(tokens.codeSurface)
                                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous))
                                .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                            } else {
                                Toggle("Force override", isOn: sessionBinding.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported)
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                            }
                        }

                        // 3. Context Window + KV Cache Precision
                        HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 12) {
                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 1) {
                                Text("Context window")
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                Text("\(sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.numCtx) tok · ~\(String(format: "%.2f GB", snap.kvCacheGB)) KV")
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5).monospacedDigit())
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                            }
                            Spacer()
                            let tiers = [4096, 8192, 16384, 32768].filter { $0 <= max(4096, m.maxContextLength) }
                            HStack(spacing: 2) {
                                ForEach(tiers, id: \.self) { t in
                                    let selected = sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.numCtx == t
                                    Button {
                                        sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.numCtx = t
                                        store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                                        store.recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
                                    } label: {
                                        Text("\(t / 1024)k")
                                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: selected ? .semibold : .regular).monospacedDigit())
                                            .foregroundColor(selected ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.secondaryText)
                                            .padding(.horizontal, 9).padding(.vertical, 4)
                                            .background(
                                                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous)
                                                    .fill(selected ? tokens.composerSurface : Color.clear)
                                            )
                                            .overlay(
                                                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous)
                                                    .strokeBorder(selected ? tokens.hairline : Color.clear, lineWidth: 1)
                                            )
                                    }
                                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                }
                            }
                            .padding(2.5)
                            .background(tokens.codeSurface)
                            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous))
                            .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                        }

                        HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 12) {
                            Text("KV cache precision")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            Spacer()
                            Picker("", selection: sessionBinding.parameters.kvCacheQuant) {
                                ForEach(KVCacheQuantization.allCases) { q in Text(q.displayTitle).tag(q) }
                            }
                            .pickerStyle(.menu)
                            .labelsHidden()
                            .frame(width: 265)
                            .onChange(of: sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.kvCacheQuant) { _, newQuant in
                                store.applyKVCacheQuantizationLive(newQuant)
                            }
                        }

                        HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                        // 4. Clean 3-Column Sampling Strip (Temperature, Top-P, Min-P)
                        HStack(spacing: 18) {
                            compactQuickSlider(
                                label: "Temperature",
                                value: sessionBinding.parameters.temperature,
                                fallback: 0.70,
                                range: 0.0...2.0
                            )
                            compactQuickSlider(
                                label: "Top-P",
                                value: sessionBinding.parameters.topP,
                                fallback: 0.90,
                                range: 0.05...1.0
                            )
                            compactQuickSlider(
                                label: "Min-P",
                                value: sessionBinding.parameters.minP,
                                fallback: 0.05,
                                range: 0.0...0.5
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(18)
                    .frame(maxWidth: min(columnWidth, 620))
                    .background(tokens.codeSurface.opacity(0.55))
                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14, style: .continuous))
                    .overlay(
                        RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14, style: .continuous)
                            .strokeBorder(
                                isFlashingCompletion ? tokens.statusOnline.opacity(max(0.55, flashOpacity)) : tokens.hairline,
                                lineWidth: isFlashingCompletion ? 1.5 : 1.0
                            )
                    )
                } else {
                    Button("Open Settings to pull a local model…") {
                        store.settingsRequestedTab = "Models & Storage"
                        store.isSettingsModalPresented = true
                    }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }

                Spacer(minLength: 24)
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 24)
            .padding(.bottom, max(96, composerMeasuredHeight + 14))
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
            .background(OverlayScrollConfigurator(isDark: tokens.isEffectivelyDark).frame(width: 1, height: 1))
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)
    }

    private func compactQuickSlider(
        label: String,
        value: Binding<Double?>,
        fallback: Double,
        range: ClosedRange<Double>
    ) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
            HStack(spacing: 4) {
                Text(label)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                Spacer()
                if let v = value.wrappedValue {
                    Text(String(format: "%.2f", v))
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold).monospacedDigit())
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    Button {
                        value.wrappedValue = nil
                        store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "arrow.counterclockwise")
                            .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .medium))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .help("Reset \(label) to model default")
                } else {
                    Text("Default")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                }
            }
            Slider(
                value: Binding(
                    get: { value.wrappedValue ?? fallback },
                    set: {
                        value.wrappedValue = $0
                        store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                    }
                ),
                in: range
            )
            .controlSize(.small)
            .tint(value.wrappedValue == nil ? tokens.mutedText.opacity(0.45) : tokens.primaryText)
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Responsive Desktop Conversation Stream (Session-Keyed So Chat Switching Never Goes Blank)
    private func conversationStreamCanvas(
        session: ConversationSession,
        columnWidth: CGFloat,
        horizontalPadding: CGFloat,
        opticalFontSize: CGFloat,
        isFullscreenOrWide: Bool
    ) -> some View {
        let thread = session.activeBranchThread
        let lastTurnID = thread.last?.id
        let dynamicBottomClearance = max(124.0, composerMeasuredHeight + 24.0)
        let jumpButtonBottomOffset = max(92.0, composerMeasuredHeight + 6.0)

        return ScrollViewReader { proxy in
            ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: true) {
                LazyVStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 26) {
                    ForEach(thread) { node in
                        ConversationTurnRow(
                            store: store,
                            node: node,
                            session: session,
                            isLatestTurn: node.id == lastTurnID,
                            opticalFontSize: opticalFontSize,
                            isFullscreenOrWide: isFullscreenOrWide,
                            columnWidth: columnWidth,
                            tokens: tokens,
                            editingMessageNodeID: $editingMessageNodeID,
                            editMessageDraft: $editMessageDraft,
                            clearThinkingOnAssistantEdit: $clearThinkingOnAssistantEdit,
                            isEditBoxExpandedFull: $isEditBoxExpandedFull,
                            confirmingDeleteNodeID: $confirmingDeleteNodeID
                        )
                        .id(node.id)
                    }

                    Color.clear
                        .frame(height: dynamicBottomClearance)
                        .id("STREAM_BOTTOM_ANCHOR")
                }
                .background(
                    ChatScrollBridgeView(
                        controller: scrollController,
                        isDark: tokens.isEffectivelyDark
                    ) { isAway in
                        store.isScrolledAwayFromBottom = isAway
                    }
                    .frame(width: 1, height: 1)
                )
                .frame(maxWidth: columnWidth, alignment: .leading)
                .padding(.horizontal, horizontalPadding)
                .padding(.top, 26)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
            }
            .id(session.id)
            .onAppear {
                DispatchQueue.main.async {
                    proxy.scrollTo("STREAM_BOTTOM_ANCHOR", anchor: .bottom)
                }
            }
            .onChange(of: store.streamScrollPulse) { _, _ in
                if store.isStreamingGeneration && !store.isScrolledAwayFromBottom && !store.isAutoScrollPaused {
                    scrollController.scrollToBottom(animated: false)
                }
            }
            .onChange(of: store.pendingScrollToMessageID) { _, targetID in
                if let id = targetID {
                    withAnimation(.spring(response: 0.28, dampingFraction: 0.86)) { proxy.scrollTo(id, anchor: .center) }
                    store.pendingScrollToMessageID = nil
                }
            }
            .overlay(alignment: .bottom) {
                HStack {
                    Spacer()
                    ScrollJumpOverlayView(
                        controller: scrollController,
                        isHidden: store.isComposerCanvasExpanded || !store.matchedSlashPersonas.isEmpty,
                        tokens: tokens,
                        onJumpTop: {
                            store.isAutoScrollPaused = true
                            scrollController.scrollToTop(animated: true)
                        },
                        onJumpBottom: {
                            store.isScrolledAwayFromBottom = false
                            store.isAutoScrollPaused = false
                            scrollController.scrollToBottom(animated: true)
                        }
                    )
                }
                .frame(maxWidth: columnWidth)
                .padding(.horizontal, horizontalPadding)
                .padding(.bottom, jumpButtonBottomOffset)
            }
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Single Conversation Turn (Rock-Solid Equatable Prose + Dual Top/Bottom Thinking Collapse)
private struct ConversationTurnRow: View {
    @ObservedObject var store: WorkstationStore
    let node: ConversationMessageNode
    let session: ConversationSession
    let isLatestTurn: Bool
    let opticalFontSize: CGFloat
    let isFullscreenOrWide: Bool
    let columnWidth: CGFloat
    let tokens: MotifTokens

    @Binding var editingMessageNodeID: UUID?
    @Binding var editMessageDraft: String
    @Binding var clearThinkingOnAssistantEdit: Bool
    @Binding var isEditBoxExpandedFull: Bool
    @Binding var confirmingDeleteNodeID: UUID?

    @State private var isHovered: Bool = false
    @State private var isCopied: Bool = false
    @State private var isBottomCollapseHovered: Bool = false

    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }
    private var isNodeStreaming: Bool { store.isStreamingGeneration && store.streamingNodeID == node.id }
    private var siblingMeta: (currentIndex: Int, totalCount: Int) { session.siblingInfo(for: node.id) }

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: node.role == .user && editingMessageNodeID != node.id ? .trailing : .leading, spacing: 8) {
            if editingMessageNodeID == node.id {
                inlineMessageEditor
            } else if node.role == .user {
                userTurnBody
            } else {
                assistantTurnBody
            }

            if let err = node.errorNote {
                Text(err)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5))
                    .foregroundColor(.red)
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
            }

            if editingMessageNodeID != node.id {
                turnActionAndTelemetryBar
            }
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: node.role == .user && editingMessageNodeID != node.id ? .trailing : .leading)
        .onHover { hovering in
            withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.14)) { isHovered = hovering }
        }
        .contextMenu {
            Button("Copy Message") { copyNodeText() }
            Button(node.role == .user ? "Edit Prompt…" : "Edit / Trim Reply to Save KV RAM…") { beginEditing() }
            Button("Branch to New Chat from Here") { store.branchMessageToNewChat(upToNodeID: node.id) }
            Divider()
            Button("Delete Message (Free ~\(node.estimatedTokenFootprint) tok)…", role: .destructive) {
                confirmingDeleteNodeID = node.id
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - User Turn (Right-Aligned Soft Neutral Pill)
    private var userTurnBody: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .trailing, spacing: 8) {
            if !node.attachments.isEmpty {
                HStack(spacing: 6) {
                    ForEach(node.attachments) { att in
                        HStack(spacing: 5) {
                            Image(systemName: att.kind == .downscaledVisionImage ? "photo" : "doc.text")
                                .font(.system(size: 10))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                            Text("\(att.title) · \(att.formattedByteSize)")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .regular).monospacedDigit())
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        }
                        .padding(.horizontal, 9).padding(.vertical, 4.5)
                        .background(tokens.codeSurface)
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous))
                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                    }
                }
            }

            if !node.content.isEmpty {
                Text(node.content)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: opticalFontSize, weight: .regular))
                    .lineSpacing(opticalFontSize * 0.42)
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    .textSelection(.enabled)
                    .fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 16)
                    .padding(.vertical, 11)
                    .background(tokens.userBubbleSurface)
                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16, style: .continuous))
                    .frame(maxWidth: max(280, columnWidth * 0.78), alignment: .trailing)
            }
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .trailing)
    }

    // MARK: - Assistant Turn (Distinct Indented Reasoning Marginalia + Full-Width Primary Answer)
    private var assistantTurnBody: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
            if !node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty || (!node.isThinkingComplete && isNodeStreaming) {
                editorialThinkingSection
            }

            if node.content.isEmpty && isNodeStreaming {
                if store.isLoadingModelWeights {
                    let isHoldingDone = store.isHoldingLoadCompletionState
                    let frac = isHoldingDone ? 1.0 : min(0.99, max(0.02, store.memorySnapshot.loadProgressAgainstTarget))
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                        Text(isHoldingDone
                             ? "\(store.loadingModelName) loaded into Unified Memory (100%)"
                             : "Loading \(store.loadingModelName) into Unified Memory (\(Int(frac * 100))%)…")
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: .regular).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(isHoldingDone ? tokens.statusOnline : tokens.mutedText)
                        ProgressView(value: frac)
                            .tint(isHoldingDone ? tokens.statusOnline : tokens.primaryText)
                            .frame(maxWidth: 240)
                    }
                } else if store.isEvaluatingConversationContext && node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty {
                    Text("Reading conversation context…")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 13))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                } else if node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty {
                    Text("Generating…")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 13))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                }
            } else if !node.content.isEmpty {
                StreamSafeMarkdownView(
                    rawMarkdown: isNodeStreaming ? (node.content + " ▍") : node.content,
                    isStreaming: isNodeStreaming,
                    effectiveFontSize: opticalFontSize,
                    tokens: tokens,
                    typo: typo,
                    onOpenCodeInInspector: { _, _ in
                        store.userManuallyClosedRightPaneInWideMode = false
                        store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen = true
                    }
                )
                .equatable()
            }
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
    }

    /// Crisp non-animated LazyVStack state flip so expanding/collapsing thinking NEVER drops or flickers glyphs.
    private func toggleThinkingExpanded() {
        guard let sIdx = store.sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == store.selectedSessionID }),
              var target = store.sessions[sIdx].nodes[node.id] else { return }
        target.isThinkingExpanded.toggle()
        store.sessions[sIdx].nodes[node.id] = target
    }

    // MARK: - Editorial Thinking Block (Top Toggle + Distinct Muted Scratchpad + Seamless Bottom Collapse Cap)
    @ViewBuilder
    private var editorialThinkingSection: some View {
        let isActivelyThinking = !node.isThinkingComplete && isNodeStreaming
        let relFont = max(11.5, opticalFontSize - 1.8)
        let thinkSec = node.isThinkingComplete ? (node.metrics?.thinkingDurationSec ?? 0.0) : store.liveElapsedSeconds

        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 0) {
            Button(action: toggleThinkingExpanded) {
                HStack(spacing: 6) {
                    Image(systemName: "sparkles")
                        .font(.system(size: 11.5, weight: .regular))
                        .foregroundColor(isActivelyThinking ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.mutedText)

                    Text(node.wasStoppedMidThinking ? "Thought process · Stopped" : (isActivelyThinking ? "Thinking…" : "Thought process"))
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(isActivelyThinking ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.secondaryText)

                    if thinkSec >= 0.1 {
                        Text(String(format: "· %.1fs", thinkSec))
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 12).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                    }

                    Image(systemName: "chevron.right")
                        .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                        .rotationEffect(.degrees(node.isThinkingExpanded ? 90 : 0))
                }
                .padding(.vertical, 3)
                .contentShape(Rectangle())
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)

            if node.isThinkingExpanded {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 0) {
                    HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 12) {
                        Rectangle()
                            .fill(tokens.strongBorder.opacity(0.65))
                            .frame(width: 1.5)
                            .contentShape(Rectangle().inset(by: -6))
                            .onTapGesture { toggleThinkingExpanded() }
                            .help("Collapse thought process")

                        StreamSafeMarkdownView(
                            rawMarkdown: node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty ? "Reasoning…" : node.thinkingTrace,
                            isStreaming: isActivelyThinking,
                            effectiveFontSize: relFont,
                            tokens: tokens,
                            typo: typo,
                            useMonospaceBody: false,
                            overrideTextColor: tokens.thinkingText,
                            onOpenCodeInInspector: { _, _ in
                                store.userManuallyClosedRightPaneInWideMode = false
                                store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen = true
                            }
                        )
                        .equatable()
                        .padding(.vertical, 10)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                        .background(tokens.thinkingSurface)
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous))
                    }
                    .padding(.leading, 5)
                    .padding(.top, 6)

                    // Natural bottom collapse boundary right where the final answer begins
                    HStack(spacing: 10) {
                        Button(action: toggleThinkingExpanded) {
                            HStack(spacing: 5) {
                                Image(systemName: "chevron.up")
                                    .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .semibold))
                                Text("Collapse thought")
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                            }
                            .foregroundColor(isBottomCollapseHovered ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.mutedText)
                            .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                            .padding(.vertical, 3.5)
                            .background(
                                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous)
                                    .fill(isBottomCollapseHovered ? tokens.hoverSurface : Color.clear)
                            )
                            .contentShape(Rectangle())
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        .onHover { hovering in
                            withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.12)) { isBottomCollapseHovered = hovering }
                        }

                        HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)
                    }
                    .padding(.top, 8)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Inline Message Editor
    private var inlineMessageEditor: some View {
        let approxLines = max(5, editMessageDraft.components(separatedBy: .newlines).reduce(0) { $0 + max(1, ($1.count / 72) + 1) })
        let naturalH = CGFloat(approxLines) * (opticalFontSize * 1.55) + 32.0
        let maxStdH: CGFloat = isFullscreenOrWide ? 560.0 : 420.0
        let editorHeight: CGFloat = isEditBoxExpandedFull
            ? min(880.0, max(maxStdH, naturalH))
            : min(maxStdH, max(160.0, naturalH))

        return VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            HStack {
                Text(node.role == .user ? "Edit prompt" : "Edit reply (trim to reclaim KV cache)")
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                Spacer()
                Button {
                    withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.14)) { isEditBoxExpandedFull.toggle() }
                } label: {
                    Text(isEditBoxExpandedFull ? "Compact height" : "Expand height")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }

            MotifStyledTextEditor(text: $editMessageDraft, font: typo.nsFont(size: opticalFontSize), tokens: tokens)
                .frame(height: editorHeight)
                .padding(6)
                .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
                .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.strongBorder, lineWidth: 1))

            HStack(spacing: 12) {
                if node.role == .assistant {
                    if !node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty {
                        Toggle("Clear stored thinking trace", isOn: $clearThinkingOnAssistantEdit)
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    }
                    let savedTok = max(0, max(1, node.content.count / 4) - max(1, editMessageDraft.count / 4))
                    if savedTok > 0 {
                        Text("Saves ~\(savedTok) KV tok")
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.statusOnline)
                    }
                }
                Spacer()
                Button("Cancel") { editingMessageNodeID = nil }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)

                Button(node.role == .user ? "Save & resubmit" : "Save reply") {
                    let updated = editMessageDraft
                    let clearThink = clearThinkingOnAssistantEdit
                    editingMessageNodeID = nil
                    if node.role == .user {
                        store.createEditedMessageBranch(originalNodeID: node.id, updatedText: updated)
                    } else {
                        store.updateAssistantMessageInPlace(nodeID: node.id, updatedText: updated, clearThinking: clearThink)
                    }
                }
                .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                .padding(.horizontal, 12).padding(.vertical, 5.5)
                .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
    }

    // MARK: - Quiet Hover-Revealed Turn Controls & Telemetry
    @ViewBuilder
    private var turnActionAndTelemetryBar: some View {
        let forceVisible = isNodeStreaming
            || confirmingDeleteNodeID == node.id
            || (node.wasStoppedMidStream && isLatestTurn)
            || (node.role == .user && node.childrenIDs.isEmpty && !store.isStreamingGeneration)
        let shouldShowControls = isHovered || forceVisible || (isLatestTurn && node.role == .assistant)

        if node.role == .user {
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                Spacer()

                if confirmingDeleteNodeID == node.id {
                    deleteConfirmationInline
                } else {
                    if siblingMeta.totalCount > 1 { branchPaginationControl }

                    HStack(spacing: 10) {
                        if node.childrenIDs.isEmpty && !store.isStreamingGeneration {
                            Button("Generate reply") { store.regenerateOrAnswerUserNode(nodeID: node.id) }
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        }

                        subtleIconAction(isCopied ? "checkmark" : "doc.on.doc", help: "Copy") { copyNodeText() }
                        subtleIconAction("pencil", help: "Edit & branch") { beginEditing() }
                        subtleIconAction("arrow.triangle.branch", help: "Branch to new chat") { store.branchMessageToNewChat(upToNodeID: node.id) }
                        subtleIconAction("trash", help: "Delete turn") { confirmingDeleteNodeID = node.id }
                    }
                    .opacity(shouldShowControls ? 1.0 : 0.0)
                }
            }
            .frame(height: 22)
        } else {
            HStack(spacing: 12) {
                if isNodeStreaming {
                    let isThinkingPhase = !node.isThinkingComplete && node.content.isEmpty
                    HStack(spacing: 6) {
                        Circle().fill(tokens.primaryText.opacity(0.6)).frame(width: 4.5, height: 4.5)
                        Text(isThinkingPhase
                             ? String(format: "%.1f tok/s · %d thinking tok", store.liveTokensPerSec, max(1, store.liveThinkingTokens))
                             : String(format: "%.1f tok/s · %d tok", store.liveTokensPerSec, max(1, store.liveTotalTokens)))
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                    }
                    Spacer()
                    Button("Stop") { store.stopActiveGeneration() }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                } else if confirmingDeleteNodeID == node.id {
                    deleteConfirmationInline
                    Spacer()
                } else {
                    if siblingMeta.totalCount > 1 { branchPaginationControl }

                    HStack(spacing: 12) {
                        subtleIconAction(isCopied ? "checkmark" : "doc.on.doc", help: "Copy") { copyNodeText() }
                        subtleIconAction("pencil", help: "Edit / trim reply") { beginEditing() }
                        subtleIconAction("arrow.clockwise", help: "Regenerate") {
                            store.regenerateAssistantBranch(forAssistantNodeID: node.id)
                        }
                        subtleIconAction("arrow.triangle.branch", help: "Branch to new chat") {
                            store.branchMessageToNewChat(upToNodeID: node.id)
                        }
                        subtleIconAction("trash", help: "Delete turn") { confirmingDeleteNodeID = node.id }

                        if node.wasStoppedMidStream && isLatestTurn {
                            Button("Continue") { store.continueStoppedGeneration(forAssistantNodeID: node.id) }
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                                .padding(.horizontal, 9).padding(.vertical, 3)
                                .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        }

                        let shortLabel = LocalModelDescriptor.shortenModelName(node.modelUsed.isEmpty ? "Assistant" : node.modelUsed)
                        Menu {
                            Text("Regenerate with model:")
                            ForEach(store.availableModels) { model in
                                Button(model.shortDisplayName) {
                                    store.regenerateAssistantBranch(forAssistantNodeID: node.id, withOverrideModel: model.name)
                                }
                            }
                        } label: {
                            HStack(spacing: 3) {
                                Text(shortLabel)
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                                    .lineLimit(1)
                                Image(systemName: "chevron.down")
                                    .font(.system(size: 8, weight: .medium))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                            }
                        }
                        .menuStyle(.borderlessButton)
                        .fixedSize()
                    }
                    .opacity(shouldShowControls ? (isHovered ? 1.0 : 0.72) : 0.0)

                    Spacer()

                    let footer = buildTelemetryFooter()
                    Text(footer.text)
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11).monospacedDigit())
                        .foregroundColor(footer.isWarning ? tokens.statusWarning : tokens.mutedText)
                        .opacity(shouldShowControls ? (isHovered ? 0.95 : 0.65) : 0.0)
                }
            }
            .frame(height: 22)
        }
    }

    private var deleteConfirmationInline: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 8) {
            Text("Delete & free ~\(node.estimatedTokenFootprint) tok?")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
            Button("Delete") {
                confirmingDeleteNodeID = nil
                store.deleteSingleMessageNode(node.id)
            }
            .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
            .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
            .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 3)
            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            Button("Cancel") { confirmingDeleteNodeID = nil }
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
        }
    }

    private var branchPaginationControl: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 2) {
            Button { store.switchBranchVersion(forNodeID: node.id, delta: -1) } label: {
                Image(systemName: "chevron.left").font(.system(size: 9.5, weight: .medium)).frame(width: 18, height: 18)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            .disabled(siblingMeta.currentIndex == 0)
            .opacity(siblingMeta.currentIndex == 0 ? 0.35 : 1.0)

            Text("\(siblingMeta.currentIndex + 1)/\(siblingMeta.totalCount)")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11).monospacedDigit())
                .padding(.horizontal, 2)

            Button { store.switchBranchVersion(forNodeID: node.id, delta: 1) } label: {
                Image(systemName: "chevron.right").font(.system(size: 9.5, weight: .medium)).frame(width: 18, height: 18)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            .disabled(siblingMeta.currentIndex >= siblingMeta.totalCount - 1)
            .opacity(siblingMeta.currentIndex >= siblingMeta.totalCount - 1 ? 0.35 : 1.0)
        }
        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
    }

    private func subtleIconAction(_ symbol: String, help: String, action: @escaping () -> Void) -> some View {
        Button(action: action) {
            Image(systemName: symbol)
                .font(.system(size: 11.5, weight: .regular))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                .frame(width: 20, height: 20)
                .contentShape(Rectangle())
        }
        .buttonStyle(.plain)
        .help(help)
    }

    private func copyNodeText() {
        NSPasteboard.general.clearContents()
        NSPasteboard.general.setString(node.content.isEmpty ? node.thinkingTrace : node.content, forType: .string)
        isCopied = true
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 1.4) { isCopied = false }
    }

    private func beginEditing() {
        editMessageDraft = node.content
        clearThinkingOnAssistantEdit = false
        isEditBoxExpandedFull = false
        editingMessageNodeID = node.id
    }

    private func buildTelemetryFooter() -> (text: String, isWarning: Bool) {
        let answerTok = node.content.isEmpty ? 0 : max(node.estimatedTokenFootprint, node.metrics?.totalTokens ?? 0)
        let m = node.metrics
        let tps = m?.tokensPerSecond ?? 0.0
        let cached = m?.promptEvalCachedTokens ?? 0
        let promptTok = m?.promptEvalTokens ?? 0
        let doneReason = m?.doneReason ?? ""

        var parts: [String] = []
        if tps > 0 {
            parts.append(String(format: "%.1f tok/s", tps))
            parts.append("\(answerTok) tok")
        } else {
            parts.append("~\(answerTok) tok")
        }
        if promptTok > 0 && cached > 0 {
            let pct = Int((Double(cached) / Double(promptTok)) * 100.0)
            parts.append("KV \(pct)%")
        }
        if doneReason == "length" { parts.append("Truncated") }
        return (parts.joined(separator: " · "), doneReason == "length")
    }
}
EOF
)
