import SwiftUI
import AppKit

public struct ProgressiveComposerBar: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public var maxBarWidth: CGFloat = 820.0
    public var activeFontSize: CGFloat = 14.5
    public var isWideOrFullscreen: Bool = false

    @State private var isComposerFocused: Bool = false
    @State private var compactEditorHeight: CGFloat = 32.0
    @State private var wrappedLineCount: Int = 1

    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }
    private var isSendEnabled: Bool { store.isStreamingGeneration || store.sendDisabledReason == nil }
    private var matchingPersonas: [SlashPersona] { store.matchedSlashPersonas }
    private var effectiveEditorHeight: CGFloat { store.isComposerCanvasExpanded ? 230.0 : compactEditorHeight }
    private var isMultiLine: Bool { wrappedLineCount > 1 || store.isComposerCanvasExpanded }

    public var body: some View {
        let haloOpacity = store.composerHaloOpacity
        let isPulsingHalo = haloOpacity > 0.01
        let pillRadius: CGFloat = isMultiLine ? 20.0 : 24.0

        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
            if !matchingPersonas.isEmpty {
                SlashPersonaCommandPaletteCard(
                    personas: matchingPersonas,
                    highlightedIndex: min(max(0, store.slashMenuHighlightedIndex), max(0, matchingPersonas.count - 1)),
                    tokens: tokens,
                    typo: typo,
                    onSelect: { store.applySlashPersona($0) },
                    onManagePersonas: {
                        store.isSlashMenuDismissedForCurrentToken = true
                        store.settingsRequestedTab = "Slash Personas (/)"
                        store.isSettingsModalPresented = true
                    },
                    onDismiss: { store.isSlashMenuDismissedForCurrentToken = true }
                )
                .frame(maxWidth: min(maxBarWidth, 480))
                .transition(.opacity.combined(with: .move(edge: .bottom)))
            }

            // Pending attachments & active persona pill float cleanly above the single-line pill
            if !store.pendingAttachments.isEmpty || store.activeSession?.activePersonaTrigger != nil {
                ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                    HStack(spacing: 6) {
                        if let activeTrigger = store.activeSession?.activePersonaTrigger {
                            HStack(spacing: 5) {
                                Text(activeTrigger)
                                    .font(typo.mono(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                Button {
                                    if let idx = store.sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == store.selectedSessionID }) {
                                        store.sessions[idx].activePersonaTrigger = nil
                                        store.saveAllState()
                                    }
                                } label: {
                                    Image(systemName: "xmark")
                                        .font(.system(size: 8.5, weight: .semibold))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            }
                            .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 4.5)
                            .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                            .clipShape(Capsule())
                            .overlay(Capsule().strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                        }

                        ForEach(store.pendingAttachments) { chip in
                            HStack(spacing: 6) {
                                Image(systemName: chip.kind == .downscaledVisionImage ? "photo" : "doc.text")
                                    .font(.system(size: 10))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                Text("\(chip.title) · \(chip.formattedByteSize)")
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .regular).monospacedDigit())
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                Button {
                                    store.pendingAttachments.removeAll { $0.id == chip.id }
                                } label: {
                                    Image(systemName: "xmark")
                                        .font(.system(size: 8.5, weight: .semibold))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            }
                            .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 4.5)
                            .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                            .clipShape(Capsule())
                            .overlay(Capsule().strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal, 4)
                }
            }

            // Sleek Single-Line Pill Composer (All Controls Aligned on One Row; Expands Smoothly on Long Input)
            HStack(alignment: .bottom, spacing: 8) {
                Button(action: attachLocalFileOrVisionAsset) {
                    ZStack {
                        Circle()
                            .fill(Color.clear)
                            .frame(width: 32, height: 32)
                        Image(systemName: "plus")
                            .font(.system(size: 14, weight: .regular))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    }
                    .overlay(Circle().strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                    .contentShape(Circle())
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                .help("Attach code, text file, or image (or ⌘V to paste screenshot)")

                IMESafeComposerTextView(
                    text: Binding(
                        get: { store.composerDraft },
                        set: { store.handleComposerTextChange($0) }
                    ),
                    isFocused: $isComposerFocused,
                    compactHeight: $compactEditorHeight,
                    lineCount: $wrappedLineCount,
                    focusTrigger: store.composerFocusTrigger,
                    placeholder: "Send a message…",
                    tokens: tokens,
                    typo: typo,
                    fontSize: activeFontSize,
                    isSlashMenuVisible: !matchingPersonas.isEmpty,
                    onSend: {
                        if !store.isStreamingGeneration && store.sendDisabledReason == nil {
                            store.sendActivePrompt()
                        }
                    },
                    onSlashMoveUp: { store.moveSlashSelection(delta: -1) },
                    onSlashMoveDown: { store.moveSlashSelection(delta: 1) },
                    onSlashConfirm: { store.applyHighlightedSlashPersona() },
                    onSlashDismiss: { store.isSlashMenuDismissedForCurrentToken = true },
                    onLargePaste: { pasted in store.handleLargePasteAsChip(pasted) },
                    onImagePaste: { imgData in store.handleClipboardImagePaste(imgData) }
                )
                .frame(height: effectiveEditorHeight)

                HStack(spacing: 6) {
                    if isMultiLine {
                        Button {
                            withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.16)) { store.isComposerCanvasExpanded.toggle() }
                        } label: {
                            Image(systemName: store.isComposerCanvasExpanded ? "arrow.down.right.and.arrow.up.left" : "arrow.up.left.and.arrow.down.right")
                                .font(.system(size: 10.5, weight: .regular))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                                .frame(width: 26, height: 32)
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        .help(store.isComposerCanvasExpanded ? "Restore compact pill" : "Expand prompt area")
                    }

                    Button { store.isParameterPopoverPresented.toggle() } label: {
                        HStack(spacing: 5) {
                            Image(systemName: "slider.horizontal.3")
                                .font(.system(size: 11.5, weight: .regular))
                            Image(systemName: store.isParameterPopoverPresented ? "chevron.down" : "chevron.up")
                                .font(.system(size: 8.5, weight: .semibold))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(store.isParameterPopoverPresented ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.secondaryText)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 11)
                        .frame(height: 32)
                        .background(Capsule().fill(store.isParameterPopoverPresented ? tokens.codeSurface : Color.clear))
                        .overlay(Capsule().strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                        .contentShape(Capsule())
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .help("Parameters, Thinking Mode & Presets (⌘⇧P)")
                    .popover(isPresented: $store.isParameterPopoverPresented, arrowEdge: .top) {
                        ParameterInspectorPopover(store: store, tokens: tokens)
                    }

                    Button {
                        if store.isStreamingGeneration { store.stopActiveGeneration() }
                        else { store.sendActivePrompt() }
                    } label: {
                        ZStack {
                            Circle()
                                .fill(isSendEnabled ? tokens.sendButtonActive : tokens.sendButtonDisabled)
                                .frame(width: 32, height: 32)
                            if store.isStreamingGeneration {
                                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 2.5, style: .continuous)
                                    .fill(tokens.sendButtonForeground)
                                    .frame(width: 9, height: 9)
                            } else {
                                Image(systemName: "arrow.up")
                                    .font(.system(size: 13, weight: .semibold))
                                    .foregroundColor(isSendEnabled ? tokens.sendButtonForeground : tokens.mutedText)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .disabled(!isSendEnabled)
                    .help(store.isStreamingGeneration ? "Stop generation (⌘\(store.shortcuts.stopGenerationKey))" : (store.sendDisabledReason ?? "Send message"))
                }
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 8)
            .padding(.vertical, 7)
            .background(tokens.composerSurface)
            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: pillRadius, style: .continuous))
            .overlay(
                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: pillRadius, style: .continuous)
                    .strokeBorder(
                        isPulsingHalo
                            ? tokens.statusOnline.opacity(0.80 * haloOpacity)
                            : (isComposerFocused ? tokens.strongBorder : tokens.hairline),
                        lineWidth: isPulsingHalo ? 1.5 : 1.0
                    )
            )
            .shadow(
                color: isPulsingHalo
                    ? tokens.statusOnline.opacity(0.22 * haloOpacity)
                    : Color.black.opacity(tokens.isEffectivelyDark ? 0.28 : (isComposerFocused ? 0.065 : 0.042)),
                radius: isComposerFocused ? 16 : 12,
                x: 0,
                y: 4
            )
            .animation(.easeOut(duration: 0.14), value: pillRadius)
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: maxBarWidth)
    }

    private func attachLocalFileOrVisionAsset() {
        let panel = NSOpenPanel()
        panel.title = "Attach File or Image"
        panel.allowsMultipleSelection = true
        panel.canChooseDirectories = false
        if panel.runModal() == .OK { store.attachFilesFromURLs(panel.urls) }
    }
}

public struct SlashPersonaCommandPaletteCard: View {
    public let personas: [SlashPersona]
    public let highlightedIndex: Int
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public let typo: TypographyConfiguration
    public let onSelect: (SlashPersona) -> Void
    public let onManagePersonas: () -> Void
    public let onDismiss: () -> Void

    public var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 0) {
            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                Text("Slash personas")
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                Spacer()
                Text("↑↓ · ↵ apply · esc")
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                Button(action: onDismiss) {
                    Image(systemName: "xmark").font(.system(size: 9.5, weight: .medium)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 12).frame(height: 30)

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: true) {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                    ForEach(Array(personas.enumerated()), id: \.element.id) { idx, persona in
                        let isHighlighted = idx == highlightedIndex
                        Button { onSelect(persona) } label: {
                            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                                Text(persona.trigger)
                                    .font(typo.mono(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                                    .foregroundColor(isHighlighted ? tokens.accentForeground : tokens.primaryText)
                                    .padding(.horizontal, 7).padding(.vertical, 3)
                                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(isHighlighted ? tokens.accentAction : tokens.codeSurface))
                                    .frame(width: 92, alignment: .leading)
                                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                                    Text(persona.title).font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: .medium)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText).lineLimit(1)
                                    Text(persona.systemPrompt).font(typo.sans(size: 11)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText).lineLimit(1)
                                }
                                Spacer(minLength: 4)
                                if isHighlighted {
                                    Text("↵").font(typo.mono(size: 11, weight: .medium)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                }
                            }
                            .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 6)
                            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).fill(isHighlighted ? tokens.selectionPill : Color.clear))
                            .contentShape(Rectangle())
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }
                }
                .padding(6)
            }
            .frame(maxHeight: 210)

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            HStack {
                Button(action: onManagePersonas) {
                    Text("Manage personas in Settings…")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .regular))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                Spacer()
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 12).frame(height: 28)
        }
        .background(tokens.composerSurface)
        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
        .shadow(color: Color.black.opacity(tokens.isEffectivelyDark ? 0.30 : 0.08), radius: 14, y: 4)
    }
}

public struct ModelPickerPopoverView: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }

    public var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            HStack {
                Text("Local models")
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                Spacer()
                Button("Refresh") {
                    Task {
                        await store.refreshLocalEngineModels()
                        await store.pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
                    }
                }
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            if store.availableModels.isEmpty {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                    Text("No models found in Ollama.")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    Button("Open Settings to pull or select external drive…") {
                        store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented = false
                        store.settingsRequestedTab = "Models & Storage"
                        store.isSettingsModalPresented = true
                    }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
                .padding(.vertical, 6)
            } else {
                ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: false) {
                    VStack(spacing: 3) {
                        ForEach(store.availableModels) { model in
                            let isSelected = model.name == store.activeModelDescriptor?.name
                            let isLoaded = model.residency == .activeInUnifiedRAM

                            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                                Circle()
                                    .fill(isLoaded ? tokens.statusOnline : tokens.mutedText.opacity(0.45))
                                    .frame(width: 6, height: 6)

                                Button {
                                    let isMidChat = !(store.activeSession?.activeBranchThread.isEmpty ?? true)
                                    if isMidChat { store.selectAndLoadModel(model) } else { store.selectModelOnly(model) }
                                    store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented = false
                                } label: {
                                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                                        HStack(spacing: 5) {
                                            Text(model.shortDisplayName)
                                                .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: isSelected ? .semibold : .regular))
                                                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                                .lineLimit(1)
                                            if model.supportsVision {
                                                Text("Vision")
                                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 9.5, weight: .medium))
                                                    .padding(.horizontal, 5).padding(.vertical, 1)
                                                    .background(tokens.codeSurface)
                                                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 4, style: .continuous))
                                                    .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 4, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                            }
                                            if model.supportsTools {
                                                Text("Tools")
                                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 9.5, weight: .medium))
                                                    .padding(.horizontal, 5).padding(.vertical, 1)
                                                    .background(tokens.codeSurface)
                                                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 4, style: .continuous))
                                                    .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 4, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                            }
                                        }
                                        let details = [
                                            model.parameterSize,
                                            model.quantizationLevel,
                                            model.formattedSize,
                                            model.loadedContextLength.map { "\($0 / 1024)k loaded" } ?? model.thinkingProfile.kind.rawValue
                                        ].filter { !$0.isEmpty }
                                        Text(details.joined(separator: " · "))
                                            .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5).monospacedDigit())
                                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                    }
                                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                                    .contentShape(Rectangle())
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)

                                if isLoaded {
                                    Button("Unload") { Task { await store.unloadModelFromMemory(model) } }
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .regular))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                        .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 4)
                                        .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                } else {
                                    Button("Load") {
                                        store.selectAndLoadModel(model)
                                        store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented = false
                                    }
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                                    .padding(.horizontal, 9).padding(.vertical, 4)
                                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                }
                            }
                            .padding(.horizontal, 9).padding(.vertical, 7)
                            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).fill(isSelected ? tokens.selectionPill : Color.clear))
                        }
                    }
                }
                .frame(maxHeight: 330)
            }
        }
        .padding(14)
        .frame(width: 392)
        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
    }
}

// MARK: - Complete 18-Control CLI Parameter Inspector Popover (Refined Minimal Styling)
public struct ParameterInspectorPopover: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    @State private var isAdvancedExpanded: Bool = false

    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }
    private let presetGridColumns = [GridItem(.adaptive(minimum: 108, maximum: 185), spacing: 6)]

    private var activeSessionBinding: Binding<ConversationSession>? {
        guard let id = store.selectedSessionID,
              let idx = store.sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return nil }
        return $store.sessions[idx]
    }

    public var body: some View {
        ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: true) {
            if let session = activeSessionBinding {
                let model = store.activeModelDescriptor
                let profile = model?.thinkingProfile ?? ModelThinkingProfile()

                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                    // Tight-RAM Survival Optimizer Card
                    HStack(spacing: 10) {
                        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                            Text("Tight-RAM optimizer")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            Text("Q4_0 KV cache, Flash Attention, Parallel=1, 4k ctx, 128 batch & safe GPU layers.")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        }
                        Spacer()
                        Button(session.wrappedValue.parameters.tightRAMModeEnabled ? "Re-apply" : "Optimize") {
                            store.applyTightRAMOptimization()
                        }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 5)
                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }
                    .padding(11)
                    .background(tokens.codeSurface)
                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10, style: .continuous))
                    .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))

                    // Presets Grid
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                        HStack {
                            Text("Presets (\(store.presets.count))")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            Spacer()
                            Button("+ Save preset…") {
                                store.isParameterPopoverPresented = false
                                store.isNewPresetSheetPresented = true
                            }
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        }

                        LazyVGrid(columns: presetGridColumns, alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                            ForEach(store.presets) { preset in
                                let isMatch = session.wrappedValue.parameters == preset.parameters
                                Button {
                                    session.wrappedValue.parameters = preset.parameters
                                    session.wrappedValue.systemPrompt = preset.systemPrompt
                                    store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                                    if store.supervisor.kvCacheTypeEnv != preset.parameters.kvCacheQuant {
                                        store.applyKVCacheQuantizationLive(preset.parameters.kvCacheQuant)
                                    } else {
                                        store.recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
                                    }
                                } label: {
                                    Text(preset.name)
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: isMatch ? .semibold : .regular))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                        .lineLimit(1)
                                        .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 6)
                                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous).fill(isMatch ? tokens.selectionPill : tokens.codeSurface))
                                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(isMatch ? tokens.strongBorder : tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                .help("\(preset.summary) — Right-click to update or delete")
                                .contextMenu {
                                    Button("Update “\(preset.name)” with Current Settings") { store.updatePresetWithCurrentSettings(preset.id) }
                                    Divider()
                                    Button("Delete Preset", role: .destructive) { store.deletePreset(preset.id) }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                    // Introspected Thinking Mode
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                        HStack(spacing: 6) {
                            Text("Thinking mode").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            ParameterInfoButton(
                                title: "Introspected Thinking Profile",
                                explanation: "Motif queries POST /api/show for authoritative thinking levels and echoes exact server strings. Tokens: \(profile.detectedTemplateTokens)",
                                tokens: tokens,
                                typo: typo
                            )
                            Spacer()
                            Text(profile.kind.rawValue).font(typo.sans(size: 10.5, weight: .medium)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        }
                        Text("Template tokens: \(profile.detectedTemplateTokens)")
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText).lineLimit(1)

                        if profile.kind != .unsupported || session.wrappedValue.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported {
                            let opts: [ThinkingModeOption] = session.wrappedValue.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported
                                ? [.off, .on, .level("low"), .level("medium"), .level("high")]
                                : profile.validOptions
                            Picker("", selection: session.parameters.thinkingMode) {
                                ForEach(opts) { mode in Text(mode.displayLabel).tag(mode) }
                            }
                            .pickerStyle(.segmented).labelsHidden()
                            .onChange(of: session.wrappedValue.parameters.thinkingMode) { _, _ in store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                        } else {
                            Toggle("Force thinking override for custom GGUF", isOn: session.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported)
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        }
                    }

                    HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                    // Context Window & KV Cache Quantization
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                        HStack(spacing: 6) {
                            Text("Context window (num_ctx)").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            ParameterInfoButton(
                                title: "Context Window (num_ctx)",
                                explanation: "Changing num_ctx triggers a one-time reload on the next turn. Model max: \(model?.maxContextLength ?? 32768) tokens.",
                                tokens: tokens,
                                typo: typo
                            )
                            Spacer()
                            Text("\(session.wrappedValue.parameters.numCtx) tok · ~\(String(format: "%.2f GB", store.memorySnapshot.kvCacheGB))")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit()).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        }
                        let tiers = [2048, 4096, 8192, 16384, 32768, 65536].filter { $0 <= max(4096, model?.maxContextLength ?? 32768) }
                        Picker("", selection: session.parameters.numCtx) {
                            ForEach(tiers, id: \.self) { t in Text("\(t / 1024)k").tag(t) }
                        }
                        .pickerStyle(.segmented).labelsHidden()
                        .onChange(of: session.wrappedValue.parameters.numCtx) { _, _ in
                            store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                            store.recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
                        }

                        HStack(spacing: 6) {
                            Text("KV cache precision").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            ParameterInfoButton(
                                title: "KV Cache Quantization (OLLAMA_KV_CACHE_TYPE)",
                                explanation: "Requires Flash Attention. In Managed mode Motif restarts its daemon automatically; in External mode copy the launchctl command in Settings.",
                                tokens: tokens,
                                typo: typo
                            )
                        }
                        .padding(.top, 2)

                        Picker("", selection: session.parameters.kvCacheQuant) {
                            ForEach(KVCacheQuantization.allCases) { q in Text(q.displayTitle).tag(q) }
                        }
                        .pickerStyle(.menu).labelsHidden()
                        .onChange(of: session.wrappedValue.parameters.kvCacheQuant) { _, newQuant in
                            store.applyKVCacheQuantizationLive(newQuant)
                        }
                    }

                    HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                    // Core Sampling Overrides
                    HStack {
                        Text("Sampling overrides").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        Spacer()
                        Button("Reset to model default") {
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.temperature = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.topP = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.topK = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.minP = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.repeatPenalty = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.typicalP = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.repeatLastN = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.presencePenalty = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.frequencyPenalty = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.mirostat = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.mirostatTau = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.mirostatEta = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.numPredict = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.numKeep = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.seed = nil
                            session.wrappedValue.parameters.penalizeNewline = nil
                            store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                        }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }

                    OptionalParameterSliderRow(title: "Temperature", explanation: "Lower (0.1–0.3) = deterministic code/facts; higher (0.8–1.2) = creative.", value: session.parameters.temperature, fallbackValue: 0.70, range: 0.0...2.0, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                    OptionalParameterSliderRow(title: "Top-P", explanation: "Nucleus sampling cumulative probability.", value: session.parameters.topP, fallbackValue: 0.90, range: 0.05...1.0, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                    OptionalParameterSliderRow(title: "Min-P", explanation: "Minimum probability relative to top token.", value: session.parameters.minP, fallbackValue: 0.05, range: 0.0...0.5, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                    OptionalParameterSliderRow(title: "Repeat Penalty", explanation: "Penalizes repetition (1.0 off; 1.10 standard).", value: session.parameters.repeatPenalty, fallbackValue: 1.10, range: 0.8...1.6, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }

                    HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                    // All 14 Advanced Ollama CLI & Hardware Controls
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                        Button {
                            withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.14)) { isAdvancedExpanded.toggle() }
                        } label: {
                            HStack {
                                Image(systemName: "chevron.right")
                                    .font(.system(size: 9.5, weight: .medium))
                                    .rotationEffect(.degrees(isAdvancedExpanded ? 90 : 0))
                                Text("Advanced Ollama CLI & hardware").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                                Spacer()

                                ParameterIntStepperRow(title: "Metal GPU Layers (-1 = Auto)", explanation: "Transformer layers offloaded to GPU (-1 = Auto fit; model has \(model?.numLayers ?? 32) layers).", value: session.parameters.numGpu, defaultValue: -1, range: -1...128, step: 1, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                                ParameterIntStepperRow(title: "CPU Threads (0 = Auto Perf)", explanation: "0 uses fast Apple Silicon Performance cores.", value: session.parameters.numThread, defaultValue: 0, range: 0...32, step: 1, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }

                                OptionalParameterSliderRow(title: "Typical-P (typical_p)", explanation: "Locally typical sampling mass (1.0 disables).", value: session.parameters.typicalP, fallbackValue: 1.00, range: 0.1...1.0, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
