import SwiftUI
import AppKit
import UniformTypeIdentifiers

// MARK: - Reusable Named Item Creation Sheet (Workspaces & Parameter Presets)
public struct CreateNamedItemSheet: View {
    public let heading: String
    public let placeholder: String
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public let typo: TypographyConfiguration
    public let onCommit: (String) -> Void
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
    @State private var nameDraft: String = ""

    public var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
            Text(heading)
                .font(typo.sans(size: 14, weight: .semibold))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)

            TextField(placeholder, text: $nameDraft)
                .textFieldStyle(.plain)
                .font(typo.sans(size: 13))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                .padding(9)
                .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous))
                .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                .onSubmit { commitAndClose() }

            HStack {
                Spacer()
                Button("Cancel") { dismiss() }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)

                Button("Create") { commitAndClose() }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 12).padding(.vertical, 5)
                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .disabled(nameDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty)
            }
        }
        .padding(20)
        .frame(width: 350)
        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
    }

    private func commitAndClose() {
        let trimmed = nameDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !trimmed.isEmpty else { return }
        onCommit(trimmed)
        dismiss()
    }
}

// MARK: - Code Artifact Inspector Pane (Save File, Open in External Editor, Syntax-Highlighted MotifCodeBlockView)
public struct RightUtilityArtifactPane: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    public let tokens: MotifTokens

    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }
    private var artifacts: [WorkspaceArtifact] { store.activeSession?.artifacts ?? [] }

    private var currentArtifact: WorkspaceArtifact? {
        if let id = store.selectedArtifactID, let match = artifacts.first(where: { $0.id == id }) {
            return match
        }
        return artifacts.last
    }

    public var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 0) {
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                Text("Inspector")
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)

                Spacer()

                if let artifact = currentArtifact {
                    Button("Save File…") { saveArtifactToDisk(artifact) }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        .help("Save snippet to disk")

                    Button("Open in Editor") { openArtifactInExternalEditor(artifact) }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        .help("Open snippet in your default code editor")
                }

                QuietIconButton(systemName: "xmark", tokens: tokens, size: 11.5, helpText: "Close Inspector (⌘\(store.shortcuts.toggleInspectorKey))") { store.userManuallyClosedRightPaneInWideMode = true; store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen = false }
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 16)
            .frame(height: 44)

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            if let artifact = currentArtifact {
                ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                    HStack(spacing: 6) {
                        ForEach(artifacts) { item in
                            let isSelected = item.id == artifact.id
                            Button {
                                store.selectedArtifactID = item.id
                            } label: {
                                Text(item.title)
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: isSelected ? .semibold : .regular))
                                    .foregroundColor(isSelected ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.secondaryText)
                                    .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 4)
                                    .background(
                                        RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous)
                                            .fill(isSelected ? tokens.selectionPill : Color.clear)
                                    )
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal, 14)
                    .padding(.vertical, 8)
                }

                HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: true) {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
                        MotifCodeBlockView(
                            language: artifact.language,
                            code: artifact.content,
                            tokens: tokens,
                            typo: typo,
                            onOpenInArtifactPane: {}
                        )
                    }
                    .padding(14)
                    .background(OverlayScrollConfigurator(isDark: tokens.isEffectivelyDark).frame(width: 1, height: 1))
                }
            } else {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                    Text("No code snippets in this chat yet.")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    Text("Fenced code blocks generated during conversation appear here for quick editing, saving, and external editor handoff.")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    Spacer()
                }
                .padding(16)
            }
        }
        .background(tokens.sidebarBackground)
    }

    private func sanitizedExtension(for language: String) -> String {
        let lower = language.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).lowercased()
        switch lower {
        case "swift": return "swift"
        case "python", "py": return "py"
        case "javascript", "js": return "js"
        case "typescript", "ts": return "ts"
        case "rust", "rs": return "rs"
        case "bash", "sh", "zsh", "shell": return "sh"
        case "json": return "json"
        case "yaml", "yml": return "yml"
        case "html": return "html"
        case "css": return "css"
        case "sql": return "sql"
        case "markdown", "md": return "md"
        default: return lower.isEmpty ? "txt" : lower
        }
    }

    private func saveArtifactToDisk(_ artifact: WorkspaceArtifact) {
        let panel = NSSavePanel()
        panel.title = "Save Code Snippet"
        let ext = sanitizedExtension(for: artifact.language)
        panel.nameFieldStringValue = "\(artifact.title.replacingOccurrences(of: " ", with: "_").replacingOccurrences(of: "#", with: "")).\(ext)"
        if panel.runModal() == .OK, let url = panel.url {
            try? artifact.content.write(to: url, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
        }
    }

    private func openArtifactInExternalEditor(_ artifact: WorkspaceArtifact) {
        let ext = sanitizedExtension(for: artifact.language)
        let safeTitle = artifact.title.replacingOccurrences(of: " ", with: "_").replacingOccurrences(of: "#", with: "")
        let tempURL = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("Motif_\(safeTitle).\(ext)")
        try? artifact.content.write(to: tempURL, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
        NSWorkspace.shared.open(tempURL)
    }
}

// MARK: - Complete 6-Tab Workstation Settings Modal
public struct WorkstationSettingsModal: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss

    public enum SettingsCategory: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable {
        case appearance = "Appearance & Fonts"
        case personas = "Slash Personas (/)"
        case modelsAndDrives = "Models & Storage"
        case engineSupervisor = "Local Engine & RAM"
        case shortcuts = "Keyboard Shortcuts"
        case about = "About Motif"

        public var id: String { rawValue }
    }

    @State private var selectedCategory: SettingsCategory = .appearance
    @State private var pullModelInput: String = "qwen3:8b"

    // Click-to-edit & multi-line Slash Persona composer state
    @State private var editingPersonaID: UUID? = nil
    @State private var personaTriggerDraft: String = "/"
    @State private var personaTitleDraft: String = ""
    @State private var personaPromptDraft: String = ""

    // Engine helper feedback states
    @State private var copiedLaunchctlCommand: Bool = false
    @State private var copiedDaemonLogs: Bool = false
    @State private var isConfirmingDaemonTakeover: Bool = false

    private var tokens: MotifTokens {
        MotifTokens(theme: store.activeTheme, customPalettes: store.customPalettes, isWindowActive: true)
    }
    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }

    public var body: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 0) {
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                Text("Settings")
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 13.5, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 16)
                    .padding(.top, 16)
                    .padding(.bottom, 8)

                ForEach(SettingsCategory.allCases) { category in
                    let isSelected = selectedCategory == category
                    Button {
                        selectedCategory = category
                    } label: {
                        HStack {
                            Text(category.rawValue)
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: isSelected ? .semibold : .regular))
                                .foregroundColor(isSelected ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.secondaryText)
                            Spacer()
                        }
                        .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                        .frame(height: 30)
                        .background(
                            RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous)
                                .fill(isSelected ? tokens.selectionPill : Color.clear)
                        )
                        .contentShape(Rectangle())
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                }

                Spacer()

                Button("Done") { dismiss() }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 16).padding(.vertical, 6)
                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .padding(16)
            }
            .frame(width: 205)
            .background(tokens.sidebarBackground)

            HairlineDivider(axis: .vertical, tokens: tokens)

            ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: true) {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 18) {
                    switch selectedCategory {
                    case .appearance: appearanceSection
                    case .personas: personasSection
                    case .modelsAndDrives: modelsSection
                    case .engineSupervisor: engineSection
                    case .shortcuts: shortcutsSection
                    case .about: aboutSection
                    }
                }
                .padding(24)
                .background(OverlayScrollConfigurator(isDark: tokens.isEffectivelyDark).frame(width: 1, height: 1))
            }
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)
            .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
        }
        .frame(width: 780, height: 570)
        .preferredColorScheme(tokens.isEffectivelyDark ? .dark : .light)
        .onAppear {
            if let req = store.settingsRequestedTab {
                if let exact = SettingsCategory(rawValue: req) {
                    selectedCategory = exact
                } else if req.lowercased().contains("persona") {
                    selectedCategory = .personas
                } else if req.lowercased().contains("model") {
                    selectedCategory = .modelsAndDrives
                } else if req.lowercased().contains("engine") || req.lowercased().contains("daemon") {
                    selectedCategory = .engineSupervisor
                }
                store.settingsRequestedTab = nil
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Universal WCAG-Safe Custom Color Bindings
private var universalCanvasColorBinding: Binding<Color> {
    Binding<Color>(
        get: {
            let u = store.customPalettes.universal
            if u.isCustomCanvasEnabled {
                return Color(red: u.canvasRed, green: u.canvasGreen, blue: u.canvasBlue)
            }
            return tokens.canvasBackground
        },
        set: { newColor in
            if let cg = NSColor(newColor).usingColorSpace(.sRGB) {
                store.customPalettes.universal.isCustomCanvasEnabled = true
                store.customPalettes.universal.canvasRed = Double(cg.redComponent)
                store.customPalettes.universal.canvasGreen = Double(cg.greenComponent)
                store.customPalettes.universal.canvasBlue = Double(cg.blueComponent)
            }
        }
    )
}

private var universalThinkingColorBinding: Binding<Color> {
    Binding<Color>(
        get: {
            let u = store.customPalettes.universal
            if u.isCustomThinkingEnabled {
                return Color(red: u.thinkingRed, green: u.thinkingGreen, blue: u.thinkingBlue)
            }
            return tokens.thinkingSurface
        },
        set: { newColor in
            if let cg = NSColor(newColor).usingColorSpace(.sRGB) {
                store.customPalettes.universal.isCustomThinkingEnabled = true
                store.customPalettes.universal.thinkingRed = Double(cg.redComponent)
                store.customPalettes.universal.thinkingGreen = Double(cg.greenComponent)
                store.customPalettes.universal.thinkingBlue = Double(cg.blueComponent)
            }
        }
    )
}

private func applyQuickCanvasSwatch(r: Double, g: Double, b: Double) {
    store.customPalettes.universal.isCustomCanvasEnabled = true
    store.customPalettes.universal.canvasRed = r
    store.customPalettes.universal.canvasGreen = g
    store.customPalettes.universal.canvasBlue = b
}

// MARK: - 1. Appearance, WCAG-Safe Custom Color Studio & Typography
private var appearanceSection: some View {
    let previewTokens = tokens
    let isCustomActive = tokens.hasCustomCanvas || tokens.hasCustomThinking || tokens.hasCustomAccent
    let contrastVal = previewTokens.computedTextContrastRatio

    return VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 16) {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
            Text("Custom paper & surface studio")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 14, weight: .semibold))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
            Text("Choose any background color. Motif automatically calibrates sidebar, pill composer, bubbles, hairlines, and WCAG-verified text ink so conversation prose stays razor-sharp.")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
        }

        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                let swatches: [(String, Double, Double, Double)] = [
                    ("Paper", 1.00, 1.00, 1.00),
                    ("Linen", 0.98, 0.973, 0.953),
                    ("Sand", 0.957, 0.937, 0.898),
                    ("Mist", 0.945, 0.965, 0.984),
                    ("Sage", 0.941, 0.965, 0.949),
                    ("Graphite", 0.118, 0.125, 0.141),
                    ("Espresso", 0.110, 0.102, 0.094),
                    ("Obsidian", 0.045, 0.048, 0.055)
                ]

                ForEach(swatches, id: \.0) { item in
                    Button {
                        applyQuickCanvasSwatch(r: item.1, g: item.2, b: item.3)
                    } label: {
                        VStack(spacing: 4) {
                            Circle()
                                .fill(Color(red: item.1, green: item.2, blue: item.3))
                                .frame(width: 24, height: 24)
                                .overlay(Circle().strokeBorder(tokens.strongBorder, lineWidth: 1))
                            Text(item.0)
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 9.5))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        }
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
                Spacer()
            }

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            HStack(spacing: 16) {
                ColorPicker("Custom canvas", selection: universalCanvasColorBinding, supportsOpacity: false)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)

                ColorPicker("Thinking tint", selection: universalThinkingColorBinding, supportsOpacity: false)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)

                Spacer()

                Button("Reset custom") {
                    store.resetCustomPalette(for: store.activeTheme)
                }
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                .foregroundColor(isCustomActive ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.mutedText)
                .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 5)
                .background(tokens.composerSurface)
                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                .disabled(!isCustomActive)
            }

            // Live WCAG Contrast & Surface Preview Card
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                HStack {
                    Text("Live readability preview")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(previewTokens.mutedText)
                    Spacer()
                    Text(String(format: "Contrast %.1f:1 · %@", contrastVal, contrastVal >= 7.0 ? "AAA" : "AA"))
                        .font(typo.mono(size: 10, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(previewTokens.statusOnline)
                }

                HStack {
                    Spacer()
                    Text("How does this color feel for long reading?")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                        .foregroundColor(previewTokens.primaryText)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 11).padding(.vertical, 6)
                        .background(previewTokens.userBubbleSurface)
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
                }

                Text("Primary ink and secondary captions stay cleanly balanced against every surface without harsh contrast spikes.")
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12))
                    .foregroundColor(previewTokens.primaryText)
            }
            .padding(12)
            .background(previewTokens.canvasBackground)
            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 9, style: .continuous))
            .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 9, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(previewTokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
        }
        .padding(14)
        .background(tokens.codeSurface)
        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10, style: .continuous))
        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))

        HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

        Text("Base presets")
            .font(typo.sans(size: 13.5, weight: .semibold))
            .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)

        LazyVGrid(columns: [GridItem(.flexible(), spacing: 8), GridItem(.flexible(), spacing: 8)], spacing: 8) {
            ForEach(AppThemeID.allCases) { item in
                let isCurrent = store.activeTheme == item && !tokens.hasCustomCanvas
                let presetTok = MotifTokens(theme: item, customPalettes: CustomStudioPalettes(), isWindowActive: true)

                Button {
                    store.resetCustomPalette(for: item)
                    store.activeTheme = item
                } label: {
                    HStack(spacing: 10) {
                        HStack(spacing: 0) {
                            Rectangle().fill(presetTok.canvasBackground)
                            Rectangle().fill(presetTok.userBubbleSurface)
                            Rectangle().fill(presetTok.primaryText)
                        }
                        .frame(width: 34, height: 20)
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous))
                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))

                        Text(item.rawValue)
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: isCurrent ? .semibold : .regular))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            .lineLimit(1)

                        Spacer()

                        if isCurrent {
                            Image(systemName: "checkmark")
                                .font(.system(size: 10.5, weight: .semibold))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 8)
                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).fill(isCurrent ? tokens.selectionPill : tokens.codeSurface))
                    .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(isCurrent ? tokens.strongBorder : tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }
        }

        HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

        Text("Typography")
            .font(typo.sans(size: 13.5, weight: .semibold))
            .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)

        Picker("Font Family", selection: $store.typography.fontFamily) {
            ForEach(AppFontFamily.allCases) { f in Text(f.rawValue).tag(f) }
        }
        .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5))

        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
            HStack {
                Text("Windowed Text Size").font(typo.sans(size: 12))
                Spacer()
                Text(String(format: "%.1f pt", store.typography.windowedBaseFontSize))
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
            }
            Slider(value: $store.typography.windowedBaseFontSize, in: 11.5...18.5, step: 0.5)
        }

        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
            HStack {
                Text("Fullscreen Text Size").font(typo.sans(size: 12))
                Spacer()
                Text(String(format: "%.1f pt", store.typography.fullscreenBaseFontSize))
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
            }
            Slider(value: $store.typography.fullscreenBaseFontSize, in: 13.0...22.0, step: 0.5)
        }
    }
}

    private var personasSection: some View {
        let draftTokens = personaPromptDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty
            ? 0
            : max(1, personaPromptDraft.count / 4)

        return VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
            HStack {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                    Text("Slash Command System Personas")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 14, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    Text("Type '/' in the chat box to apply a persona. Click any saved persona below to edit it in place.")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                }
                Spacer()
            }

            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                HStack {
                    Text(editingPersonaID == nil ? "Create New Persona" : "Editing \(personaTriggerDraft)")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(editingPersonaID == nil ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.accentAction)
                    Spacer()
                    if editingPersonaID != nil {
                        Button("Cancel Edit") {
                            resetPersonaDraft()
                        }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }
                }

                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                    TextField("/trigger", text: $personaTriggerDraft)
                        .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
                        .font(typo.mono(size: 11.5))
                        .frame(width: 130)
                    TextField("Role title (e.g. Security Auditor)", text: $personaTitleDraft)
                        .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12))
                }

                HStack {
                    Text("System Prompt Instructions:")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    Spacer()
                    Text("~\(draftTokens) KV tok")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                }

                MotifStyledTextEditor(
                    text: $personaPromptDraft,
                    font: typo.nsFont(size: 12.5),
                    tokens: tokens,
                    placeholder: "Write multi-line system instructions here (e.g. role, reasoning style, output format, constraints)…"
                )
                .frame(height: 132)
                .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous))
                .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.strongBorder, lineWidth: 1))

                HStack {
                    Text("Word-wraps automatically and scrolls smoothly for longer prompts.")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                    Spacer()
                    Button(editingPersonaID == nil ? "Add Persona" : "Update Persona") {
                        commitPersonaDraft()
                    }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 13).padding(.vertical, 5.5)
                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .disabled(
                        personaTriggerDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).count < 2
                        || personaTitleDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty
                        || personaPromptDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty
                    )
                }
            }
            .padding(14)
            .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 9, style: .continuous))
            .overlay(
                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 9, style: .continuous)
                    .strokeBorder(editingPersonaID == nil ? tokens.hairline : tokens.accentAction.opacity(0.65), lineWidth: 1)
            )

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                ForEach(store.slashPersonas) { persona in
                    let isEditingThis = editingPersonaID == persona.id
                    HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 10) {
                        Button {
                            editingPersonaID = persona.id
                            personaTriggerDraft = persona.trigger
                            personaTitleDraft = persona.title
                            personaPromptDraft = persona.systemPrompt
                        } label: {
                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                                    Text(persona.trigger)
                                        .font(typo.mono(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                                    Text(persona.title)
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: .semibold))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                    Text("· ~\(max(1, persona.systemPrompt.count / 4)) tok")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5).monospacedDigit())
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                                }
                                Text(persona.systemPrompt)
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                    .lineLimit(3)
                                    .fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)
                            }
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                            .contentShape(Rectangle())
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        .help("Click to edit \(persona.trigger)")

                        Button("Delete") {
                            if editingPersonaID == persona.id { resetPersonaDraft() }
                            store.slashPersonas.removeAll { $0.id == persona.id }
                            store.saveAllState()
                        }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }
                    .padding(10)
                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous).fill(isEditingThis ? tokens.selectionPill : tokens.elevatedSurface.opacity(0.55)))
                    .overlay(
                        RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous)
                            .strokeBorder(isEditingThis ? tokens.accentAction.opacity(0.6) : tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private func resetPersonaDraft() {
        editingPersonaID = nil
        personaTriggerDraft = "/"
        personaTitleDraft = ""
        personaPromptDraft = ""
    }

    private func commitPersonaDraft() {
        var trig = personaTriggerDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        if !trig.hasPrefix("/") { trig = "/" + trig }
        let title = personaTitleDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        let prompt = personaPromptDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard trig.count > 1, !title.isEmpty, !prompt.isEmpty else { return }

        if let editID = editingPersonaID, let idx = store.slashPersonas.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == editID }) {
            store.slashPersonas[idx].trigger = trig
            store.slashPersonas[idx].title = title
            store.slashPersonas[idx].systemPrompt = prompt
        } else {
            store.slashPersonas.append(SlashPersona(trigger: trig, title: title, systemPrompt: prompt))
        }
        store.saveAllState()
        resetPersonaDraft()
    }

    // MARK: - 3. Models & Storage
    private var modelsSection: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
            Text("Pull Model from Ollama Registry")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 14, weight: .semibold))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)

            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                TextField("Model tag (e.g. qwen3:8b, gemma3:12b, deepseek-r1:14b)…", text: $pullModelInput)
                    .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
                Button("Pull") {
                    store.startModelDownload(reference: pullModelInput)
                    pullModelInput = ""
                }
                .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                .padding(.horizontal, 14).padding(.vertical, 5.5)
                .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                .disabled(pullModelInput.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty)
            }

            ForEach(store.downloadTasks) { task in
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 5) {
                    HStack {
                        Text(task.modelReference).font(typo.mono(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        Spacer()
                        Text(task.formattedProgressGB)
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        if !task.isCompleted {
                            Button("Cancel") { store.cancelModelDownload(task.id) }
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                                .foregroundColor(.red)
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        }
                    }
                    ProgressView(value: task.progressFraction)
                        .tint(task.isCompleted ? tokens.statusOnline : tokens.accentAction)
                }
                .padding(10)
                .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous))
            }

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            HStack {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                    Text("External Model Directory (OLLAMA_MODELS)")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    Text(store.driveManager.resolvedModelsPath.isEmpty ? "~/.ollama/models (Default)" : store.driveManager.resolvedModelsPath)
                        .font(typo.mono(size: 10.5))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                }
                Spacer()
                Button("Select Folder…") { store.selectExternalDriveAndReloadModels() }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                    .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 5)
                    .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - 4. Local Engine & RAM (External launchctl Helper, Opt-In Takeover & Live Stderr Log Viewer)
    private var engineSection: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
            HStack {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                    Text("Local Ollama Daemon — \(store.supervisor.daemonMode.rawValue)")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 14, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    Text(store.supervisor.supervisorStatusLog)
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5).monospacedDigit())
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                }
                Spacer()
                Button("Probe / Reconnect") {
                    Task { await store.bootstrapLocalOllamaConnection() }
                }
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 5)
                .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }

            if store.supervisor.daemonMode == .external {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                    Text("Motif is safely attached to your external Ollama server and will never force-kill it automatically. To enable Flash Attention and KV cache quantization on Ollama.app, run this command in Terminal and restart Ollama.app—or let Motif take over as a managed daemon.")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)

                    HStack(spacing: 10) {
                        Button(copiedLaunchctlCommand ? "Copied launchctl Command!" : "Copy launchctl Setup Command") {
                            let cmd = store.supervisor.launchctlSetupCommand(externalModelsPath: store.driveManager.resolvedModelsPath)
                            NSPasteboard.general.clearContents()
                            NSPasteboard.general.setString(cmd, forType: .string)
                            copiedLaunchctlCommand = true
                            DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 2.0) { copiedLaunchctlCommand = false }
                        }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 5)
                        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)

                        if isConfirmingDaemonTakeover {
                            Button("Confirm Takeover (Stops External Ollama)") {
                                isConfirmingDaemonTakeover = false
                                Task {
                                    _ = await store.supervisor.takeOverAndLaunchManagedDaemon(modelsPath: store.driveManager.resolvedModelsPath)
                                    await store.refreshLocalEngineModels()
                                    await store.pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
                                }
                            }
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                            .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 5)
                            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)

                            Button("Cancel") { isConfirmingDaemonTakeover = false }
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText).buttonStyle(.plain)
                        } else {
                            Button("Switch to Motif-Managed Daemon…") {
                                isConfirmingDaemonTakeover = true
                            }
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        }
                    }
                }
                .padding(11)
                .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous))
                .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
            }

            HStack {
                Text("Endpoint URL").font(typo.sans(size: 12)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                Spacer()
                TextField("http://127.0.0.1:11434", text: $store.supervisor.engineEndpoint)
                    .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
                    .frame(width: 260)
            }

            Picker("KV Cache Quantization (OLLAMA_KV_CACHE_TYPE)", selection: $store.supervisor.kvCacheTypeEnv) {
                ForEach(KVCacheQuantization.allCases) { q in Text(q.displayTitle).tag(q) }
            }
            .font(typo.sans(size: 12))

            ParameterIntStepperRow(title: "Parallel Slots (OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL — 1 maximizes context)", explanation: "Each extra parallel slot multiplies KV cache RAM.", value: $store.supervisor.parallelRequestSlots, defaultValue: 1, range: 1...4, step: 1, tokens: tokens, typo: typo)
            ParameterIntStepperRow(title: "Max Loaded Models (OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS)", explanation: "Keep at 1 on 16GB–24GB Macs to avoid double model residency.", value: $store.supervisor.maxLoadedModels, defaultValue: 1, range: 1...4, step: 1, tokens: tokens, typo: typo)

            Toggle("Enable Flash Attention (OLLAMA_FLASH_ATTENTION=1, required for KV quant)", isOn: $store.supervisor.enableFlashAttentionEnv).font(typo.sans(size: 12))
            Toggle("Air-Gapped Local Only (OLLAMA_NO_CLOUD=1)", isOn: $store.supervisor.airGappedNoCloud).font(typo.sans(size: 12))
            Toggle("Bind to local network (0.0.0.0:11434 — off by default for security)", isOn: $store.supervisor.bindToLocalNetwork).font(typo.sans(size: 12))
            Toggle("Release Motif-loaded RAM when window is minimized", isOn: $store.supervisor.autoUnloadOnMinimize).font(typo.sans(size: 12))
            Toggle("Release Motif-loaded RAM when Mac goes to sleep", isOn: $store.supervisor.autoUnloadOnSleep).font(typo.sans(size: 12))
            Toggle("Unload Motif-loaded models on app quit (external mode)", isOn: $store.supervisor.autoUnloadOnQuit).font(typo.sans(size: 12))
            Toggle("Use model thinking when summarizing chat context", isOn: $store.supervisor.useThinkingForSummary).font(typo.sans(size: 12))

            if store.supervisor.isManagedByMotif {
                HStack(spacing: 12) {
                    Button("Apply & Restart Managed Daemon") { store.applyDaemonConfigurationAndRestart() }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 12).padding(.vertical, 6)
                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)

                    Button("Stop Managed Daemon") { store.supervisor.terminateManagedDaemonGracefully() }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
            }

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            HStack {
                Text("Managed daemon logs")
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                Spacer()
                if !store.supervisor.daemonLogLines.isEmpty {
                    Button(copiedDaemonLogs ? "Copied" : "Copy Logs") {
                        NSPasteboard.general.clearContents()
                        NSPasteboard.general.setString(store.supervisor.daemonLogLines.joined(separator: "\n"), forType: .string)
                        copiedDaemonLogs = true
                        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 1.5) { copiedDaemonLogs = false }
                    }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
            }

            ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: true) {
                Text(store.supervisor.daemonLogLines.isEmpty ? "No managed daemon output yet." : store.supervisor.daemonLogLines.joined(separator: "\n"))
                    .font(typo.mono(size: 10.5))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    .textSelection(.enabled)
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                    .padding(8)
            }
            .frame(height: 115)
            .background(tokens.codeSurface)
            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
            .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
        }
    }

    // MARK: - 5. Keyboard Shortcuts
    private var shortcutsSection: some View {

            Text("Customize Key Combinations (⌘ + Key)")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 14, weight: .semibold))

            shortcutEditorRow("New Chat", binding: $store.shortcuts.newChatKey)

            shortcutEditorRow("Stop Generation", binding: $store.shortcuts.stopGenerationKey)

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            Text("Built-In Workstation Shortcuts")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .semibold))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)

            staticShortcutRow("Open Settings", "⌘ ,")
            staticShortcutRow("Focus Message Composer", "⌘ L")
            staticShortcutRow("Open Model Picker", "⌘ ⇧ M")
            staticShortcutRow("Open Session Parameters", "⌘ ⇧ P")
            staticShortcutRow("Copy Latest Assistant Reply", "⌘ ⇧ C")
            staticShortcutRow("Increase / Decrease Text Size", "⌘ =  /  ⌘ -")
            staticShortcutRow("Paste Screenshot / Image Directly", "⌘ V")
        }
    }

    private func shortcutEditorRow(_ title: String, binding: Binding<String>) -> some View {
        HStack {
            Text(title).font(typo.sans(size: 12.5)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
            Spacer()
            Text("⌘ +").font(typo.sans(size: 11.5)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
            TextField("", text: binding)
                .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                .frame(width: 44)
        }
    }

    private func staticShortcutRow(_ title: String, _ keys: String) -> some View {
        HStack {
            Text(title).font(typo.sans(size: 12)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
            Spacer()
            Text(keys).font(typo.mono(size: 11, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
        }
    }

    // MARK: - 6. About Motif
    private var aboutSection: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            Text("Motif")
                .font(typo.wordmark(size: 26))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
            Text("Version 15.0 · Native macOS Workstation for Local Ollama Models")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 12))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
            if !store.supervisor.ollamaVersionString.isEmpty {
                Text("Connected Ollama Engine: v\(store.supervisor.ollamaVersionString) (\(store.supervisor.daemonMode.rawValue))")
                    .font(typo.mono(size: 11))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
            }
            Text("SQLite WAL + FTS5 storage · Apple Silicon Metal Telemetry · Exact KV-Cache Prefix Continuity")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                .padding(.top, 4)
        }
    }
}

rm -f Motif/Sources/Motif/Views/MainWorkstationView.swift
cat << 'EOF' > Motif/Sources/Motif/MotifApp.swift
import SwiftUI
import AppKit

// MARK: - AppKit Window Configurator (Edge-to-Edge Fullscreen, Zero Toolbar Waste & Appearance Sync)
public struct FullSizeWindowConfigurator: NSViewRepresentable {
    public let isDark: Bool

    public init(isDark: Bool) { self.isDark = isDark }

    public func makeNSView(context: Context) -> NSView {
        let view = NSView()
        DispatchQueue.main.async {
            guard let window = view.window else { return }
            window.titleVisibility = .hidden
            window.titlebarAppearsTransparent = true
            window.styleMask.insert(.fullSizeContentView)
            window.toolbar = nil
            window.appearance = NSAppearance(named: isDark ? .darkAqua : .aqua)
        }
        return view
    }

    public func updateNSView(_ nsView: NSView, context: Context) {
        DispatchQueue.main.async {
            guard let window = nsView.window else { return }
            if window.toolbar != nil { window.toolbar = nil }
            if !window.styleMask.contains(.fullSizeContentView) {
                window.styleMask.insert(.fullSizeContentView)
            }
            let targetApp = NSAppearance(named: isDark ? .darkAqua : .aqua)
            if window.appearance != targetApp { window.appearance = targetApp }
        }
    }
}

public struct MotifRootView: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    @Environment(\.controlActiveState) private var controlActiveState

    private var isWindowActive: Bool { controlActiveState != .inactive }
    private var tokens: MotifTokens {
        MotifTokens(theme: store.activeTheme, customPalettes: store.customPalettes, isWindowActive: isWindowActive)
    }
    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }

    public var body: some View {
        GeometryReader { geo in
            HStack(spacing: 0) {
                if store.isSidebarVisible {
                    SidebarNavigationView(store: store, tokens: tokens)
                        .frame(width: store.sidebarWidth)

                    DraggableSplitHandle(
                        width: $store.sidebarWidth,
                        minWidth: MotifTokens.sidebarMinWidth,
                        maxWidth: MotifTokens.sidebarMaxWidth,
                        invertDelta: false,
                        tokens: tokens
                    )
                    .ignoresSafeArea(.container, edges: .top)
                }

                EditorialConversationView(store: store, tokens: tokens)
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)

                if store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen {
                    DraggableSplitHandle(
                        width: $store.inspectorWidth,
                        minWidth: MotifTokens.inspectorMinWidth,
                        maxWidth: MotifTokens.inspectorMaxWidth,
                        invertDelta: true,
                        tokens: tokens
                    )
                    .ignoresSafeArea(.container, edges: .top)

                    RightUtilityArtifactPane(store: store, tokens: tokens)
                        .frame(width: store.inspectorWidth)
                }
            }
            .background(FullSizeWindowConfigurator(isDark: tokens.isEffectivelyDark).frame(width: 0, height: 0))
            .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
            .ignoresSafeArea(.container, edges: .top)
            .onChange(of: geo.size.width) { oldWidth, newWidth in
                let hasArtifacts = !(store.activeSession?.artifacts.isEmpty ?? true)
                if oldWidth < 1540 && newWidth >= 1540 && hasArtifacts && !store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen && !store.userManuallyClosedRightPaneInWideMode {
                    store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen = true
                } else if oldWidth >= 1220 && newWidth < 1220 && store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen {
                    store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen = false
                }
            }
        }
        .sheet(isPresented: $store.isSettingsModalPresented) {
            WorkstationSettingsModal(store: store)
        }
        .sheet(isPresented: $store.isNewWorkspaceSheetPresented) {
            CreateNamedItemSheet(
                heading: "New Workspace",
                placeholder: "Workspace name (e.g. Systems Architecture)",
                tokens: tokens,
                typo: typo
            ) { name in
                store.createWorkspace(name: name)
            }
        }
        .sheet(isPresented: $store.isNewPresetSheetPresented) {
            CreateNamedItemSheet(
                heading: "Save Parameter Preset",
                placeholder: "Preset name (e.g. Deep Reasoning)",
                tokens: tokens,
                typo: typo
            ) { name in
                store.createPreset(name: name)
            }
        }
        .alert(
            "Large Model Memory Warning",
            isPresented: Binding(
                get: { store.pendingSSDSwapWarningModel != nil },
                set: { if !$0 { store.pendingSSDSwapWarningModel = nil } }
            ),
            presenting: store.pendingSSDSwapWarningModel
        ) { targetModel in
            Button("Load with Tight-RAM Optimization (Recommended)") {
                store.pendingSSDSwapWarningModel = nil
                store.applyTightRAMOptimization(andLoadModel: targetModel)
            }
            Button("Load Anyway (May Use SSD Swap)", role: .destructive) {
                store.selectAndLoadModel(targetModel, bypassSwapGuard: true)
            }
            Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) {
                store.pendingSSDSwapWarningModel = nil
            }
        } message: { targetModel in
            let reclaimableGB = Double(store.liveOllamaTotalBytes) / 1_073_741_824.0
            let availableGB = store.memorySnapshot.headroomGB + reclaimableGB
            Text("“\(targetModel.shortDisplayName)” (\(targetModel.formattedSize)) plus its KV cache exceeds your \(String(format: "%.1f GB", availableGB)) available Unified Memory before swap. Tight-RAM Optimization applies Q4_0 KV cache, 4k context, and safe GPU layer splitting.")
        }
    }
}

public final class MotifAppDelegate: NSObject, NSApplicationDelegate {
    public func applicationDidFinishLaunching(_ notification: Notification) {
        MainActor.assumeIsolated {
            CustomFontRegistrar.registerBundledFonts()
        }
        NSWindow.allowsAutomaticWindowTabbing = false
    }
}

@main
public struct MotifApp: App {
    @NSApplicationDelegateAdaptor(MotifAppDelegate.self) private var appDelegate
    @StateObject private var store: WorkstationStore

    public init() {
        CustomFontRegistrar.registerBundledFonts()
        _store = StateObject(wrappedValue: WorkstationStore())
    }

    private func keyEq(_ raw: String, fallback: Character) -> KeyEquivalent {
        let ch = raw.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).lowercased().first ?? fallback
        return KeyEquivalent(ch)
    }

    public var body: some Scene {
        WindowGroup("Motif") {
            let rootTokens = MotifTokens(theme: store.activeTheme, customPalettes: store.customPalettes, isWindowActive: true)
            MotifRootView(store: store)
                .frame(minWidth: 860, minHeight: 560)
                .preferredColorScheme(rootTokens.isEffectivelyDark ? .dark : .light)
        }
        .windowStyle(.hiddenTitleBar)
        .commands {
            CommandGroup(replacing: .appSettings) {
                Button("Settings…") { store.isSettingsModalPresented = true }
                    .keyboardShortcut(",", modifiers: [.command])
            }
            CommandGroup(replacing: .newItem) {
                Button("New Chat") { store.createNewSession(folderID: nil) }
                    .keyboardShortcut(keyEq(store.shortcuts.newChatKey, fallback: "n"), modifiers: [.command])
                Button("Toggle Left Sidebar") { store.isSidebarVisible.toggle() }
                    .keyboardShortcut(keyEq(store.shortcuts.toggleSidebarKey, fallback: "s"), modifiers: [.command])
                Button("Toggle Code Inspector") {
                    store.userManuallyClosedRightPaneInWideMode = store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen
                    store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen.toggle()
                }
                .keyboardShortcut(keyEq(store.shortcuts.toggleInspectorKey, fallback: "\\"), modifiers: [.command])

                Divider()

                Button("Focus Composer") { store.requestComposerFocus() }
                    .keyboardShortcut("l", modifiers: [.command])
                Button("Model Picker") { store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented.toggle() }
                    .keyboardShortcut("m", modifiers: [.command, .shift])
                Button("Session Parameters") { store.isParameterPopoverPresented.toggle() }
                    .keyboardShortcut("p", modifiers: [.command, .shift])
                Button("Copy Latest Reply") { store.copyLatestAssistantReply() }
                    .keyboardShortcut("c", modifiers: [.command, .shift])

                Divider()

                Button("Increase Font Size") { store.adjustActiveFontSize(delta: 0.5) }
                    .keyboardShortcut("=", modifiers: [.command])
                Button("Decrease Font Size") { store.adjustActiveFontSize(delta: -0.5) }
                    .keyboardShortcut("-", modifiers: [.command])

                Divider()

                Button("Stop Generation") { store.stopActiveGeneration() }
                    .keyboardShortcut(keyEq(store.shortcuts.stopGenerationKey, fallback: "."), modifiers: [.command])
                    .disabled(!store.isStreamingGeneration)
            }
        }
    }
}
EOF
)

printf "\n===== [15/17] Compile Release Apple Silicon Binary (Swift 6 Toolchain) =====\n"
(
  cd Motif
  swift build -c release --arch arm64
  ls -lh .build/arm64-apple-macosx/release/Motif
)

printf "\n===== [16/17] Assemble Signed macOS Motif.app Bundle =====\n"
(
  set -e
  APP_NAME="${APP_NAME:-Motif}"
  DISPLAY_NAME="${DISPLAY_NAME:-Motif}"
  BUNDLE_ID="${BUNDLE_ID:-com.motif.studio.ollama}"
  VERSION="${VERSION:-15.0.0}"
  BUILD_NUMBER="${BUILD_NUMBER:-1500}"

  rm -rf build/dmg_staging/*
  APP_BUNDLE="build/dmg_staging/${APP_NAME}.app"
  mkdir -p "${APP_BUNDLE}/Contents/MacOS"
  mkdir -p "${APP_BUNDLE}/Contents/Resources/Fonts"

  BIN_PATH="$(cd Motif && swift build -c release --arch arm64 --show-bin-path)"
  cp "${BIN_PATH}/Motif" "${APP_BUNDLE}/Contents/MacOS/${APP_NAME}"
  chmod +x "${APP_BUNDLE}/Contents/MacOS/${APP_NAME}"

  if [ -f "build/AppIcon.icns" ]; then
    cp "build/AppIcon.icns" "${APP_BUNDLE}/Contents/Resources/AppIcon.icns"
  fi
  if [ -d "Motif/Sources/Motif/Resources/Fonts" ]; then
    cp -R Motif/Sources/Motif/Resources/Fonts/* "${APP_BUNDLE}/Contents/Resources/Fonts/" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi
  if [ -d "${BIN_PATH}/Motif_Motif.bundle" ]; then
    cp -R "${BIN_PATH}/Motif_Motif.bundle" "${APP_BUNDLE}/Contents/Resources/"
  fi

  cat << EOF > "${APP_BUNDLE}/Contents/Info.plist"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
<plist version="1.0">
<dict>
  <key>CFBundleDevelopmentRegion</key>
  <string>en</string>
  <key>CFBundleExecutable</key>
  <string>${APP_NAME}</string>
  <key>CFBundleIconFile</key>
  <string>AppIcon</string>
  <key>CFBundleIdentifier</key>
  <string>${BUNDLE_ID}</string>
  <key>CFBundleInfoDictionaryVersion</key>
  <string>6.0</string>
  <key>CFBundleName</key>
  <string>${DISPLAY_NAME}</string>
  <key>CFBundleDisplayName</key>
  <string>${DISPLAY_NAME}</string>
  <key>CFBundlePackageType</key>
  <string>APPL</string>
  <key>CFBundleShortVersionString</key>
  <string>${VERSION}</string>
  <key>CFBundleVersion</key>
  <string>${BUILD_NUMBER}</string>
  <key>LSMinimumSystemVersion</key>
  <string>14.0</string>
  <key>NSHighResolutionCapable</key>
  <true/>
  <key>NSSupportsAutomaticGraphicsSwitching</key>
  <true/>
  <key>NSAppTransportSecurity</key>
  <dict>
    <key>NSAllowsLocalNetworking</key>
    <true/>
  </dict>
</dict>
</plist>
EOF

  codesign --force --deep --sign - "${APP_BUNDLE}"
  codesign --verify --verbose "${APP_BUNDLE}"
)

printf "\n===== [17/17] Package Compressed macOS Disk Image (.dmg) =====\n"
(
  set -e
  APP_NAME="${APP_NAME:-Motif}"
  DISPLAY_NAME="${DISPLAY_NAME:-Motif}"
  VERSION="${VERSION:-15.0.0}"

  ln -fsn /Applications build/dmg_staging/Applications
  DMG_NAME="${APP_NAME}-${VERSION}-macOS-arm64.dmg"
  rm -f "build/${DMG_NAME}"
  hdiutil create \
    -volname "${DISPLAY_NAME}" \
    -srcfolder build/dmg_staging \
    -ov \
    -format UDZO \
    "build/${DMG_NAME}"
  ls -lh "build/${DMG_NAME}"
  printf "\nBuild complete: build/%s\n" "${DMG_NAME}"
)
